### [1] [Collaboratively Eliciting Gestures for Geospatial Data Exploration on an MSE with Tangibles and Styluses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00007)
*Karen Penaranda Valdivia,Nujaimah Ahmed,Aswah Butt,Mashrufa Orchi,Roozbeh Manshaei,Sarah Hoyos-Hoyos,Emmanuel Kyeremeh,Gabby Resch,Robert McLeman,Jamy Li,Ali Mazalek*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过手势启发研究，探索在使用触控物体和触控笔进行地理空间数据可视化协作任务时，用户自定义的手势交互方式，并产出了一套手势分类体系及用户经验主题分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型桌面显示和多表面环境有助于地理空间数据的可视化探索与协作，但传统多点触控交互存在误识别瞬时运动为控制输入的问题。触控物体和触控笔虽是有前景的替代方案，但如何将其有效应用于地理空间数据可视化交互仍需深入研究。

Method: 开展了一项手势启发研究：16个地理空间数据可视化任务被作为真实协作工作流程的组成部分呈现（该流程与地理学和迁移研究者共同设计），参与者为这些任务提出交互建议，随后对收集的数据进行手势分类体系构建和主题分析。

Result: 研究产出了使用触控物体和触控笔与地理空间数据交互的用户自定义手势分类体系，并完成了关于用户对可视化任务和交互技术体验的主题分析。

Conclusion: 研究结果有助于理解如何更有效地利用触控物体和触控笔进行地理空间数据可视化交互，为该领域未来的交互设计和系统开发提供了指导性框架。

Abstract: Large tabletop displays and multi-surface environments offer potential for enhancing visual data exploration and collaborative work with geospatial datasets. These systems typically rely on multi-touch interactions, which can pose challenges when the multi-touch sensors misrepresent transitory movements as control inputs, leading to interruptions. Active tangibles and styluses offer an alternative to multi-touch interactions in MSEs, and have shown the potential to facilitate sense-making around large datasets. However, further research is needed to better understand how these modalities can be effectively leveraged for interacting with geospatial data visualizations. To address this, a gesture elicitation study was conducted in which users suggested interactions for 16 geospatial data visualization tasks, presented as a realistic collaborative workflow co-designed with geography and migration researchers. The study produced a taxonomy of user-defined gestures using tangibles and styluses for engaging with geospatial data, along with a thematic analysis of users' experiences with visualization tasks and interaction techniques.

</details>


### [2] [Cyber-Physical Digital Factory Architecture as the Enabler of Disembodied Work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00195)
*Tero Kaarlela,Ivan Ruchkin,Jose Outeiro,Souradeep Dutta*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种网络物理数字工厂架构，通过整合数字孪生、分层云边AI、工业物联网和扩展现实（XR）远程操作界面，实现"离身工作"（disembodied work），即制造系统可由人类操作员通过XR界面远程监督和操控，并与AI协同控制。该架构在CNC加工、机器人辅助磨抛和机器人拆解等典型制造操作中得到验证，证明了离身制造工作的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的数字孪生、人工智能和工业物联网技术虽然显著推动了制造数字化，但通常被应用于单个制造过程，而非整合到统一的网络物理制造环境中，缺乏一个集成化的框架来实现远程监督与操作的离身制造工作。

Method: 提出一种网络物理数字工厂架构，集成同步数字孪生、分层的云-边缘AI、工业物联网以及XR远程操作界面，构建支持AI与人类操作员协作的离身制造环境。

Result: 通过CNC加工、机器人辅助磨抛和机器人化拆解三种代表性制造操作的验证，证明了所提出架构在离身制造工作中的可行性，并提供了可复用的网络物理框架。

Conclusion: 该架构为未来人类-AI协作控制的数字工厂提供了一种可复用的网络物理框架，展示了离身制造工作的可行性。

Abstract: Digital Twins (DTs), Artificial Intelligence (AI), and Industrial Internet of Things (IIoT) technologies have significantly advanced manufacturing digitalization. However, these technologies are typically applied to individual manufacturing processes rather than integrated into a unified cyber-physical manufacturing environment. This paper proposes a cyber-physical digital factory architecture that enables disembodied work, where manufacturing systems can be supervised and operated remotely through eXtended Reality (XR) user interfaces in collaboration between AI-based control and human operators. The architecture integrates synchronized DTs, hierarchical cloud-edge AI, IIoT, and XR teleoperation interfaces into a cyber-physical manufacturing environment. The proposed approach is validated through representative manufacturing operations, including CNC machining, robotic-assisted abrasive finishing, and robotized disassembly. The results demonstrate the feasibility of the proposed architecture for disembodied manufacturing work and provide a reusable cyber-physical framework for future human-AI-controlled digital factories.

</details>


### [3] [AniMaster: From Story Texts to Animated Videos via Cinematic Script Generation and Interactive Authoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00346)
*Ruiqi Yu,Dekun Qian,Jiale Xu,Sizhe Cheng,Yize Li,Xiangyang Wu,Zhiguang Zhou,Wei Chen,Yong Wang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出AniMaster，一个基于视频生成模型(VGM)的交互式动画视频创作工具，帮助普通创作用户从自由文本故事生成高质量的长篇动画视频。它通过一个三层设计框架（故事文本、电影脚本、动画视频）实现从故事到专业电影脚本再到高质量视频的自动翻译，并支持用户通过文本指令和直观交互进行编辑和优化。用户研究和专家访谈验证了其有效性和可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型虽然能生成短视频片段，但普通创作者仍难以利用它们从简短故事文本制作长篇精品动画视频。主要挑战在于：(1)普通用户缺乏将自由文本故事转换为专业电影脚本并最终生成高质量动画视频的专业知识；(2)缺乏有效方式将视频设计意图传达给镜头构图、摄像机控制和镜头序列等视觉叙事关键变量。

Method: 首先通过形成性研究与新手创作者和电影专家合作识别核心挑战；然后借鉴叙事学和电影研究，提出一个三层设计框架（故事文本、电影脚本、动画视频）及各层间翻译机制；基于该框架开发AniMaster工具，能够自动将简短故事文本扩展为详细电影脚本，并按照专业视觉叙事原则翻译为精细视频，同时支持用户交互式编辑脚本和通过文本指令优化生成视频。

Result: 通过16名参与者的深入用户研究、两个案例研究和2名电影专业人士的专家访谈进行了广泛评估。结果表明AniMaster在帮助普通创作者从自由形式故事文本创作精美动画视频方面具有有效性和可用性。

Conclusion: AniMaster成功弥合了普通创作者与专业电影制作之间的鸿沟，证明了基于三层框架的VGM驱动创作工具能够显著降低动画视频创作的专业门槛，使日常创作者也能产出符合视觉叙事原则的长篇动画视频。

Abstract: Recent advances in Video Generation Models (VGMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in producing short video clips. However, it is still challenging for everyday creators to leverage these models to produce polished long-form animated videos from brief story texts. Informed by a formative study with both novice creators and film experts, we identify two major challenges of interactive video authoring: (1) the lack of expertise in translating free-form story texts to professional cinematic scripts and finally high-quality animated videos, and (2) the absence of effective ways to convey video design intents to key variables of visual storytelling, such as shot composition, camera controls and shot sequencing. Drawing on narratology and film studies, we propose a three-layer design framework that defines the key design dimensions across three layers (i.e., story texts, cinematic scripts, and animated videos) as well as the translation between them. Built on this framework, we present AniMaster, a VGM-powered authoring tool to enable everyday creators to easily produce smooth animated videos from free-form story texts. AniMaster automatically expands brief story texts to detailed cinematic scripts, and further translates cinematic scripts into polished videos by following professional visual storytelling principles. It also allows users to interactively edit the scripts and refine the generated videos via text instructions and intuitive interactions. We extensively evaluated AniMaster through an in-depth user study with 16 participants, two case studies, and expert interviews with 2 film professionals. The results demonstrate the effectiveness and usability of AniMaster in helping everyday creators create polished animated videos from free-form story texts.

</details>


### [4] [MorphPatch: Enhancing VR Interaction on Shape Displays using Surface Approximation and Visuo-Haptic Illusions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00371)
*Wen Ying,KyeongMin Kim,Adil Rahman,JaeYoung Seon,HyeongYeop Kang,Seongkook Heo*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出MorphPatch系统，通过基于符号距离场的表面逼近流程实现动态形状显示器与虚拟表面的实时对齐，并结合笔重定向与视觉-触觉错觉补偿残余错位，在多项评估中优于空中交互和平板式交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚拟现实中表面交互能通过物理支撑和触觉反馈提升输入性能，但现有形状显示器分辨率有限，导致物理表面与虚拟表面对齐不准确，影响可用性和用户体验。

Method: MorphPatch采用基于符号距离场（SDF）的表面逼近管线，为多样化的几何形状寻找实用的对齐方案；对于仍存在的残余偏差，引入笔重定向技术，利用视觉-触觉错觉对错位进行感知层面的补偿。

Result: 三项评估表明：几何对齐精度得到改善，笔重定向用户可容忍阈值合理，且在控制、表面引导和建模任务中相比空中交互和平板式交互表现更优。

Conclusion: MorphPatch有效解决了动态形状显示器与虚拟表面之间因分辨率限制导致的错位问题，通过对齐算法与感知补偿相结合，显著提升了VR中表面交互的性能与体验。

Abstract: On-surface interaction in Virtual Reality improves input performance through physical support and tactile feedback, but current shape displays are constrained by limited resolution. This can misalign physical and virtual surfaces, degrading usability and user experience. We present MorphPatch, a system that enables real-time alignment between a dynamic shape display and virtual surfaces. MorphPatch uses a Signed Distance Field-based surface approximation pipeline to find practical alignments for diverse geometries. For residual discrepancies, MorphPatch incorporates pen redirection with visuo-haptic illusion to perceptually compensate for misalignment. Three evaluations show improved geometric alignment, tolerable redirection thresholds, and better control, surface guidance, and modeling results over mid-air and tablet-like interaction.

</details>


### [5] [FocusBuddy: Encouraging Healthy Desk-Work Habits by Caring for a Virtual Pet on a Water Bottle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00412)
*Mohamed Ouf,Rowan Hussein*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了FocusBuddy，一种包裹在标准水瓶外的织物保护套原型，内置微控制器、环境传感器和小型显示屏，通过虚拟宠物来激励用户喝水、起身运动和补充水分。经过20名大学生两周的使用测试，自我报告显示每日饮水量从中位数3杯增至4杯，运动次数从2次增至4次。研究还发现两个设计难题：健康提醒需尊重专注工作状态，以及宠物被忽视可能将健康提醒转化为负罪感来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 久坐办公人群会长时间不中断地坐着，且实际饮水量低于预期。虽然软件提醒能同时解决这两个问题，但这类提醒容易被忽视也容易引起用户反感，因此需要探索更友好的激励方式。

Method: 作者设计并实现了一个名为FocusBuddy的概念验证原型——一种包裹标准水瓶的织物外壳，内置微控制器、环境传感器和显示虚拟宠物的小屏幕。宠物状态映射用户自我关怀行为：喝水滋养宠物、站立活动与宠物玩耍、空瓶加水为宠物清洁。研究招募20名大学生在日常课程学习环境中使用该设备两周，并通过书面访谈收集数据。

Result: 两周使用后，自我报告的每日饮水量中位数从3杯提升到4杯，日常运动次数从2次提升到4次。访谈揭示了两个关键矛盾：一是健康提示必须尊重用户的专注工作状态，二是一只被忽视的宠物可能把原本积极的健康提醒变成用户的负罪感来源。

Conclusion: 本研究提供了FocusBuddy原型、首次部署中用户自报健康习惯朝积极方向转变的证据，以及面向情感化健康设备的设计启示。这项工作为以虚拟宠物等情感框架促进自我关怀行为提供了实证基础与设计方向。

Abstract: People who study or work at a desk sit for long uninterrupted periods and drink less water than they intend to. Software reminders address both problems but are easy to dismiss and easy to resent. We present FocusBuddy, a proof-of-concept fabric case that wraps a standard water bottle and houses a microcontroller, environmental sensors, and a small display showing a virtual pet. The pet's condition mirrors the user's self-care: drinking water feeds the pet, standing up to move plays with it, and refilling an empty bottle cleans it. Twenty undergraduate students used FocusBuddy for two weeks during their regular coursework and completed a written interview. Self-reported water intake rose from a median of 3 to 4 cups per day, movement episodes rose from 2 to 4 per day, and interviews surfaced two tensions: that wellness prompts must respect focused work, and that pet neglect can convert a wellness prompt into a source of guilt. We contribute the prototype, first-deployment evidence of healthy-direction shifts in self-reported habits, and design implications for emotionally framed wellness devices.

</details>


### [6] [ErgoAssist: Cognition-Aware Posture Feedback in Wearable Ergonomic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00440)
*Sarmistha Sarna Gomasta,Bhawana Chhaglani,VP Nguyen,Prashant Shenoy*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: ErgoAssist是一种头戴式人体工学助手，通过IMU头动追踪检测不良姿势，并结合消费级EEG头带估算认知负荷，实现认知感知的姿势干预提醒。在受控实验中姿势分类准确率达81%，认知负荷估计准确率达90.2%；实时部署中减少81%的提醒频率，显著提升可用性、任务表现和姿势纠正率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人体工学可穿戴设备仅依赖姿势阈值提醒用户，在高专注时刻频繁打断用户，导致提醒疲劳和用户弃用。然而姿势与认知负荷紧密耦合，现有系统缺乏认知感知能力，需要一种能同时考虑两者、在恰当时间进行干预的系统。

Method: 提出ErgoAssist系统：(1) 基于IMU的头动追踪检测不良姿势；(2) 采用消费级EEG头带估计任务诱发的认知负荷；(3) 结合姿势状态和认知负荷实现认知感知的提醒机制，在用户高投入时避免打扰，在合适时机提供干预。

Result: 在留一被试交叉验证下，姿势分类准确率达81%，任务诱发认知负荷估计准确率达90.2%。初步实时部署中，认知感知提醒将提醒频率降低81%，感知可用性提升43%，任务表现提升25%，姿势纠正率提升38%。

Conclusion: 认知感知的提醒策略能实现更少但更适时的人体工学干预，而非简单地抑制告警，有效减轻了传统姿势提醒系统带来的疲劳问题，同时提升了用户可用性和实际姿势纠正效果。

Abstract: Prolonged digital device use has made poor posture and musculoskeletal discomfort pervasive among knowl- edge workers. Existing ergonomic wearables rely solely on posture thresholds, frequently interrupting users during high-focus moments and leading to alert fatigue and abandonment. Yet posture and cognitive load are closely coupled, and most systems remain cognitively unaware. We present ErgoAssist, a head-worn ergonomic assistant that detects poor posture using IMU-based head tracking and estimates task-induced cognitive load using a consumer-grade EEG headband for continuous everyday use. In a controlled lab study, ErgoAssist achieves 81% posture classification and 90.2% task induced cognitive load estimation accuracy under leave-one-subject-out evaluation. In a preliminary real-time deployment, cognition-aware alerting reduces alert frequency by 81%, improves perceived usability by 43%, task performance by 25%, and improves posture correction rate by 38%, delivering fewer but better-timed interventions rather than merely suppressing alerts.

</details>


### [7] [Design principles to Increase Technology Self-efficacy for Older Australians with Mild Cognitive Impairment (MCI) and Older Carers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00480)
*Snezna Bizilj Schmidt,Nathan D'Cunha,Stephen Isbel,Blooma John,Ram Subramanian*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过半结构化访谈探讨澳大利亚轻度认知障碍（MCI）老年人及其照护者采用和持续使用技术的动机与促进因素，提出基于自我效能理论和保护动机理论的两项技术设计原则及相关指南，旨在提升技术自我效能感、减轻技术快速变化带来的负面影响，促进技术持续采用以支持独立生活。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球人口老龄化导致认知或身体受损人群增加，技术有潜力支持独立生活，但我们对老年人（尤其是认知障碍者）技术采用和持续使用的动机因素理解存在空白。

Method: 采用半结构化访谈收集老年人及其照护者的个人人口统计学特征、需求、优先级、生活方式、挑战及技术使用经验数据，并通过归纳性、反思性主题分析进行数据处理。

Result: 研究发现独立、自主和生活质量的愿望是技术使用的动机，技术自我效能感和IT素养是促进因素；保护动机理论显示技术的持续变化会降低自我效能感和IT素养，成为技术采用和持续使用的障碍。基于理论提出两项高层次设计原则及相关指南。

Conclusion: 提出的设计原则和指南旨在增强自我效能感和技术使用信心，同时减少技术变革的不利影响，鼓励MCI老年人持续采用技术以支持独立生活和生活质量。

Abstract: The number of people with age related physical or cognitive impairments is increasing due to the worlds ageing population. Technology has the potential to support independent living, and to achieve aged and health service efficiencies, however, there are gaps in our understanding of factors that motivate technology adoption and ongoing use by older adults, especially those with cognitive impairments. This study aims to explore motivators and enablers for technology adoption and ongoing use by older Australians with mild cognitive impairment (MCI) and their carers, to identify technology design principles and guidelines that maximise adoption. Semi structured interviews were used to gather data about individual demographics, needs, priorities, lifestyle, challenges, and experiences with technology. Results of inductive, reflective, thematic analysis indicate that a desire for independence, autonomy and quality of life motivate use of technology, and perceived technology self-efficacy and IT literacy are enablers. The Protection Motivation Theory illustrates that constant technology change is a disabler for technology adoption and sustained use, because it lowers perceived technology self-efficacy and IT literacy, and reduces confidence to use technology. Two high-level technology design principles and related guidelines are proposed, grounded in theory and aligned with Banduras four sources of self-efficacy. These design principles and guidelines are intended to increase feelings of self-efficacy and confident use of technology, while also reducing adverse impacts of technological change and encouraging sustained technology adoption by older adults with MCI to support independent living and quality of life.

</details>


### [8] [UniScale: Exploring Unimanual Gesture Mapping Strategies for Gaze+Pinch-based Scaling Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00500)
*Kyoungwhan Mheen,Jinwook Kim,Sang Ho Yoon*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了UniScale，一组基于注视和手部交互的单手缩放技术，作为传统双手捏合缩放的替代方案，让用户在XR环境中进行对象缩放时保持另一只手可用于并行的空间操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对象缩放是XR环境中基础且重要的空间操作，但标准的双手捏合缩放需要占用双手，限制了用户在缩放过程中进行其他并发空间操作的能力，因此需要探索单手缩放方案。

Method: 设计了五种基于物理隐喻的单手缩放映射策略（涉及深度、角度、微手势和手指距离等输入维度），并与标准的双手捏合缩放基线进行对比；通过用户研究评估这些技术在3D对象缩放任务中的表现，涵盖有clutching和无clutching两种条件。

Result: 双手缩放依赖clutching来保持稳定控制，而单手技术在没有clutching的条件下表现更优，能够显著减少物理手部移动量。

Conclusion: 研究结果提供了在XR中开发高效3D多模态交互的设计启示，表明单手缩放技术在特定条件下优于传统双手方案。

Abstract: Object scaling serves as a fundamental spatial manipulation that enables complex and productive tasks in XR environments. This paper investigates unimanual scaling techniques for XR using gaze and hand interactions. We propose UniScale, a set of unimanual alternatives to the standard bimanual pinch, allowing users to scale objects while preserving hand availability for concurrent spatial manipulations. We design five distinct mapping strategies based on physical metaphors, exploring unimanual control that varies depth, angle, micro-gestures, and finger-distance input. We then compare these techniques against a standard bimanual baseline, in which users adjust the inter-hand distance via a bimanual pinch gesture. In a user study, we evaluate their effectiveness in a 3D object scaling task under both clutching and clutching-free conditions. The results indicate that while bimanual scaling relies on clutching for stable control, unimanual techniques excel in clutching-free conditions, significantly reducing physical hand movement. From the results, we derive valuable design implications for developing efficient 3D multimodal interactions in XR.

</details>


### [9] [RecalibrateGPT: AI Fatigue Resilient Conversational Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00506)
*Nikhil Wani*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出RecalibrateGPT系统，通过五种跨轮次操作符（Anchor、Replay、Delta、Scope、Steer）和AssistiveButton交互界面，解决大语言模型对话中的AI疲劳问题。实验表明该系统将认知工作负荷降低一半（NASA-TLX=2.7），可用性评分高（SUS=86.5）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽强大，但其交互界面常陷入"输入→阅读→再输入"的循环，导致对话式AI疲劳、认知负荷增加，最终使用户放弃任务。现有研究多关注模型质量问题，而忽略了交互流程本身的成本。

Method: 提出RecalibrateGPT系统：1）设计五种跨轮次操作符（Anchor、Replay、Delta、Scope、Steer），每种针对一种特定疲劳类型，通过结构化面板作用于完整对话历史，一键重新校准LLM响应；2）用户通过AssistiveButton调用操作符，提供三种操作符面板布局（Vertical、Arc、Tablet）；3）开展两项试点研究：先通过定性研究建立四种疲劳类型分类法（重输入、扫描、决策瘫痪、上下文漂移），再通过定量研究评估系统效果。

Result: 两项涉及12名高级LLM用户的试点研究显示：定性研究识别出四种疲劳类型的分类法并推导出两个设计目标；定量评估发现系统将感知认知工作负荷降低一半（NASA-TLX=2.7），具有较高的感知可用性（SUS=86.5）。

Conclusion: AI疲劳不仅是一个模型质量问题，更是一个交互流程成本问题，而适当的界面设计可以消除这一成本。RecalibrateGPT通过结构化操作符和界面的方式有效缓解了LLM对话中的疲劳问题。

Abstract: Large language models are powerful, but their interfaces often devolve into a type $\rightarrow$ read $\rightarrow$ retype loop, creating conversational AI fatigue, cognitive load, and eventual task abandonment. To mitigate this, we present RecalibrateGPT, a system introducing five cross-turn operators (Anchor, Replay, Delta, Scope, and Steer) that each target a distinct fatigue type, recalibrating LLM responses through a structured panel by acting on the full conversation history with a single click. Users invoke these operators through the AssistiveButton in one of three operator palette layouts: Vertical, Arc, or Tablet. We conducted two pilot studies with the same 12 advanced LLM users. An initial formative qualitative study identifies a taxonomy of four fatigue types (retyping, scanning, decision paralysis, and context drift) and derives two design objectives for RecalibrateGPT. A follow-up quantitative evaluation finds it reduces perceived cognitive workload by half (NASA-TLX = 2.7) at high perceived usability (SUS = 86.5), suggesting AI fatigue is not just a model-quality issue but an interaction-flow cost that interfaces can remove.

</details>


### [10] [Are We There Yet? Assessing Computer-Use Agents for Blind Users' Accessible Interaction with Desktop Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00524)
*Satwik Ram Kodandaram,Monalika Padma Reddy,Xiaojun Bi,Jiawei Zhou,I. V. Ramakrishnan,Vikas Ashok*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过为期三周的日记研究，招募8名盲人用户使用无障碍屏幕阅读器友好的CUA原型OLLA，涵盖12个应用、1258条命令，评估了GPT-5等五款模型的成功率表现，并深入分析了失败原因与用户需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机使用智能体（CUA）是新兴的人机交互范式，但对盲人屏幕阅读器用户在真实桌面工作流中的有效性尚不明确，现有研究缺乏针对该群体的实证评估。

Method: 采用三周日记研究法，8名盲人用户使用OLLA（无障碍CUA原型），收集1258条命令及截图、UI树、模型响应和操作轨迹等多模态数据；部署GPT-5进行评估，并用四个额外模型重新执行相同命令进行对比。

Result: GPT-5成功率最高达52.5%；轨迹分析揭示出grounding（界面锚定）、规划、约束追踪和终止四类失败模式；访谈还发现了超越纯自动化的用户需求。

Conclusion: 当前CUA模型对盲人用户的实际桌面工作流支持仍存在显著不足，需要同时改进模型的技术能力并关注无障碍场景中的非自动化需求。

Abstract: Computer-use agents are emerging as a paradigm for agentic human-AI interaction, combining language reasoning with multi-modal interface grounding to operate GUIs. Yet their effectiveness for blind screen-reader users in real-world desktop workflows remains unclear. We present a three-week diary study with 8 blind users using OLLA, a screen-reader-accessible CUA prototype, collecting 1,258 commands across 12 applications with screenshots, UI trees, model responses, and action traces. We evaluate GPT-5 during deployment and re-execute the same commands with four additional models. GPT-5 achieved the highest success rate at 52.5%. Trace analysis reveals grounding, planning, constraint-tracking, and termination failures, while interviews reveal beyond-automation needs.

</details>


### [11] [Learning Feasibility-Aware Latent Spaces for Preference-Based Exploration of Procedural Automotive Wheel Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00527)
*Takashi Owaki,Yuki Koyama,Tomoyasu Nakano,Takahiro Yamaguchi,Masataka Goto,Hiroyuki Sakai*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向交互的表示学习流水线，用于程序化模型的偏好优化。该方法结合几何规则和有限元分析筛选程序化生成的样本，从中学习降维潜在空间，并引入监督功能对齐使维度具有物理意义（刚度、应力、重量）。模拟和40人受控实验均表明，筛选降维和功能对齐显著提升了建议的可行性与目标形状检索效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏好优化驱动的智能设计界面在工程设计中非常有用，但程序化模型的原生参数往往纠缠且产生大量无效输出，导致人在环路优化浪费比较次数。现有方法忽视了向用户暴露的表示空间本身也是交互设计的重要部分。

Method: 首先利用几何规则和有限元分析（FEA）筛选程序化生成的轮毂样本，然后在筛选后的子集上学习降维潜在空间；进一步引入监督式功能对齐，将潜在维度保留给刚度、应力强度和重量等功能相关属性，使搜索能偏向有功能意义的区域。

Result: 模拟实验表明，经过筛选的降维改善了目标形状检索和建议的可行性率，而未筛选的降维则使两者恶化；沿学习到的功能维度约束搜索能加速目标功能属性的探索。40人受控实验显示，5D可行性感知空间比原始9D程序化参数化产生更高的形状相似性和更多可行建议。

Conclusion: 对于工程设计中的智能用户界面而言，向用户暴露的表示形式是交互设计的核心组成，而不仅仅是为优化器服务的预处理步骤。

Abstract: Intelligent design interfaces that rely on preference-based optimization are most useful when their suggestions are both meaningful to users and feasible within the target domain. Procedural models offer compact and editable design spaces, but their native parameters can be entangled and can generate many invalid outputs, causing human-in-the-loop optimizers to waste comparisons. We propose an interaction-oriented representation-learning pipeline for procedural models and study it in automotive wheel design. The method first screens procedurally generated samples using geometric rules and finite-element analysis, then learns a reduced latent space from the screened subset. We further introduce supervised functional alignment, which reserves selected latent dimensions for stiffness, strength-related stress response, or weight so that search can be biased toward functionally meaningful regions. Simulation experiments show that screened reduction improves target-shape retrieval and the feasibility rate of suggestions, whereas unscreened reduction degrades both. Additional simulations show that constraining search along learned functional dimensions accelerates exploration toward target functional properties. A controlled study with 40 participants further shows that a 5D feasibility-aware space yields higher shape similarity and more feasible suggestions than the original 9D procedural parameterization. These results suggest that, for intelligent user interfaces in engineering design, the representation exposed to the user is a central part of the interaction design, not merely a preprocessing step for the optimizer.

</details>


### [12] [GazeTune: Facilitating Precise Gaze-Driven Interactions with Cascaded Touch Input](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00716)
*Jina Kim,Eric J. Gonzalez,Yang Zhang,Sang Ho Yoon*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出GazeTune，一种结合眼动与触觉的级联多模态交互技术，用于空间计算中的精确选择与操作。触觉作为注视瞄准的细化通道，提升拖拽精度并缓解运动导致的界面不稳定问题。用户研究（N=20）表明，在2D拖拽任务中，GazeTune相比纯眼动和眼动+捏合手势方法，在保持相近完成时间的同时显著降低了操作误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 眼动追踪虽在空间计算中不可或缺，但其粗略瞄准和扫视特性限制了精度与连续交互（如拖拽）；眼动+捏合手势虽为标准XR交互方式，但存在不精确、易疲劳和社会接受度低等问题。现有方法无法同时兼顾眼动速度与稳定的精细控制，亟需新的交互范式。

Method: GazeTune采用级联多模态交互架构，以眼动作为主瞄准通道、触觉作为细化通道，实现光标准确控制与目标操控；通过触觉输入修正眼动误差，增强拖拽精度并抑制运动引起的界面不稳定性。研究设计为三条件对比实验（GazeTune vs. 纯眼动 vs. 眼动+捏合），在2D拖拽任务中评估。

Result: N=20的用户研究结果表明，GazeTune在拖拽任务中显著降低了误差，同时执行时间与对比方法相当，验证了其在时间与精度之间的良好平衡。

Conclusion: GazeTune作为一种新兴的多模态交互范式，有效弥合了眼动速度与精细控制之间的鸿沟，为空间计算中高精度连续交互提供了可行方案，并为后续眼动+触觉融合交互研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Eye gaze has become an essential input for spatial computing, but its coarse targeting and saccadic nature limit precision and complicate continuous interactions such as dragging, especially under user motion. Gaze+pinch has also become standard in XR for its convenience, yet mid-air gestures remain imprecise, fatiguing, and socially unacceptable. These limitations underscore the need for an approach that preserves the speed of gaze while enabling stable, fine control. We present GazeTune, a cascaded multimodal interaction technique combining gaze and touch to refine gaze-based selection and manipulation. Touch serves as a refinement channel within gaze pointing, allowing precise cursor and target control. Our work investigates how gaze-and-touch enhances dragging and mitigates Motion-Induced instability. In a study (N=20), we compared GazeTune against gaze-only and gaze-pinch methods in 2D dragging. Results show that GazeTune achieves significantly lower error with comparable execution time, validating its effectiveness and balanced trade-off between time and accuracy.

</details>


### [13] [No Pixel Left Behind: Filling Gaps in Anime Colorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00800)
*Masahiro Kono,Akinobu Maejima,Yuki Koyama,Yotam Sechayk,Takeo Igarashi*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了GapFill工具，用于解决动漫制作流程中线条稿数字着色时遗漏小面积未着色区域（"缝隙"）的问题，通过深度学习方法参考周围区域推荐填充颜色，经13位专业着色师用户研究验证，相比传统方法提升了性能和可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动漫制作中的数字着色流程常因油漆桶工具遗漏微小的封闭区域（缝隙）而导致效率低下，这些区域需要人工发现和填充，耗时费力，且这一挑战此前未被充分研究。

Method: 作者首先在日本动漫制作流程中进行了形成性研究以了解实际问题；随后开发了基于深度学习的方法GapFill，利用动漫图像扁平着色的特性，通过参考周围区域来建议合适的填充颜色，并集成了缝隙检测、自动缩放和颜色选择等辅助功能。

Result: 在13位专业着色师参与的用户研究中，GapFill在缝隙填充任务中的性能和可用性均优于传统方法。研究还发现：预测准确率并非可用性的首要因素，合适的颜色可能存在上下文歧义，且GapFill能根据用户对AI辅助工具的信任程度与传统工具形成互补。

Conclusion: GapFill是一种植根于专业实践的可行工具，能有效减轻缝隙检测和填充的负担；同时该研究为AI辅助创作工具的设计提供了洞见，即预测准确率之外，用户信任、上下文歧义和工具互补性等因素同样影响可用性。

Abstract: Animation production workflows often involve digital colorization of line art, where small unpainted regions ("gaps") frequently occur and remain an underexplored challenge. We conducted a formative study in Japanese animation (anime) pipelines and found that while the paint bucket tool is widely used for base coloring, tiny enclosed areas are frequently overlooked, resulting in time-consuming manual detection and filling. We introduce GapFill, a tool grounded in professional practices that reduces the effort of gap detection, zooming, and color selection. Our deep-learning method suggests appropriate fill colors by referencing surrounding regions, leveraging the flat-color nature of anime-style images. In a user study with 13 professional colorists, our system improved performance and usability in gap-filling tasks over conventional methods. The study also suggested that prediction accuracy alone is not the primary factor for usability, that appropriate colors can be contextually ambiguous, and that GapFill can complement existing tools depending on users' trust in new AI-powered assistance.

</details>


### [14] [Ctrl-F-Resist. Practices, Challenges, and Technical Needs of Civil Society Organizations Monitoring the Far-Right Online](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00808)
*Elisabeth Steffen,Helena Mihaljević*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过质性研究（访谈12家德国公民社会组织共15名从业者），揭示了极右翼在线监控领域CSO的实践现状、挑战与技术需求，提出"人工劳动陷阱"概念，并介绍了开源Telegram监控原型的实施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极右翼势力日益利用在线平台传播意识形态，公民社会组织（CSO）在监控反民主动态中扮演重要但被忽视的角色，然而现有研究与技术开发往往忽视这些行为者，缺乏对其实践和技术需求的深入理解。

Method: 对12家德国CSO的15名从业者进行定性研究（质性访谈），分析其监控实践、挑战与对技术支持的期待；基于发现提出概念性监控工作流，并开发开源Telegram监控原型。

Result: 监控工作仍以人工为主，缺乏定制化工具；增强搜索是最迫切的技术需求。参与者对AI辅助功能（如媒体处理、内容发现）持开放态度，但对自动化分类普遍怀疑，担心信任、法律可用性和专业可信度问题。研究提出"人工劳动陷阱"概念并实现Telegram监控原型。

Conclusion: 监控型CSO应被视为数字空间治理中的关键利益相关者。研究提出了具体的设计、政策与研究建议，强调打破"人工劳动陷阱"、为CSO开发适配性技术工具的必要性。

Abstract: As far-right actors increasingly exploit online platforms to disseminate ideology and mobilize supporters, civil society organizations (CSOs) play a vital yet underrecognized role in monitoring antidemocratic dynamics online. Unlike fact-checkers or content moderators, CSOs engage in long-term, contextualized analysis, often in resource-constrained settings and under precarious conditions. Despite their critical societal role, CSOs face significant barriers to adopting or co-developing technical solutions, including legal uncertainty, limited platform access, and chronic underfunding. Existing research and tool development efforts have largely overlooked these actors in favor of more institutionally embedded stakeholders. This paper addresses this gap through a qualitative study with 15 practitioners from 12 Germany-based CSOs engaged in online monitoring, positioning them as key yet overlooked stakeholders in the governance of digital spaces. We explore their current practices, challenges, and expectations regarding technological support. Our findings show that monitoring remains largely manual due to the lack of tailored tools, with enhanced search capabilities emerging as the most pressing technical need. While participants express openness to AI-supported features such as media processing and content discovery, many remain skeptical of automated classification, citing concerns around trust, legal usability, and professional credibility. Grounded in these findings, we introduce a conceptual monitoring workflow and describe its implementation in an open-source Telegram monitoring prototype designed to flexibly support diverse monitoring goals. We outline concrete design, policy, and research recommendatios, and introduce the manual labor trap as an empirically grounded concept that explains why monitoring CSOs tend to remain locked into labor-intensive, low-capacity arrangements.

</details>


### [15] [Beyond Technological Solutionism: Rethinking XR in Healthcare](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01028)
*Md Haseen Akhtar,Cecilia Landa-Avila,Shital Desai,Claire Warden,Andrew Morris,Mark Anderson,Thomas Cochrane,Janakarajan Ramkumar*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过三个个人叙事案例探讨了XR技术在医疗领域的应用反而加剧了医疗不平等的问题，提出了"创新悖论"概念，并基于SEIPS 3.0模型指出医疗效果主要由人际关系（50-60%）、组织协调（25-30%）和社会文化因素（15-20%）决定，而非技术先进性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战HCI社区及医疗行业盲目追求技术创新的"技术解决方案主义"思维，揭露先进技术可能加剧而非解决医疗护理差距的现实问题。

Method: 采用挑衅性（provocation）论文形式，通过三个个人叙事案例（农村患者、城市专业人士、第一代移民），结合SEIPS 3.0模型对糖尿病-心血管疾病护理协调进行分析，提出护理相互依赖框架。

Result: 识别出"创新悖论"现象——先进技术反而创造了新的护理障碍；量化了影响医疗结果的核心因素：人际关系占50-60%、组织协调占25-30%、社会文化因素占15-20%，技术并非主导因素。

Conclusion: 提出新的医疗创新框架，主张优先考虑人际关系而非技术能力、系统性变革而非功能集、实际护理交付而非技术雄心；呼吁医疗领域从技术解决方案主义思维中退后一步。

Abstract: The healthcare industry's enthusiastic adoption of Extended Reality (XR) technologies obscures a concerning reality: we were building increasingly sophisticated ways to perpetuate fundamentally broken healthcare systems. Through three deeply personal narratives - a rural patient cut off from care infrastructure, an urban professional navigating fragmented services, and a first-generation immigrant confronting cultural barriers - this provocation paper exposes how our obsession with technological innovation often worsens rather than resolves healthcare disparities. By applying the SEIPS 3.0 model to examine diabetes-CVD care coordination, we identify an "innovation paradox" where advanced technology creates new barriers to effective care. Our care interdependencies framework reveals that healthcare outcomes are shaped primarily by human relationships (50-60%), organizational coordination (25-30%), and sociocultural factors (15-20%), not technological sophistication. This research challenges the HCI community to confront its role in perpetuating healthcare inequities, demands a fundamental rethinking and proposes a new framework for healthcare innovation that prioritizes human relationships over technical capability, systemic change over feature sets, and actual care delivery over technological ambition. For healthcare providers, technology developers, and policymakers, our findings suggest that effective care coordination requires us to step back from our techno-solutionist mindset and engage

</details>


### [16] [Cross-Modal Guidance for Out-of-View Object Search in Simulated Prosthetic Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01438)
*Adyah Rastogi,Apurv Varshney,Tobias Höllerer,Michael Beyeler*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究探讨在模拟假体视觉（SPV）条件下，不同引导模态（视觉、触觉、听觉）对目标搜索任务的影响。19名参与者在两种SPV条件下（10x10和20x20电极密度）进行目标搜索，结果显示三种引导模态均能减少搜索时间和头部移动，其中听觉和触觉引导比视觉引导快约25%的总体搜索速度和11-13%的目标获取速度。在严重视觉受限条件下（10x10），三种引导均将垂直定位误差减少约45-58%，尽管它们不提供任何高度信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视场外（out-of-view）引导在虚拟和增强现实中已被广泛研究，但其有效性可能取决于用户的视觉带宽。研究者希望探索在假体视觉这一严重视觉受限场景下，不同引导模态与稀疏视觉表征共享资源时的表现差异，以指导人工视觉设备的辅助设计。

Method: 招募19名参与者在模拟假体视觉条件下执行目标搜索任务。实验包含两个SPV因素（电极密度和光幻视扩散：10x10与20x20）和四种引导条件（无引导、视觉、触觉、听觉），所有引导均由相同的水平目标偏移变量驱动，比较各条件下的搜索时间、头部运动和定位精度。

Result: 所有三种引导模态均减少了搜索时间和头部运动。听觉和触觉引导的整体搜索速度快约25%，目标获取速度快11-13%，优于视觉引导。触觉和听觉引导还缩短了获取后的搜索时间。在10x10 SPV条件下，最终头部-目标角度偏移减少更为显著，且三种引导均将垂直定位误差降低了约45-58%，尽管引导本身不含高度信息。

Conclusion: 在严重视觉约束条件下，引导性能取决于线索实现方式和搜索阶段。非视觉模态（听觉、触觉）在整体搜索效率上优于视觉引导，但视觉引导在特定场景下仍有价值，这为人工视觉辅助设备中引导模态的设计选择提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Out-of-view guidance is well established in virtual and augmented reality, but its effectiveness may depend on the visual bandwidth available to the user. We test this under simulated prosthetic vision (SPV), where visual guidance must share the same sparse representation used to inspect the scene. Nineteen participants performed object search under two SPV conditions differing in electrode density and phosphene spread (10x10 and 20x20) and four guidance conditions (no guidance, visual, haptic, audio) all driven by the same horizontal target-offset variable. All three modalities reduced search time and head movement. The tested auditory and haptic cues produced approximately 25% faster overall search and 11-13% faster target acquisition than the visual cue, despite similarly direct orienting trajectories. The tested haptic and auditory cues also shortened post-acquisition search. Final head-target angular offset was reduced substantially more in the 10x10 SPV condition; there, all three cues also reduced vertical localization error by approximately 45-58% despite providing no elevation information. Under severe visual constraints, guidance performance depended on cue implementation and search stage.

</details>


### [17] [Better Situational Awareness in AR-HRC? A Comparative Study of Augmented Reality and Mobile Interfaces for Human-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01461)
*Zhehan Qu,Christian Fronk,Jaewoong Jeong,Pavel Manakhov,Maria Gorlatova*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过30人的被试间实验，比较了AR与移动界面在安全关键人机协作场景中的表现，发现AR仅提升了对机器人的感知级态势感知，未能提升更高层次意识或环境意识，眼动追踪揭示AR释放的注意力被重新投入共形视觉而非物理环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AR在人机协作中虽能提供空间可视化优势，但在安全关键场景（如搜救）中可能重塑视觉注意力并产生竞争性态势感知需求。已有研究缺乏同时考虑机器人与环境意识、并在多层次态势感知维度上进行严格评估的工作。

Method: 采用被试间实验设计，30名参与者分别使用自定义AR界面和移动界面（两者呈现等效信息），使用SAGAT方法在三个态势感知层次上测量机器人及环境意识，并同步进行眼动追踪以分析注意力机制。

Result: 两种界面均达到高可用性；相比移动基线，AR提升了机器人感知级意识，但在更高层次的机器人意识或任何层次的环境意识上均无增益。眼动分析表明：AR虽将注意力从地图上解放出来，但注意力被重新投入共形视觉而非物理环境。

Conclusion: 将视线从屏幕上解放出来并不等同于引导眼睛看向真实世界，AR界面在设计时必须正视这一区别，尤其是在安全关键的人机协作场景中。

Abstract: Augmented reality (AR) facilitates human-robot collaboration (HRC) by enabling in-situ spatial visualizations of the robot and the joint task. However, in safety-critical HRC scenarios such as search-and-rescue, spatial visualizations may also reshape visual attention in ways that create competing situational awareness (SA) demands, potentially introducing new safety concerns. While prior AR-HRC work suggests potential benefits for SA, rigorous evaluations that jointly consider robot and environmental awareness across multiple levels of SA remain limited. We address this through a between-subjects study with 30 participants comparing custom AR and mobile interfaces presenting equivalent information, measuring robot and environmental SA with the Situation Awareness Global Assessment Technique (SAGAT) across all three levels, with concurrent eye tracking to identify the attentional mechanisms underlying any SA differences. Both interfaces achieved high usability; relative to the mobile baseline, AR improved perception-level awareness of the robot but yielded no gains in higher-level robot awareness or in environmental awareness at any level. Gaze analysis explained this: AR freed attention from the map, but that attention was re-invested in the conformal visuals rather than the physical environment. Freeing the eyes from a screen is not the same as directing them to the world, a distinction AR interfaces for safety-critical HRC must design around.

</details>


### [18] [Evaluating Usability in Biomedical Visualization: Rethinking Heuristic Evaluation for Spatial Omics and Multidisciplinary Research Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01569)
*Yulia A. Levites Strekalova,Rachel Liu Galvin,Jessica M. Ray,Samuel P. Border,Mishal Khan,Samantha Hoffman,Christina D. Beharry,Katie Kloss,Philipp Haessner,David Manthey,Sanjay Jain,Michael T. Eadon,Laura Barisoni,Pinaki Sarder*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过对39名参与者进行两项互补研究，评估了临床研究信息学（CRI）平台的可用性，提出了三个领域特定的启发式准则：主动参数透明性、使用点指导和分阶段功能披露，以弥补传统可用性框架在处理高维生物医学数据平台时的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统可用性框架只能识别一般性问题，无法捕捉高维生物医学数据平台特有的可用性挑战；新兴技术（如空间组学和AI影像）虽扩展了研究能力，但引入了复杂界面并增加了认知负担。

Method: 开展两项互补研究共涉及39名参与者：研究1包含19名本科生完成交互任务，研究2涉及20名临床专业人员完成异步分层任务框架。采用基于标准可用性启发式和新兴CRI主题的演绎编码对观察与访谈数据进行分析。

Result: 复杂数据叠加与分析工具的同步呈现使参与者（尤其缺乏空间组学经验者）感到不堪重负；参与者依赖试错探索，并在数据密集环境中难以识别未标注工具。

Conclusion: 高维研究平台需要超越传统框架的领域特定可用性标准。作者提出三个专门的启发式规则（主动参数透明性、使用点指导、分阶段功能披露），帮助开发者管理复杂性、提供情境化支持并改善多学科研究团队的可达性。

Abstract: Introduction: Clinical research informatics (CRI) platforms support biomedical discovery by integrating advanced computational tools into research workflows. Emerging technologies such as spatial omics and AI-enabled imaging expand research capabilities but introduce complex interfaces that increase cognitive burden and alter established analytical processes. Traditional usability frameworks identify general usability issues but often miss challenges specific to high-dimensional biomedical data. Methods: We conducted two complementary studies involving 39 participants to evaluate conventional usability heuristics and identify CRI-specific criteria. Study 1 included 19 undergraduates completing interactive tasks, and Study 2 involved 20 clinical professionals completing an asynchronous hierarchical task framework. Observational and interview data were analyzed using deductive coding based on standard usability heuristics and emerging CRI-specific themes. Results: Simultaneous presentation of complex data overlays and analytical tools overwhelmed users, particularly those with limited spatial-omics experience. Participants relied on trial-and-error exploration and struggled with unlabeled tools in data-rich environments. Feedback indicated that users benefit from phased onboarding, contextual guidance, and progressive feature introduction rather than immediate access to all functionality. Discussion: High-dimensional research platforms require domain-specific usability criteria beyond traditional frameworks. We propose three specialized heuristics: Active Parameter Transparency, Point-of-Use Guidance, and Phased Feature Disclosure. These heuristics help developers manage complexity, provide contextual support, and improve accessibility for multidisciplinary research teams.

</details>


### [19] [Designing Proactive Thought Partners for Writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01588)
*Chao Zhang,Abe Davis,Chih-Wei Chen,Chin-Chia Hsu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文研究"主动思考伙伴"（proactive thought partners）的设计空间——即在写作过程中主动提供可定制、高层次认知支持的AI智能体。作者通过技术探针原型让16名参与者使用一周，参与者可配置智能体的角色和主动性。研究发现用户通过前瞻性规划来配置主动支持，将建议用于想法生成和自我监控，并偏好轻量级视觉表示与非指令性修辞框架来实现非侵入式干预。文章最后为主动写作助手的设计提出了关于定制化、时机、参与度和表示形式的启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动式AI写作工具大多只提供通用的文本辅助（如自动补全），缺乏对写作者个体差异和不同时刻动态需求的理解，因此需要探索如何设计能够主动提供高层次认知支持的写作助手。

Method: 采用设计空间研究方法，构建"主动思考伙伴"概念并实例化为技术探针原型，招募16名参与者进行为期一周的实地部署使用，收集用户配置行为和使用反馈进行定性分析。

Result: 参与者通过前瞻性规划配置主动支持；建议被用于想法生成和自我监控双重目的；轻量级视觉表示和非指令性修辞框架有助于实现非侵入式干预。

Conclusion: 主动写作助手的设计应围绕定制化、时机、参与度和表示形式四个维度展开，以在提供有价值支持的同时避免侵入用户的写作流程。

Abstract: Writing involves diverse cognitive activities, from ideation to revision, and writers' needs vary across individuals and moments. Proactive AI promises to provide the right support at the right time, yet existing proactive tools largely focus on generic textual assistance, such as autocomplete. This paper studies the design space of proactive thought partners: AI agents that proactively offer customizable, higher-level cognitive support during writing. We instantiated this concept in a technology probe and deployed it with 16 participants for one week. The probe allows users to create partners by configuring their roles and proactivity. As users write, relevant partners take the initiative at appropriate moments to offer suggestions. Our findings show that participants configured proactive support through prospective planning, used suggestions for both idea generation and self-monitoring, and valued lightweight visual representations alongside non-directive rhetorical framing for non-intrusive interventions. We derive implications for designing proactive writing assistants around customization, timing, engagement, and representation.

</details>

### [20] [HyperWorld: Hypergraph-Structured State Serialization Improves Learned Textual World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00002)
*Yun-Jian Zhang,Chen-Wei Liang,Tian-Yi Zhang,Jian Ding,Yi-Lun Wu,Ao-Bo Li,Wei-Cong Su,Saifullah,Hong-Yu An,Mu-Jiang-Shan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HyperWorld系统研究了文本环境中选择不同状态序列化表示对世界模型学习的影响，发现将多个相关事实按实体-关系分组的超边（hyperedge）序列化是最有效的归纳偏置，尤其在模型规模较小时和分布偏移场景下表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在文本环境中，语言模型智能体需要从序列化的状态描述中学习符号动作效果，但状态序列化结构的作用此前未被充分探索。作者希望通过受控实验来系统性地回答"如何序列化状态对学到的文本世界模型影响最大"这一核心问题。

Method: 提出HyperWorld框架，对比四种状态表示：原始观测、独立句子、成对三元组、以及围绕实体和关系分组的实体中心超边单元。所有变体使用相同的训练目标：给定状态和动作后预测符号效果或判断动作不可行。实验覆盖不同模型规模（0.5B-1.5B）、数据预算、分布内与分布外测试世界，并在下游贪婪规划任务中评估。

Result: 超边序列化在0.5B-1.5B模型和分布偏移条件下带来最显著的增益；更大的模型可缩小差距；成对三元组在分布内精确匹配上可匹敌或略超超边，但超边在分布外事实F1和中小规模下可行性检测与效果预测之间的权衡上表现最佳。在下游贪婪规划中，超边世界模型的成功率也是最高的。

Conclusion: 高阶状态组织（超边表示）是一种简单但有效的归纳偏置，尤其适用于模型容量有限或测试环境与训练分布不同的场景。

Abstract: World models enable language-model agents to predict environment dynamics and plan before acting. In text environments, the model must learn symbolic action effects from serialized state descriptions, but the role of serialization structure remains underexplored. We present HyperWorld, a controlled study of state serialization for learned textual world models. We compare raw observations with three symbolic serializations of the same ground-truth state: independent sentences, pairwise triples, and entity-centered hyperedge units that group multiple related facts around entities and relations. All variants use the same training objective: given a state and an action, predict symbolic effects or judge the action infeasible. Across model scales, data budgets, and in-distribution and out-of-distribution test worlds, hyperedge serialization gives the clearest gains for 0.5B--1.5B models and under distribution shift. Larger models reduce the gap, and pairwise triples can match or slightly exceed hyperedges on in-distribution exact match, but hyperedges achieve the strongest out-of-distribution fact F1 and the best small-to-medium scale trade-off between feasibility detection and effect prediction. In downstream greedy planning, the hyperedge world model also attains the highest success rate among the tested representations. These results show that higher-order state organization is a simple but effective inductive bias for learned symbolic world models, especially when model capacity is limited or test environments differ from training.

</details>


### [21] [I-CARE: Analysis of interference-related phenomena in a controllable, diverse and representative unlearning setting for text-to-image models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00003)
*Leonardo Santiago Benitez Pereira,Marcos Escudero Viñolo,Luis Herranz Arribas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出I-CARE，一个将"干扰"（interference）正式化为生成式机器遗忘研究对象的系统性方法论框架。I-CARE并非提出新基准或新算法，而是提供任务、指标和结果报告模板的形式化定义，使干扰研究在不同遗忘设置下可系统复现，并配有开源实现和Web可视化界面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式机器遗忘虽发展迅速，但"干扰"现象（即本应保留的语义相关概念被意外遗忘）长期以来缺乏清晰的表征和一致的评估方式，阻碍了该方向的研究进展与结果比较。

Method: I-CARE方法论为生成式遗忘中的干扰提供形式化的任务定义、评估指标和结果报告模板；设计上保持模型与算法演化下的长期有效性，将科学洞见与短期实证结果解耦；并采用现有最先进算法和常用数据集进行可行性验证。

Result: 可行性演示表明，I-CARE能够在多种遗忘设置下有效分析干扰模式，验证了该框架的实际适用性。同时提供了开源实现和基于Web的图形化界面，便于无编程背景的研究者探索实验结果。

Conclusion: I-CARE为生成式机器遗忘中的干扰研究提供了统一、规范且可复现的方法论基础，既适用于当前技术也具备面向未来的扩展性，是该领域标准化评估的重要一步。

Abstract: Machine unlearning studies the removal of knowledge from an AI model, making the system forget a concept it previously learned. Despite rapid progress in generative machine unlearning, the unintended degradation of semantically related concepts that should have been retained (henceforth, interference) remains poorly characterized and inconsistently evaluated. This paper introduces I-CARE, a methodology that formalizes interference as a first-class object of study in generative unlearning. Rather than proposing a new benchmark or unlearning algorithm, I-CARE provides formal definitions for tasks, metrics, and templates for reporting results, enabling the systematic and reproducible study of interference across unlearning settings. While our methodology is designed to remain valid as models and unlearning algorithms evolve, decoupling long-term scientific insight from transient empirical results, we present a feasibility demonstration with state-of-the-art algorithms and frequently used datasets. The results demonstrate that I-CARE enables meaningful analysis of interference patterns across multiple unlearning settings, establishing the practical applicability of the framework. The software implementation of the methodology is provided in an open-source framework, together with a web-based graphical interface that enables exploration of the outcomes of this study without requiring direct interaction with the codebase or specialized data analysis tools.

</details>


### [22] [Discrete-Time MDP Modeling for Multi-Item Capacitated Lot Sizing with Stochastic Demand Timing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00004)
*Léa Bayati,Mohamed Dahmoune,Melek Rodoplu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限时域内多物品有容量限制的批量调度问题，其中需求量确定但需求到达时段随机。作者将该问题建模为离散时间马尔可夫决策过程，并提出了一种遗传算法来搜索可行的状态反馈策略。在330个基准实例上，GA的平均最优性差距约为3.44%，在90个困难实例上保持低于5%的最优性差距阈值，平均优化加速比为6.89倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统确定性批量调度模型假设需求到达时段已知，但实际生产中需求到达时段往往具有随机性。本文旨在解决随机时序下多物品容量受限批量调度问题的计算难题，因为随机时序会显著增加状态空间和转移数量，导致精确求解困难，需要发展高效的启发式算法。

Method: 将该随机问题建模为离散时间马尔可夫决策过程（DTMDP），包含状态空间、可行动作、转移核和单周期成本函数。首先通过与确定性对应物比较来量化随机时序的计算影响，然后提出遗传算法（GA），该算法在可行状态反馈策略空间上搜索，并在DTMDP转移模型下对每个策略进行精确评估。

Result: 330个基准实例的实验表明：GA在可精确求解的实例上平均最优性差距约3.44%；在90个困难实例上保持低于5%的最优性差距阈值，平均加速比为6.89（95%置信区间为±1.41）；对于无法精确求解的实例，利用经验Bellman时间回归来估计缺失的精确求解时间并推算预期加速比。

Conclusion: 随机时序会显著增加问题计算复杂度，但所提出的遗传算法能够在保持可接受最优性差距（低于5%）的同时大幅缩短求解时间，为解决实际规模下的随机时序批量调度问题提供了一种高效可行的方案。

Abstract: This paper studies a finite-horizon multi-item capacitated lot-sizing problem in which demand quantities are deterministic, while demand-arrival periods are stochastic. Each demand occurs once within a known time window and must be satisfied no later than its deadline. The proposed model makes production and allocation decisions at the demand level, allowing it to represent capacity competition, demand-specific backlog, and allocation-dependent inventory dynamics. The stochastic problem is formulated as a discrete-time Markov decision process (DTMDP), including the state space, feasible actions, transition kernel, and one-period cost function. To isolate the computational effect of stochastic timing, each stochastic instance is first compared with a deterministic counterpart in which each arrival distribution is replaced by its most likely arrival period. This comparison shows that stochastic timing substantially increases the number of states, the number of transitions, solution time, and memory pressure. A genetic algorithm (GA) is then proposed for the stochastic-timing problem. The GA searches over feasible state-feedback policies and evaluates each policy exactly under the DTMDP transition model. Computational experiments on 330 benchmark instances show that the GA remains close to the exact stochastic solution whenever the latter is available, with an average optimality gap of about $3.44\%$. On the difficult benchmark instances, comprising 90 test cases, the GA remains below the $5\%$ optimality-gap threshold and achieves an average optimization speedup of $6.89 \pm 1.41$ at the $95\%$ confidence level. For instances that cannot be solved exactly on the available hardware, an empirical Bellman-time regression is used to estimate the missing exact resolution time and extrapolate the expected GA speedup.

</details>


### [23] [Incremental Risk Assessment of Progressive Elder Financial Scams via Instruction-Tuned Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00005)
*Parviz Ghafariasl,Weimin Fu,Xiaolong Guo,Shing I. Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于累积对话轮次的诈骗风险评估框架，通过逐步聚合对话内容并持续更新风险估计，实现对老年人金融诈骗的动态监测。研究构建了覆盖投资、慈善和技术支持三类骗局的多轮对话数据集，并对四个小型语言模型（Phi-4、LLaMA-3.2、DeepSeek-R1和Qwen3）在统一训练框架下进行微调与评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对老年人的金融诈骗日益通过文本和语音渠道（电子邮件、短信、电话）发生，且诈骗过程跨越多个对话轮次逐步展开——从冒充或寒暄开始，经过信任建立和紧迫感制造，最终达到索取敏感信息或转账的目的。由于风险信号是在多个对话轮次中渐进浮现的，现有方法难以在资源受限的部署环境中实现持续动态的诈骗监测。

Method: 提出累积式逐轮风险评估框架，在每一轮对话后增量聚合会话内容并重新估算风险水平。构建了每个对话包含2至8轮的多轮对话数据集，每个累积阶段均标注定性风险等级、连续风险分数、解释性理由和安全建议。在统一训练框架下对四个小型语言模型（Phi-4、LLaMA-3.2、DeepSeek-R1和Qwen3）进行微调。

Result: 微调后的小型模型能够捕捉诈骗相关语言线索和跨轮次升级模式，同时保持紧凑的模型架构以适应移动设备和资源受限场景。在评估的模型中，Phi-4和LLaMA-3.2在与其参数量相当的条件下实现了更强的逐轮风险估算性能。

Conclusion: 结构化累积建模方法能够支持面向部署场景的增量式诈骗风险评估，同时展示了紧凑语言模型在隐私感知和端侧欺诈防护方面的潜力。

Abstract: Financial scams targeting older adults increasingly occur through text and voice channels such as email, SMS, and phone calls, unfolding over multiple conversational turns that begin with impersonation or casual contact, escalate through trust building and urgency, and culminate in requests for sensitive information or financial transfers. Because risk signals emerge incrementally across turns, effective detection requires models that continuously update risk estimates under resource-constrained deployment settings. We propose a cumulative turn-based risk assessment framework that incrementally aggregates conversational turns and re-estimates risk at each step, enabling dynamic scam monitoring across progressively evolving conversations. A multi-turn dialogue dataset is constructed to cover investment, charity, and tech support scam scenarios, with each dialogue containing two to eight turns and annotated at every cumulative stage with a qualitative risk level, a continuous risk score, an explanatory rationale, and a safety recommendation. Four small language models (Phi-4, LLaMA-3.2, DeepSeek-R1, and Qwen3) are fine-tuned and evaluated under a unified training framework. Fine-tuned small models capture fraud-related linguistic cues and cross-turn escalation patterns while maintaining compact architectures suitable for mobile and resource-constrained deployment settings. Among the evaluated models, Phi-4 and LLaMA-3.2 achieve stronger turn-aware risk estimation performance relative to their parameter scale. These results suggest that structured cumulative modeling can support incremental scam risk assessment in deployment-oriented settings while highlighting the potential of compact language models for privacy-aware and on-device fraud protection.

</details>


### [24] [Long-Horizon State Tracking in LLMs: Executing MD5 through a Deep Sequence of Dependent Tool Calls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00012)
*Dheeraj Mohandas Pai,Lu Xian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过让LLM逐步计算MD5哈希（196次依赖调用、64轮），干净地测试其长程状态跟踪能力。结果显示gpt-oss-120b在多数完成任务中能正确携带状态并输出正确摘要，且发现保持推理上下文和投票机制是成功的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程任务在LLM评估中很少见，因为逐步依赖导致错误级联，端到端失败概率随长度急剧增长。现有agent基准混淆了状态跟踪难度与指令理解，缺乏隔离状态跟踪的控制组，且易受捷径影响，无法解释长程运行失败的原因。因此需要一种干净的方法来确认LLM是否能精确地在多次工具调用间携带中间状态。

Method: 让模型逐步计算MD5哈希——从RFC 1321从头实现，包含196个依赖工具调用、64轮，模型需在上下文中携带四个32位字(a,b,c,d)。设置两种实验：1) 直接用工具调用的标准设置；2) 最强设置中将每个原始工具替换为第二个LLM（worker），由driver和worker从头计算完整哈希。关键变量包括：保持模型自身推理在上下文中、对开启thinking的worker进行投票以消除模算术错误。

Result: gpt-oss-120b（每token仅~5.5B活跃参数的MoE模型）在温度0、短固定提示下，能在全部196次调用中携带完整状态，并在大多数完成任务中返回正确摘要。最强的双LLM设置中，保持推理上下文和投票机制是决定成功的关键因素。

Conclusion: LLM确实能在长程工具调用中携带精确的中间状态；成功不由权重改变决定，而由推理上下文保留和投票去错机制决定。残差失败可按来源分类（状态携带、算术、服务），为未来改进指明方向。

Abstract: Long-horizon tasks remain uncommon in large language model (LLM) evaluation, and for a reason: when each step depends on the last, per-step accuracy that looks excellent in isolation decays catastrophically, as errors cascade and the end-to-end failure probability grows sharply with length. Existing agentic benchmarks report end-to-end success but confound this state-tracking difficulty with instruction interpretation, give no control group that isolates it, and are vulnerable to shortcuts such as a hallucinated final answer, so they cannot say why a long run fails. Whether an LLM can carry exact intermediate state across many tool calls at all is itself not well established. We test this cleanly by having the model compute a cryptographic hash, MD5, step by step: a sequence of $196$ dependent tool calls over $64$ rounds while it carries four $32$-bit words $(a,b,c,d)$ in its own context from one call to the next. Interpretation is trivial and, because we implement MD5 from scratch (RFC~1321), we align every call to the ground-truth trace and check the digest to the bit, so any failure is pure bookkeeping. gpt-oss-120b, a mixture-of-experts model with only $\sim$5.5B active parameters per token, at temperature $0$ with a short fixed prompt, carries the full state across all $196$ calls and returns the correct digest on a majority of completed runs. In the strongest setting we replace every primitive tool with a second LLM, so a driver and a worker compute the whole hash from scratch with no exact-arithmetic oracle in the loop. Two ingredients decide success and neither changes the weights: keeping the model's own reasoning in its context each turn, and voting over a thinking-enabled worker to remove its modular-arithmetic slips. We localize the residual failures by origin, separating state-carrying from arithmetic and from serving.

</details>


### [25] [OpenAgentFlow: Enabling System-Wide Safety Boundaries for Heterogeneous AI Agent Fleets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00015)
*Dongsheng Chen,Xiangyu Zhao,Xin Yao,Xuetao Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出OpenAgentFlow，一个控制平面/动作平面架构的系统级安全框架，用于在异构AI代理系统中统一执行动作治理。该系统通过将GUI、API、工具调用和LLM生成的调用标准化为统一的AgentEvent流，并在执行前经由共享策略执行点路由，实现对代理动作的一致管理和审计。在Android上的测试显示，其准确率达94.0%，攻击拦截率达95.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI代理从独立助手演变为多代理异构系统，安全问题已成为系统级的动作治理问题——需要在动作改变共享状态之前判断是否应当提交。现有防护手段覆盖提示、工具调用、GUI动作等，但往往执行碎片化，难以发现跨多步动作流程中出现的风险，且在可审计性和策略演进方面支持有限。

Method: OpenAgentFlow采用控制平面/动作平面架构，在执行边界上强制安全。它将待执行的GUI动作、API调用、工具调用和LLM生成的调用统一规范化为AgentEvent流，通过共享的执行前策略执行点路由每个事件，并在控制平面中维护来源、会话状态、审计记录和可更新的策略。这样建立了可治理的共享动作流，允许新规则生效而无需修改代理、提示、模型或执行路径。系统在Android上实现。

Result: 在300个动作事件基准测试中实现94.0%准确率和95.3%攻击拦截率；在30例动态策略测试中，安装新规则后有27例符合预期行为；在100例Android模拟器套件的98条跟踪用例中，实现90.8%原始准确率和92.9%的跟踪调整通过率。

Conclusion: OpenAgentFlow为异构AI代理集群提供了实用的共享执行边界，验证了在统一动作流上进行策略执行的有效性，展示了该系统能够在跨GUI、API和LLM规划的动作场景中可靠地实施安全治理。

Abstract: AI agents powered by large language models are evolving from isolated assistants into heterogeneous systems in which multiple agents, planners, controllers, and execution backends operate over the same user or enterprise environment. In such settings, safety becomes a system-level action-governance problem: deciding whether concrete agent-generated actions should be committed before they modify shared state. Existing safeguards cover prompts, tool calls, GUI actions, and agent-local behavior, but often leave enforcement fragmented, obscure risks that emerge across multi-step action flows, and provide limited support for auditability and policy evolution.
  We present OpenAgentFlow, a control-plane/action-plane architecture that enforces safety at the action-commit boundary. It normalizes pending GUI actions, API calls, tool calls, and LLM-generated invocations into a unified AgentEvent stream, routes each event through a shared pre-execution Policy Enforcement Point, and maintains provenance, session state, audit records, and updatable policies in the control plane. This creates a shared governable action stream and allows new rules to take effect without modifying agents, prompts, models, or execution paths.
  We instantiate OpenAgentFlow on Android. On a 300-case action-event benchmark, it achieves 94.0% accuracy and a 95.3% attack block rate. On a 30-case dynamic-policy suite, it matches expected behavior in 27 cases after new rules are installed. Across 98 traced cases from a 100-case Android emulator suite, it achieves 90.8% raw accuracy and a 92.9% trace-adjusted pass rate across GUI, API, and LLM-planned cases. These results show that OpenAgentFlow provides a practical shared enforcement boundary for heterogeneous AI agent fleets.

</details>
