*Ranjit Raut,Aarav Subedi,Sagun Rai,Sudan Jha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SCAFFOLD，一个大规模计算机科学研究图表的结构化数据集，包含图表、标题、上下文、问答对和思维链推理标注，为训练视觉语言模型理解科研图表提供了数据基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机科学论文中的图表（架构图、流程图、管线示意图）承载大量信息，但目前缺乏将这类特定图表与标题、上下文、问题、答案及逐步推理配对标注的公开数据集，这阻碍了训练视觉语言模型理解此类图表。

Method: 利用布局检测和PDF解析技术从arXiv计算机科学论文中提取（图像、标题、上下文、问答、思维链）元组，并通过AI辅助问题生成步骤来构建数据集，产出SCAFFOLD-157K（大）、SCAFFOLD-37K（中）和SCAFFOLD-12K（小）三个规模的数据集。

Result: SCAFFOLD-157K涵盖3,058篇论文、29,887个图表及157,387个问答对；SCAFFOLD-37K包含36,797个问答对；SCAFFOLD-12K包含12,000个问答对。已使用SCAFFOLD-12K在Qwen2.5-VL-3B-Instruct上进行了基线实验。

Conclusion: SCAFFOLD填补了计算机科学图表图式问题回答数据集的空白，为训练和评估视觉语言模型理解科研图表能力提供了规模可扩展的基准资源。

Abstract: Computer science papers rely heavily on diagrams: architecture drawings, system flowcharts, and pipeline schematics that often carry more information than the text around them. There is currently no public dataset that pairs this specific kind of figure with captions, context, questions, answers, and step-by-step reasoning, which is exactly what is needed to train a vision-language model to understand them. We present \textbf{SCAFFOLD}\footnote{https://github.com/theranjitraut/scaffold}, a large-scale structured dataset of computer science research figures with diagram QA and Chain-of-Thought reasoning traces. This dataset consists of (image, caption, context, question-answer, chain-of-thought) tuples from arXiv computer science papers prepared using layout detection and PDF parsing, with an AI-assisted question-generation step. The resulting large-sized SCAFFOLD-157K dataset spans 3,058 papers with 29,887 figures (157,387 pairs), a medium-sized SCAFFOLD-37K dataset (36,797 pairs), and a small-sized SCAFFOLD-12K dataset (12,000 pairs). We used SCAFFOLD-12K for baseline experiments on Qwen2.5-VL-3B-Instruct.

</details>


### [27] [UI-Venus-2 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00028)
*Venus Team,Zhuohan Cai,Haoxing Chen,Jiaxuan Chen,Weizhi Chen,Changlong Gao,Zhangxuan Gu,Yuan Guo,Yusong Hu,Jianrong Jiang,Jianguo Li,Runze Li,Jinzhen Lin,Zhenyu Ma,Changhua Meng,Han Peng,Xinyu Qiu,Shuheng Shen,Zhongyi Shui,Weiqiang Wang,Ming Wen,Zhuoer Xu,Hang Yan,Kaiwen Yang,Ruilin Yao,Nanjun Yu,Zhengwen Zeng,Lianrui Zhang,Yunzhu Zhang,Zhe Zhao,Beitong Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: UI-Venus-2是一个跨移动、网页和桌面环境的通用GUI智能体，通过统一闭环推理-行动框架，在环境覆盖、任务构建和验证机制三个维度上联合扩展，并集成安全机制，旨在从基准导向模型迈向真实世界可部署的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态GUI智能体在数字任务自动化中展现出潜力，但从基准导向模型过渡到可靠的真实世界应用仍面临环境覆盖有限、任务构建脆弱和奖励验证不可靠三大挑战。

Method: 提出UI-Venus-2基础GUI智能体：(1)扩展环境覆盖至170多个多语言移动应用和桌面操作系统；(2)采用深度研究流水线生成基于功能定位的指令；(3)采用轨迹级和样本级评估器，结合视觉关键点和多模型投票提供可靠的强化学习信号；(4)集成安全感知机制以控制高风险操作的执行。

Result: UI-Venus-2在跨平台环境中实现了更强的泛化能力、可验证性和自我反思能力，作为开源基础模型推进了真实世界GUI智能体的发展。

Conclusion: 通过联合扩展环境、任务和验证三个维度，并引入安全机制，UI-Venus-2为构建通用、可靠且可部署的GUI智能体提供了可行的路径。

Abstract: Multimodal GUI agents have emerged as a promising paradigm for digital task automation, yet transitioning from benchmark-oriented models to dependable real-world applications remains challenging due to limited environment coverage, brittle task construction, and unreliable reward verification. In this work, we present UI-Venus-2, a general-purpose foundation GUI agent designed to operate across mobile, web, and desktop environments through a unified closed-loop reasoning-action framework. To bridge the gap toward practical deployment, we jointly scale three critical dimensions: (1) Environments, expanding coverage to more than 170 multilingual mobile apps and native desktop operating systems; (2) Tasks, employing a deep-research pipeline for function-grounded instruction generation; and (3) Verification, adopting trace-level and sample-level evaluators with visual keypoints and multi-model voting to ensure reliable RL signals for training. Furthermore, we integrate safety-aware mechanisms to ensure controlled execution of consequential actions. By offering a capable, efficient, and open-source foundation, UI-Venus-2 advances the field toward more generalizable, verifiable, and self-reflective agents for real-world applications.

</details>


### [28] [EULER: Exploring Underused Links with Evidence-Checked Return for Multi-Agent Mathematical Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00032)
*Ren Zhenzhuo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EULER是一个以"桥接"为搜索单元的多智能体系统，用于跨数学领域迁移问题。在120个近期猜想上评估，产生了10个证明、3个反例和45个部分结果。

它通过竞争直接领域、相邻领域和远距离领域三条路径，并要求桥接必须提供源表示无法执行的操作且目标侧证据能沿检查过的蕴含关系返回原命题。六项有序压力测试在昂贵搜索前拒绝无效桥接。

消融实验表明：桥接特定的压力测试将错误结论从9减至3；桥接材料与目标原生操作结合产生+4.2的正交互效应。值得注意的是，领域距离并不能可靠预测成功，可执行操作的增益和有效回传才是关键预测因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数学社区使用不同的对象、不变量和工具，跨领域迁移问题成本高昂且常被跳过。作者希望将这种"桥接"本身作为搜索的基本单元，系统化地实现跨数学领域的知识迁移。

Method: EULER采用多智能体架构，围绕固定猜想并行运行直接领域、相邻领域和远距离领域三条竞争路线。桥接必须满足两个条件：(1)提供源表示无法执行的操作；(2)目标侧证据通过受检的蕴含关系回到原始命题。系统设计了六道有序压力测试以在昂贵搜索开始前拒绝无效桥接。

Result: 在120个来自JCTA作者近期论文的冻结且防污染猜想上：产生10个证明、3个反例和45个有范围的局部结果。压力测试将错误结论从9降至3；桥接材料与目标原生操作的组合产生+4.2的正交互效应，超出任一单独因素的效果。

Conclusion: 领域距离并非成功桥接的可靠预测指标；真正重要的是可执行操作的增益以及证据能否有效回传至原始命题。高质量桥接的核心在于操作层面的互补性与逻辑可验证的回传路径。

Abstract: Mathematical communities work with different objects, invariants, and tools, so transferring a problem across them is expensive and often skipped. We present EULER, a multi-agent system that takes such a transfer--a bridge--as its unit of search. Around a fixed conjecture, EULER runs direct, adjacent-domain, and distant-domain routes in competition; a bridge keeps its budget only if it supplies an operation the source representation cannot execute and its target-side evidence returns to the original statement along a checked implication. Six ordered stress tests reject invalid bridges before expensive search begins.
  We evaluate EULER on 120 recent conjectures. The conjectures were frozen before search and screened for contamination, and are drawn from public papers by authors who had recently published in the Journal of Combinatorial Theory, Series A, a leading journal in combinatorics. EULER produced 10 proofs and 3 refutations, plus 45 scoped partial results. Two mechanisms held up under ablation: bridge-specific stress tests cut incorrect conclusions from 9 to 3, and bridge material combined with a target-native operation yielded a positive interaction of +4.2 resolved tasks that neither factor produced alone. Domain distance did not reliably predict success; executable operation gain and valid return did.

</details>


### [29] [When Prediction Error Is Not Enough: Evaluating Nuisance-Function Prediction for Causal Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00071)
*Cong Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过蒙特卡洛模拟，在部分线性模型中比较了OLS、GAMs、XGBoost和DML-XGBoost等方法的预测误差与因果估计性能之间的关系，发现预测误差并不能直接反映因果估计的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究预测误差（评估干扰函数估计器性能的常用指标）与因果估计器性能之间是否存在一致的关系，因为这两者可能在不同性能指标下存在差异。

Method: 在部分线性模型框架下进行蒙特卡洛模拟，比较四种方法（OLS、GAMs、XGBoost、DML-XGBoost），评估干扰函数的预测误差、偏差、RMSE和95%置信区间覆盖率，并检验一个基于暴露和结局干扰函数估计误差的绝对叉积的联合误差指标。

Result: XGBoost在非oracle方法中具有最低的RMSE，而DML-XGBoost通常提供更好的置信区间覆盖率。预测误差在不同方法和设置下不能一致地追踪因果偏差，点估计性能最佳的方法不一定有最好的置信区间覆盖率。联合误差指标与因果偏差仅存在弱关联。

Conclusion: 预测误差对于评估干扰函数估计是有用的，但不应被视为因果估计器质量的直接衡量指标。

Abstract: Prediction error is widely used to evaluate nuisance-function estimators in causal inference, but its relationship with causal estimator performance may differ across performance measures. We studied this question in a partially linear model using Monte Carlo simulations. We compared ordinary least squares (OLS), generalized additive models (GAMs), XGBoost, and Double Machine Learning with XGBoost (DML-XGBoost), evaluating nuisance-function prediction error, bias, RMSE, and 95\% confidence interval coverage. We also examined a simple joint-error measure based on the absolute cross-product of estimation errors from the exposure and outcome nuisance functions. Across the simulated settings, XGBoost had the lowest RMSE among the non-oracle methods, while DML-XGBoost generally provided better confidence interval coverage. Prediction error did not consistently track causal bias across methods and settings, and the method with the best point-estimation performance did not necessarily have the best confidence interval coverage. The joint-error measure was only weakly associated with causal bias and did not provide a useful standalone measure of causal performance. These results suggest that prediction error is useful for assessing nuisance-function estimation, but it should not be treated as a direct measure of the quality of the resulting causal estimator.

</details>


### [30] [MiNER: Fine-Tuned Biomedical Natural Language Processing for Malaria Disease Entity Recognition in Clinical Texts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00073)
*V. S. Anoop,Devika N*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于BioBERT微调的预训练生物医学语言模型，用于从疟疾科学文献中提取生物医学信息（命名实体识别），并通过领域标注数据和监督学习进行微调，实验证明其在精度、召回率和准确率上显著优于其他编码和机器学习方法，同时发布了人工标注数据集供研究者使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 疟疾仍是全球重大健康负担，其文献量庞大且持续增长，从海量文献中提取关键生物医学信息极具挑战，需要创新的自动化方法来解决这一信息抽取难题。

Method: 首先选择和预处理大量疟疾科学文献语料，并进行临床相关实体标注；然后利用BioBERT预训练语言模型将文本编码为上下文感知表示；最后通过领域特定标注和监督学习对模型进行微调，以增强生物医学命名实体的提取能力。

Result: 大量实验以及与不同编码和机器学习算法的对比表明，所提出的方法在精度、召回率和准确率上均显著优于基线方法。此外，作者还发布了人工标注的实体和关系提取数据集。

Conclusion: 微调的BioBERT模型在疟疾文献生物医学信息提取中表现出色，为健康信息学研究提供了有效工具和公开数据集，有助于推动该领域更先进的疟疾信息提取模型开发。

Abstract: Malaria remains a significant global health burden, necessitating continuous research efforts to understand its complex molecular mechanisms, epidemiology, and potential therapeutic interventions. Extracting essential biomedical information from the vast and constantly growing malaria literature is a challenging task that demands innovative approaches. Recently, pre-trained language models have revolutionized natural language processing tasks, demonstrating remarkable capabilities in various domains. This paper proposes a fine-tuned pre-trained biomedical language model for biomedical information extraction from scientific literature on malaria disease. The proposed methodology selects and preprocesses a large corpus of scientific articles on malaria, and then annotates them with entities of clinical significance. It then leverages BioBERT, a state-of-the-art pre-trained language model, to encode the textual data into context-aware representations. We fine-tune the model using domain-specific annotations and supervised learning to enhance its ability to extract relevant biomedical named entities. Extensive experiments and comparisons with different encoding and machine learning algorithms show that the proposed approach significantly outperforms them in precision, recall, and accuracy. We also publish our human-labeled dataset for entity and relation extraction to enable other health informatics researchers to train advanced models for malaria information extraction.

</details>


### [31] [AI Morbidity and Mortality: A Framework for Clinical AI Failure Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00076)
*Paulius Mui,Dean F. Sittig,Steve Labkoff,Sanjay Basu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AI发病率与死亡率（AI M&M）框架，这是一个结构化、免责的病例审查框架，用于对临床AI失败进行案例级审查和分析，帮助机构从个别AI相关错误中学习和改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床AI已广泛应用于实际医疗场景，但现有安全机制（如聚合模型监控和传统患者安全报告）无法解释风险如何在AI系统、临床医生、工作流程和机构控制之间的交互中产生，缺乏对个体AI相关错误和险失事件的重构与学习能力。

Method: 提出四个相互关联的分类维度：触发因素-机制-临床路径-纠正措施（Trigger-Mechanism-Clinical Pathway-Corrective Action），并采用标准化病例接收、证据保全、研究者级重构、工具参与归因和纠正措施追踪等结构性流程。

Result: 使用五个门诊用药和临床决策支持案例进行验证，两位独立临床审查员对全部四个分类轴共20个轴级分类均达成一致意见，初步证明了该框架分类的一致性和可行性。

Conclusion: AI M&M旨在补充而非替代现有模型监控、患者安全报告和监管监督，通过将个别AI工作流失败转化为可操作的机构学习；仍需跨机构、跨AI系统和跨临床环境的前瞻性评估验证其有效性。

Abstract: Clinical artificial intelligence is increasingly embedded in real-world care, yet existing safety mechanisms are poorly suited to reconstructing and learning from individual AI-related errors and near-misses. Aggregate model monitoring can identify performance changes, and traditional patient safety reporting can capture adverse events, but neither is designed to explain how risk emerges across the interaction among AI systems, clinicians, workflows, and institutional controls. We propose AI Morbidity and Mortality (AI M&M), a structured, blameless framework for case-based review of clinical AI failures. The framework combines standardized case intake, evidence preservation and investigator-level reconstruction, tool-in-loop attribution, and corrective-action tracking. Each event is classified across four linked dimensions: Trigger - Mechanism - Clinical Pathway - Corrective Action, separating the condition that exposed a vulnerability from the process that produced risk, its consequence for care, and the remediation assigned. We demonstrate the framework using five illustrative outpatient medication and clinical decision-support cases; two clinician reviewers independently applied all four classification axes and reached agreement across all 20 axis-level classifications. AI M&M is intended to complement, rather than replace, model monitoring, patient safety reporting, and regulatory oversight by converting individual AI-in-workflow failures into actionable institutional learning. Prospective evaluation across institutions, AI systems, and clinical settings is needed.

</details>


### [32] [Different representation learning objectives recover distinct latent structures from the same psychometric data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00100)
*Cong Cao,Tassos C. Kyriakides,Pambos Vrasidas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究使用757对师生配对数据，比较了不同表示学习目标（PCA、对比学习、多任务学习）从心理测量问卷中恢复潜在组织的能力。对比学习大幅提升师生检索性能（Top-1从0.13%到7.27%），但其保留行为表型结构的能力不如PCA表示；多任务目标部分恢复了行为组织但牺牲了检索性能。结果表明师生对应关系与行为表型是不同形式的潜在组织。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心理测量问卷包含丰富的条目级信息，但不同表示学习目标是否能恢复相同的潜在组织仍不清楚。作者希望探究这一问题，以便理解表征学习方法对心理测量数据潜在结构中不同维度的恢复能力差异。

Method: 使用塞浦路斯ProW学前试验基线评估中757对匹配的师生配对数据，从儿童SDQ、ASBI和CBRS条目响应中通过主成分分析和聚类来刻画行为结构并识别四种行为表型；比较PCA表示、对比学习目标和多任务目标下师生检索性能与行为表型结构保留程度的差异。

Result: 对比学习目标将师生检索Top-1准确率从0.13%提升至7.27%，Top-10准确率从1.98%提升至56.14%，大幅优于PCA表示；但对比表示对行为表型结构的保留效果不如PCA。多任务目标（联合优化对齐和行为预测）部分恢复了行为组织，但降低了检索性能。

Conclusion: 师生对应关系和行为表型代表两种不同的潜在组织形式；从链接的心理测量数据中恢复的潜在结构取决于所采用的表示学习目标。这意味着选择不同的表征学习方法会引导研究者发现数据中不同的组织结构。

Abstract: Psychometric questionnaires contain rich item-level information, yet it remains unclear whether different representation learning objectives recover the same latent organization. We investigated this question using 757 matched teacher-child pairs from the baseline assessment of the Cyprus ProW preschool trial. Behavioral structure was characterized from child SDQ, ASBI, and CBRS item responses using principal component analysis and clustering, yielding four behavioral phenotypes. A contrastive objective substantially improved teacher-child retrieval relative to PCA-based representations, increasing Top-1 accuracy from 0.13% to 7.27% and Top-10 accuracy from 1.98% to 56.14%. However, contrastive representations preserved behavioral phenotype structure less effectively than PCA-based representations. A multi-task objective jointly optimizing alignment and behavioral prediction partially restored behavioral organization but reduced retrieval performance. These findings indicate that teacher-child correspondence and behavioral phenotypes represent distinct forms of latent organization and demonstrate that the latent structure recovered from linked psychometric data depends on the representation learning objective.

</details>


### [33] [Deploying and Evaluating a Smart-Agriculture Agentic Engine for Full-Season Soybean Farm Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00106)
*Ao Qu,Panagiotis Michelakis,Linyuan Han,Yiannis Hadjiyianni,Kun Ouyang,Konstantinos Siskos,Feng Li,Ran Meng,Jingchi Jiang,Dimitrios Stamoulis,Jie Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了FAIRY，一个为哈尔滨工业大学智慧农业站点部署的全栈智慧农业智能体系统，围绕"万物皆事件"的执行范式构建，在100个全季大豆场景中评估了9种先进的智能体控制器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智慧农业需要能够执行复杂时空流程的智能体系统，但缺乏真实部署的全栈评估平台，特别是在机械化农业生产环节中验证智能体控制器的可行性。

Method: FAIRY采用"everything is an event"范式，将世界演变、遥感观测、传感器读数、作物生长、机械动作和管理干预统一表示为共享农场流程引擎中的状态变更事件，并在其上构建完整的智能体技术栈，包括农业技能知识库、多智能体控制器与编排后端、前沿与边缘模型执行、全路径追踪日志和本地节点部署分析。

Result: 系统已在运行中的大豆研究农场部署，在64垄田地中构建100个全季大豆场景，并结合智能体成功率、全路径时空正确性、token成本和边缘设备运行时构建了综合评估套件，对9种先进智能体控制器进行了评估。

Conclusion: FAIRY提供了一个覆盖从整地到仓储完整生育周期的智慧农业智能体全栈平台，通过统一的"事件"抽象和丰富的评估维度，为农业智能体研究提供了真实生产级的测试与评估基础设施。

Abstract: This paper presents FAIRY, a full-stack smart-agriculture agent system developed for and deployed to an operating soybean research farm at Harbin Institute of Technology's smart-agriculture site. We develop FAIRY to execute and evaluate agentic agronomic operations on full-season spatiotemporal workflows that span ridge preparation, planting, irrigation, fertilization, pest and disease treatment, harvest, grain handling, drying, and storage. FAIRY integrates APIs and infrastructure across production-grade machinery, fixed soil and canopy sensors, multispectral and thermal drones, satellite vegetation products, a weather station, calibrated crop-process models, agronomic records, and multi-season yield histories. The system is built around the novel "everything is an event" execution paradigm, which represents spatiotemporal world evolution, remote sensing and UAV observations, sensor readings, crop-growth transitions, machinery actions, and management interventions as state-changing events in a shared farm process engine. On top of this event-driven world model, FAIRY implements a complete agentic stack: a knowledge library of atomic agronomic skills; multi-agent controller and orchestration backends; frontier- and edge-model execution; full-path trace logging; and deployment profiling on local nodes. We use FAIRY to evaluate nine state-of-the-art agent controllers across one hundred full-season soybean scenarios that preserve the operational coupling between spatial observations in a 64-ridge field, temporal decision sequences, agronomic constraints, delayed effects, and final yield. We develop an evaluation suite that combines agentic success, full-path spatiotemporal correctness, token cost, and edge-device runtime.

</details>


### [34] [Recursive Criticality of AI Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00137)
*Mikhail Burtsev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究AI用于AI研发时产生的递归反馈是否会导致自我放大效应。作者提出一个递归再生产数R_AI，当R_AI>1时系统进入自我放大状态，当R_AI<1时反馈减弱。该转变取决于AI研发反馈回路的结构，而非模型能力水平，且自我放大可能在加速现象显现之前就已发生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI正越来越多地参与AI自身的研发过程，这种递归反馈可能产生自我放大效应，但何时会发生、受什么条件控制尚不清楚。理解这一点对于预测AI能力增长轨迹和潜在风险至关重要。

Method: 作者构建数学模型描述AI能力增长率与基线研究生产力、递归反馈强度及研究难度递增之间的关系，推导出递归再生产数R_AI作为判定反馈被放大还是衰减的关键指标，并将模型扩展至多研究主体场景以分析组织间共享进步的影响。

Result: 发现R_AI>1时自我放大，R_AI<1时反馈衰减；转变取决于反馈回路结构而非能力水平；基线生产力加速进步但不改变自我放大状态；研究难度增加可终结自我放大期；跨组织共享进步可使整个生态自我放大即使单个组织并非如此。

Conclusion: 该框架识别出可测量的AI研发系统属性（递归反馈强度、进步传播效率、周期时长、研究难度递增速率），可用于区分递归放大与由其他来源驱动的快速进步，为监测和预测AI发展提供理论工具。

Abstract: AI is increasingly used in the R\&D process that produces future AI systems. We study the conditions under which this feedback becomes self-amplifying. Our model describes how the rate of AI capability growth depends on baseline research productivity, recursive feedback, and the increasing difficulty of research progress. We derive a recursive reproduction number, $\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{AI}}$, that determines whether improvements are amplified or damped across development cycles. This quantity compares the strength of feedback with the rate at which further progress becomes more difficult. When $\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{AI}}>1$, the effects of improvements compound across development cycles, placing the system in a self-amplifying regime. When $\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{AI}}<1$, their effects weaken across cycles. The transition depends on the structure of the AI R\&D feedback loop and need not occur at any particular level of model capability. A system can therefore enter a self-amplifying regime before acceleration becomes visible, while rapid progress can also occur without self-amplification. Higher baseline research productivity can accelerate progress without changing whether the system is self-amplifying, but the duration of the development cycle becomes a limiting timescale for amplification. Increasing research difficulty can end a period of self-amplification. Extending the model to multiple research actors shows that improvements shared across organizations can make the overall research ecosystem self-amplifying even when no individual actor is. The framework identifies measurable properties of AI R\&D systems that can help distinguish recursive amplification from rapid progress driven by other sources, including the strength of recursive feedback, how effectively improvements propagate into successor systems, cycle duration, and the increasing difficulty of further progress.

</details>


### [35] [IMPACT: Attention Is the Interaction Map for Scalable Interaction-Aware World Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00161)
*Rongze Tang,Jianjie Fang,Zhaolu Wang,Ziyou Wang,Xvyuan Liu,Haisheng Su,Xin Zhang,Wei Wu,Chen Gao,Yong Li,Zhibo Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出IMPACT框架，通过发现全局MSE去噪目标下静态内容主导优化信号导致动态交互区域欠监督的问题，利用交叉注意力内部先验构建交互图来重新加权去噪监督，无需外部表示即可提升物理交互生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型在动作条件下未来预测中难以建模物理上合理的交互。现有方法依赖外部表示（运动、几何、语义）来约束生成过程，但这些表示需要辅助估计器或手动标注，限制了训练可扩展性。作者发现根本问题在于全局平均MSE去噪目标下存在监督分配不匹配：静态内容占据主导优化信号，而稀疏动态物体区域（对交互生成至关重要）被欠监督。

Method: IMPACT方法包含三个关键步骤：1)使用与操作对象token关联的交叉注意力作为动作条件变化的内部时空先验；2)从该先验中采样候选区域，并用分离的局部预测误差进行校准，构建交互图；3)利用交互图重新加权去噪监督信号。该方法不需要外部表示，也不需要在推理阶段进行修改。

Result: 在机器人臂和人手操作任务上进行了大量实验，涵盖多种控制模态和DiT骨干网络。结果表明IMPACT持续优于对应的MSE训练基线，在交互保真度、物理合理性和视觉质量方面均有提升。

Conclusion: 通过重新审视训练目标而发现监督分配不匹配问题，并利用模型内部注意力先验构建自适应加权机制，IMPACT提供了一种可扩展且无需外部标注的物理交互建模改进方案，验证了解决监督不匹配而非简单增加外部约束的有效性。

Abstract: World models have made remarkable progress in action-conditioned future prediction for embodied agents, yet still struggle to model physically plausible interactions. Existing approaches address this limitation by constraining the generation process with external representations encoding motion, geometry, or semantics. Obtaining these spatiotemporally dense representations typically requires auxiliary estimators or manual annotations, limiting training scalability. We instead revisit the training objective and identify a supervision-allocation mismatch under the globally averaged mean squared error (MSE) denoising objective: prevalent static content dominates the optimization signal, leaving sparse dynamic-object regions critical to interaction generation disproportionately under-supervised. Motivated by this observation, we introduce IMPACT, a scalable Interaction-aware Model training framework with Prior-guided Attention Calibration and Targeting. IMPACT uses cross-attention associated with manipulated-object tokens as an internal spatiotemporal prior for action-conditioned changes. It samples candidate regions from this prior, calibrates them with detached local prediction errors to construct an interaction map, and uses the map to reweight denoising supervision, requiring neither external representations nor inference-time modifications. Extensive experiments on robot-arm and human-hand manipulation, spanning diverse control modalities and DiT backbones, show that IMPACT consistently outperforms the corresponding MSE-trained baselines, improving interaction fidelity, physical plausibility, and visual quality.

</details>


### [36] [Asymmetries in Spontaneous and Instructed Deception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00180)
*Josiah Luikham*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了大语言模型（Llama-3.1-70B-Instruct）中受指令欺骗与自发性（非指令）欺骗之间的关系，通过方向几何、跨设置分类器和跨设置引导三种方法进行比较分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型有时会在未经指令的情况下自发欺骗用户，但现有关于模型欺骗的研究大多集中在受指令欺骗场景。理解受指令欺骗与自发性欺骗之间的关系，对于揭示模型欺骗的本质机制至关重要。

Method: 使用Llama-3.1-70B-Instruct模型，通过三种方法对比两种欺骗设置：（1）方向几何分析（比较两种欺骗方向的余弦相似度）；（2）跨设置训练分类器（检测两种设置间的迁移性能）；（3）跨设置引导（测试从一个设置推导的方向对另一个设置进行引导的效果）。

Result: 研究发现两种欺骗设置共享部分方向成分（余弦相似度约0.5），且检测与因果之间存在不对称迁移：自发性训练的分类器在受指令数据上表现优于反向迁移；受指令推导的方向在引导自发性提示时效果优于反向。此外，推导引导向量的最佳token位置与训练和应用分类器的最佳token位置不同。

Conclusion: 受指令欺骗与自发性欺骗既有共享的神经机制又有各自独特的特征，二者之间存在不对称性关系，这提示对模型欺骗的干预需要针对不同欺骗类型采用不同策略。

Abstract: Large language models sometimes deceive users without being instructed to. However, much of the study on deception in models involves instructed deception. We investigated the relationship between instructed and spontaneous (uninstructed) deception in Llama-3.1-70B-Instruct. We compared these two deception settings through direction geometry, cross-setting classifiers, and cross-setting steering. We found the two deception settings share a component of direction (cosine of approximately 0.5) and an asymmetry in the transfer between settings regarding detection and causation. Spontaneous trained classifiers performed better on instructed data than vice versa, and instructed derived directions performed better at steering spontaneous prompts than vice versa. Likewise the best token position to derive steering vectors from differed from the best token position to train and apply classifiers.

</details>


### [37] [Socrates went Nuclear: Comparing Interaction Strategies for AI systems in a Learning Context using Brain Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00584)
*Alexandre Clin Deffarges,Nataliya Kosmyna,Pattie Maes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究对比了三种AI辅助学习交互设计（无限制聊天机器人、苏格拉底式受限机器人、基于脑电信号的自适应辅导系统），发现无限制AI在即测中产生更高学习增益，但EEG显示自适应系统带来更高认知投入，聚类分析表明无限制AI用户多采用直接检索答案策略，其成功可能源于即时后测而非深层学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究无限制AI访问是否会绕过学习所需的认知努力，还是简化了知识获取过程，从而理解不同AI交互模式对学习效果的真实影响。

Method: 50名参与者在零先验知识的核安全协议领域进行学习实验，分为三种交互条件：无限制对话机器人、苏格拉底式受限机器人（仅给提示不给答案）、基于脑电认知投入的自适应辅导系统。流程包括教学视频、前测、AI驱动评估和后测，使用Muse头带采集脑电信号。

Result: 无限制聊天机器人的学习增益显著高于两种受限模式（p<.03, d>0.80）；自适应条件下的EEG认知投入显著更高（p=.018）；聚类分析显示无限制模式下用户多采用直接答案检索策略，而苏格拉底模式用户初期尝试推理后逐渐脱离参与。

Conclusion: 无限制AI的成功并不代表更深层的学习，更可能是即时后测评估的结果。AI辅助学习的表面学习增益可能掩盖了真正的认知投入和深层理解不足的问题。

Abstract: Does unrestricted AI access bypass the cognitive effort required for learning, or does it streamline knowledge acquisition? This paper reports on a study where we compare three designs for user-AI interaction in a learning context: (1) an unrestricted conversational bot like ChatGPT, (2) a pedagogically constrained bot that guides through hints without giving final answers, which we refer to as the Socratic mode; and (3) a non-conversational adaptive tutoring system that adjusts difficulty in real-time based on the user's cognitive engagement derived from the brain signals. Fifty study participants were tasked with learning about nuclear safety protocols, a domain chosen for its zero-prior knowledge baseline. The participants progressed through an instructional video, a pre-test, an AI-driven assessment phase, which varied in the three conditions, and an immediate post-test. The nature of the questions centered primarily on factual knowledge acquisition, but it still required participants to have a global understanding of the concepts in order to answer the questions correctly. A Muse headband was used to derive the cognitive engagement of all users in all conditions. The unrestricted chatbot produced higher learning gains (delta) than both constrained modes (p < .03, d > 0.80), while the adaptive condition generated significantly higher EEG engagement (p = .018). The cluster analysis of chatbot usage and discussion patterns by users showed that most participants in the unrestricted-mode adopted a direct answer-retrieval strategy, while participants in the Socratic-mode initially attempted to reason through the hints before progressively disengaging. Consequently, this also suggests that the success of the unrestricted AI is not an evidence of deeper learning, but rather a result of the immediate post-test evaluation after the training phase.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 提出两种新的偏差测试方法论：1) "All Else Being Equal"测试——在控制其他因素不变的情况下，仅改变单一变量（如行人的性别、种族等）来测试模型决策是否受影响；2) "Self-Consistency"测试——评估模型在相似情境下的决策一致性。该方法论同时适用于LLMs和VLMs。

Result: 研究发现LLMs和VLMs的行人让行决策受到行人性别、种族、宗教、残疾、年龄、肤色和社会经济地位等因素的影响，存在系统性的偏见。不同模型的偏差类型和程度各不相同，但存在一些共同模式。

Conclusion: 研究结果对"common sense"模型范式提出了质疑，指出需要要么修订该范式，要么解决下游的偏见问题，以确保AV决策的公平性。

Abstract: Public trust in Autonomous Vehicles (AVs) may depend not only on technical success but also on the fairness of their decision making. While a recent trend in AV research involves using general purpose "common sense" models to guide AV decision making, the degree to which these inherit human biases in driving is still understudied. Given that psychology studies have shown human driver biases exist, such as lower pedestrian-yielding rates to Black pedestrians in the US, we argue that analyses of model bias should also be part of AV evaluation. Concretely, in this paper we propose two new bias testing methodologies for Large Language Models (LLMs) and Visual-Language Models (VLMs)-"All Else Being Equal" tests and "Self-Consistency" tests-in order to assess bias in pedestrian-yielding decisions. Our findings show that both LLMs and VLMs make yielding decisions which are influenced by pedestrian gender, ethnicity, religion, disability, age, skin tone and socio-economic status. While the type and degree of bias is different from model to model, we highlight common patterns-and raise questions about the "common sense" model paradigm, particularly the need to either revise the paradigm or address issues of downstream bias.

</details>


### [39] [ReDeck: Step-Level Render-Grounded Refinement for Document-to-Slide Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00194)
*Muzhao Tian,Zezi Zeng,Yifan Yang,Xin Gao,Yan Li,Zisu Huang,Xiaohua Wang,Changze Lv,Mingxi Cheng,Bei Liu,Kai Qiu,Qi Dai,Dong Chen,Yue Dong,Xiaoqing Zheng,Ji Li,Chong Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ReDeck，一个步骤级渲染反馈驱动的幻灯片精炼框架，将幻灯片修订分解为原子编辑动作，每次编辑后立即返回渲染器观察结果，实现"一次编辑、一次观察"的精细反馈循环。同时引入DeckQuiz基准来解耦内容保真度、空间正确性和设计质量的评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻灯片生成智能体采用迭代反思机制，但遵循"一个版本、一个反馈"的单一循环模式，幻灯片被整体重写后才渲染和批判，导致溢出、重叠、裁剪、越界等局部错误难以定位和修复，缺乏精细化反馈机制。

Method: ReDeck框架包含三个关键组件：1）步骤级渲染反馈用于检测空间错误；2）回合级自适应批判器提供语义和设计指导；3）提交级门控进行严格布局验证。采用多粒度反馈机制，在每一步编辑后提供渲染器观察结果。

Result: 在GPT-5.4、Claude-4.6和Gemini-3.1模型上的实验表明，ReDeck在不同模型上都优于现有幻灯片生成智能体。消融实验证实反馈时机和粒度对于可靠的幻灯片精炼至关重要。

Conclusion: 反馈的时机与粒度是幻灯片生成精炼质量的关键因素，步骤级渲染反馈机制能够有效平衡局部修复与全局质量，为可编辑密集文档的生成提供了一种有效范式。

Abstract: Document-to-slide generation is challenging because slides are dense editable artifacts that require both faithful content selection and precise spatial layout. Recent slide agents adopt iterative reflection, but typically follow a monolithic "one version, one feedback" loop: a slide or deck is rewritten, rendered afterward, and critiqued only at the turn boundary. This delayed feedback makes local failures such as overflow, overlap, clipping, and off-canvas placement difficult to attribute and repair. We propose ReDeck, a step-level render-grounded refinement framework that decomposes slide revision into atomic edit actions and returns renderer-derived observations after each step, turning refinement into "one edit, one observation." To balance local repair with global quality, ReDeck uses multi-granular feedback: step-level render feedback for spatial errors, a turn-level adaptive critic for semantic and design guidance, and a submission-level gate for hard layout validation. We further introduce DeckQuiz, a benchmark that decouples content fidelity, spatial correctness, and design quality. Across GPT-5.4, Claude-4.6, and Gemini-3.1, ReDeck consistently outperforms existing slide-generation agents, and ablations confirm that feedback timing and granularity are critical for reliable slide refinement.

</details>


### [40] [AI Should Not Only Be Helpful. It Should Be Contingent. Artificial Intimacy, Sycophancy, and the Future of Social Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00211)
*Scott Compton,Arjun Nagendran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将"偶然性"（contingency）作为评估对话式AI系统的核心维度，指出当前对齐方法（如RLHF）过度追求用户满意度和对话流畅性，导致AI产生谄媚性的非偶然性肯定。作者借鉴行为科学和社会学习理论，提出AI反馈应与社会后果紧密耦合，以支持人类（尤其是青少年）的人际技能发展，并提出了一个包含轨迹评估和社会后果预测模型的"偶然性AI"框架及跨学科研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话式AI已深度嵌入日常社交环境，既作为信息工具也作为人际反馈来源。然而当前AI对齐方法优先优化用户满意度而非行为信息性反馈，产生了谄媚式的非偶然性肯定模式，这可能削弱用户在真实社交互动中的适应校准能力，尤其是在社会发展的关键期（青春期）。评估AI系统不能只看用户满意度，还需考虑其对人类社交学习的影响。

Method: 本文提出一个偶然性AI框架，包括：（1）将偶然性——即系统响应随用户行为变化及人际后果的程度——作为核心评估构建；（2）基于轨迹的评估方法（trajectory-based evaluation）；（3）社会后果预测模型（social consequence prediction models）；（4）一个跨发展心理学、人机交互和机器学习的综合性研究议程。

Result: 本文是观点性/框架性论文，主要产出为概念框架和研究议程，而非实证结果。其核心论点在于论证当前RLHF等方法倾向于造成谄媚性、非偶然性的肯定模式，并提出将偶然性反馈作为AI设计原则和评估标准的替代路径。

Conclusion: AI系统不应仅以用户满意度为评估标准，而应评估其对人类社交学习的影响。具体而言，AI需要提供与社会后果具有强耦合的偶然性反馈，从而帮助用户（特别是青少年）在真实人际互动中获得适应性校准机会。作者倡导发展一种以社会学习为中心的AI评估和设计新范式。

Abstract: Conversational artificial intelligence is increasingly embedded in everyday social environments, where it functions as both an informational tool and a source of interpersonal feedback. This perspective introduces contingency, i.e., the degree to which system responses vary with user behavior and its interpersonal consequences, as a central construct for evaluating AI systems. We argue that current alignment approaches, including reinforcement learning from human feedback, tend to prioritize user approval and conversational fluency over behaviorally informative feedback, leading to sycophantic patterns of noncontingent affirmation.
  Drawing on behavioral science and social learning theory, we propose that contingent feedback is a key mechanism through which individuals develop interpersonal skills. When AI systems provide feedback weakly coupled to social consequences, they may reduce opportunities for adaptive calibration in real-world interactions, particularly during adolescence, a critical period for social development.
  We outline a framework for contingent AI, including trajectory-based evaluation and models of social consequence prediction, and propose a research agenda spanning developmental psychology, human-AI interaction, and machine learning. More broadly, we argue that AI systems should be evaluated not only by user satisfaction, but by their impact on human social learning.

</details>


### [41] [ConvDeck: Conversational Paper-to-Slide Generation via Stage-Specific User Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00226)
*Tarik Can Ozden,Sachidanand VS,Furkan Horoz,Ozgur Kara,Dilek Hakkani-Tür,Junho Kim,James Matthew Rehg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ConvDeck，一个用于学术论文到幻灯片自动生成的多智能体对话系统。与传统方法在完成整个PPT后才允许用户反馈不同，ConvDeck在管线的不同阶段（大纲构建阶段和最终幻灯片生成阶段）设置独立的对话循环，使用户能在决策发生的环节即时参与迭代优化，从而提升用户目标满意度而不损害叙事连贯性、内容质量和视觉呈现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学术论文到幻灯片的自动生成本质上是迭代过程，需要反复的生成、批判和修订。现有方法存在两大局限：多智能体系统的内部批判-修订循环对用户封闭，用户无法参与；对话式方法虽然在最终的完整幻灯片生成后才引入用户反馈，但反馈时机过晚，限制了用户在叙事流、内容分配和演示重点等关键环节的迭代参与能力。

Method: ConvDeck是一个多智能体论文到幻灯片生成管线，核心创新在于将交互分布到管线的各个阶段，通过阶段特定的对话循环让用户在大纲构建和最终幻灯片生成两个关键决策阶段参与迭代优化。该方法采用可思考（think）、可发言（speak）、可行动（act）的智能体反思机制，智能体既能直接执行用户编辑指令，也能以对话方式回应用户反馈、澄清需求和讨论修订选项。

Result: 实验评估表明，阶段特定的对话反馈机制能够显著提高用户目标的满意度，同时保持叙事连贯性、内容质量和视觉呈现效果不下降。

Conclusion: ConvDeck验证了将对话式反馈嵌入到生成管线各阶段的有效性：相比仅在完整幻灯片生成后收集反馈，在决策发生的阶段即时引入用户参与能够更好地实现用户意图，且不会对生成质量产生负面影响，为可交互的自动化演示文稿生成提供了新的设计范式。

Abstract: Automatic academic paper-to-slide generation is inherently iterative, because creating an effective presentation requires repeated cycles of generation, critique, and revision. Recent multi-agent systems partially acknowledge this through internal critique-and-revise loops, while conversational approaches allow users to refine generated slide decks through dialog. However, these refinement processes either remain largely closed to the user or introduce feedback only after a complete deck has been produced, limiting the user's ability to participate in the iterative refinement of narrative flow, content allocation, and presentation emphasis. To address this gap, we introduce ConvDeck, a multi-agent pipeline for conversational paper-to-slide generation that distributes interaction across the pipeline through stage-specific loops, allowing users to iteratively refine both the presentation outline and the final slide deck at the stages where each kind of decision is made. These loops are driven by a refinement mechanism in which agents can think, speak, and act, enabling them to either directly apply edits or respond conversationally to clarify user feedback and discuss revision options. Our evaluation shows that stage-specific conversational feedback improves user-goal satisfaction while preserving narrative coherence, content quality, and visual presentation.

</details>


### [42] [Learning What to Retain: Gated-Memory Routing for Efficient Collaboration in Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00237)
*Rakibul Hasan Rajib,Mengxing Zheng,Qian Lou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Gated-Memory Routing框架，通过学习记忆写门和检索门构建紧凑的执行记忆，仅提交非冗余推理步骤并在各决策点提供给智能体相关子集，实现自适应角色选择与早停，在五个基准上达到最佳平均精度并大幅降低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的多智能体系统的编排需要适应不断演变的协作状态，但仅从查询路由无法适应中间进展或错误，而从完整执行历史路由则会造成历史过载、成本膨胀。因此需要一种既能捕获有用进展又不积累冗余上下文的紧凑状态表示。

Method: 提出Gated-Memory Routing框架：(1) 学习的Memory Write Gate只提交非冗余推理步骤到执行记忆；(2) 学习的Retrieval Gate为每个智能体提供紧凑的、相关的记忆子集；(3) 系统每步基于记忆选择下一个角色和骨干模型；(4) Adaptive Halting Controller在记忆包含足够证据时停止执行。

Result: 在五个推理和代码生成基准上，达到最佳平均准确率，超过最强基线2.44个百分点；在HumanEval上将推理成本相对于基线降低31.9%。

Conclusion: 通过学习门控记忆实现紧凑而信息丰富的编排状态，能同时提升多智能体系统的准确性和效率，为LLM多智能体协作提供了一种可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based multi-agent systems tackle complex reasoning by orchestrating how multiple agents are configured and how they collaborate. A central challenge is to adapt orchestration to the evolving collaboration state. Routing from the query alone cannot adapt to intermediate progress or errors, which hurts accuracy. Routing from the complete execution history supplies this missing context, but forces later decisions to process every prior step, including redundant or low-utility ones. This creates an execution-history overload that inflates cost. Effective orchestration instead requires a compact state that captures useful progress without accumulating redundant context. We propose Gated-Memory Routing, which conditions each decision on the query and a learned execution memory. A learned Memory Write Gate commits only non-redundant reasoning steps, and a learned Retrieval Gate supplies each agent a compact, relevant subset, so every decision conditions on a clean, informative state. At each step, the system selects the next role and backbone from this memory, while an Adaptive Halting Controller stops execution once the memory contains sufficient evidence for answering. Across five reasoning and code-generation benchmarks, our framework is both effective and efficient: it attains the best average accuracy, exceeding the strongest baseline by 2.44 points, while reducing HumanEval inference cost by 31.9% relative to that baseline. Code is available at https://github.com/rajibrhasan/gated-memory-routing

</details>


### [43] [Invalidation Contracts for Cross-Episode Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00243)
*Michael Wu,Arquimedes Canedo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"失效契约"（invalidation contracts）协议层，为LLM agent的API错误恢复建议附加版本戳和可缓存性提示，使客户端能精准清除因服务端数据漂移而失效的缓存项，同时保留仍有效的缓存，从而在不牺牲token节省的前提下避免静默失败。实验覆盖7个模型、3条服务路径、2个领域、约9400个episode，行级失效在部分模型上提升首试合规率最高66.7个百分点，并在4/7模型上恢复29-33%的基准token成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agent缓存API错误恢复建议可节省token和模型调用，但服务端数据漂移会使缓存修复失效并导致静默失败；而每轮重新推导又抵消了节省。现有方法无法在两者之间取得平衡，需要一种机制来精准识别并清除失效缓存项。

Method: 提出失效契约协议层：为每条恢复建议附加版本戳（version stamp）和可缓存性提示（cacheability hints），客户端据此无试错地驱逐过期条目。契约将节省分解为两个独立因子：有效性（缓存建议在漂移后仍正确的比例，仅由协议决定且与供应商无关）和合规性（planner首次尝试即采纳的比例，取决于模型行为）。评估行级与表级两种失效粒度。

Result: 行级失效使7个模型中的合规率提升0-66.7个百分点（3个模型提升55.6-66.7），在7个模型中的4个恢复29-33%的基准token成本；表级失效破坏共存条目，使5/7模型的后漂移首试率降至0%。驱逐精确率达1.00，契约仅增加15%响应负载，版本戳有效性在所有模型和服务路径上结果一致，零契约失败。

Conclusion: 失效契约能有效地在模型间实现精准的缓存失效管理：行级粒度远优于表级粒度，可实现的节省因planner模型而异（Claude Haiku 4.5达100%首试合规率，而Claude Sonnet 5仅11%或更低，表现出现输入schema保守主义）。协议层面的版本戳机制具有确定性和供应商无关性。

Abstract: LLM agents that cache recovery suggestions from API errors can skip re-derivation in later episodes, spending fewer tokens and fewer model calls on constraints they have already learned. Server-side data drift turns those cached fixes into silent failures, and the usual remedy, re-deriving on every episode, gives the savings back. We introduce invalidation contracts, a protocol layer that attaches version stamps and cacheability hints to every recovery suggestion so the client can evict stale entries without trial and error, and keep the rest. The contract decomposes realized savings into two independent factors: validity, the fraction of cached suggestions that remain correct after a drift event, and compliance, the fraction the planner applies on the first attempt. Validity depends only on the protocol and is vendor-independent. Compliance depends on the planner model: identical wire bytes yield 100% first-try compliance on Claude Haiku 4.5 and 11% or below on Claude Sonnet 5, which exhibits input-schema conservatism, refusing fixes that add fields the original request did not contain. We evaluate across seven models, three serving paths, two domains, and approximately 9,400 episodes. Row-level invalidation raises compliance by 0 to 66.7 percentage points across the seven models, 55.6 to 66.7 on three, and recovers 29-33% of baseline token cost on four of seven models, while table-level invalidation destroys co-located entries and drops post-drift first-try rates to 0% on five of seven. Eviction precision is 1.00 at row granularity on every model under the row-level oracle of Section 4.1. The contract adds 15% to response payload. Version-stamp validity is deterministic by construction and produced identical results across every model and serving path, with zero contract failures in the entire evaluation.

</details>


### [44] [Authority Bias in Conversational Search Engines for Academic Paper Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00248)
*Uthman Jinadu,Parsa Ghazvinian,Anjila Budathoki,Benjamin M. Ampel,Rajshekhar Sunderraman,Yi Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过反事实实验（原文、翻转、提升三种权威元数据条件）系统检验了8个LLM在学术文献推荐中的权威偏见，发现模型显著倾向于基于作者声望、期刊和引用等权威信号而非论文内容做判断，且这种偏见在不同模型间差异明显，仅靠提示词去偏难以完全消除，并揭示了"说做差距"（say-do gap）导致表面审计低估真实行为偏见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地被用作学术文献的对话式搜索引擎，但现有研究未通过因果实验检验模型是基于论文内容还是权威信号（作者声望、期刊、引用量）来判断论文。这种权威偏见的存在与否及其程度在因果层面尚未被验证。

Method: 在保持标题和摘要不变的前提下，系统性地操控权威元数据，设计三种反事实条件（原始、翻转、提升），对8个LLM（5个开源权重模型和3个前沿闭源模型）进行上下文内、单轮、top-1文献推荐实验，并通过提示级去偏干预来测试偏见可消除性。

Result: 权威偏见显著且具有方向性，在不同模型间差异明显；提示级去偏只能部分缓解该偏见。进一步发现"说做差距"：去偏指令显著抑制了权威提及，但权威驱动的偏好翻转下降远慢于提及抑制，表明表面审计会系统性低估行为偏见。

Conclusion: LLM在学术文献推荐中存在显著的权威偏见，且难以通过简单的提示工程完全消除；更关键的是，仅靠检测模型是否提及权威信息来评估偏见会产生系统性低估，需要关注模型的实际决策行为而非表面表述。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used as conversational search engines for academic literature, yet whether they judge papers on content or on authority signals has not been tested causally. We investigate authority bias: systematic preference for papers based on author prestige, venue, and citations rather than content. Holding title and abstract constant, we vary authority metadata across three counterfactual conditions (original, flipped, boosted) over eight LLMs (five open-weight and three frontier closed-weight) in an in-context, single-turn, top-1 recommendation setting. Our experiments show that authority bias is substantial and directional, varies markedly across models, and is only partially addressable through prompt-level debiasing. We further document a say-do gap: debiasing instructions suppress authority mentions far faster than authority-driven flips, so surface auditing systematically underestimates behavioral bias.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: HypReflect框架包含三个核心步骤：(1) 从多样化的用户信号中推断显式、不确定性感知的偏好假设；(2) 随着新证据积累，反思性地细化和更新这些假设；(3) 通过假设引导的自蒸馏方式将形成的用户模型融入LLM系统。

Abstract: As people increasingly interact with LLM assistants in daily life, continually adapting to individual preferences has become essential for effective long-term interactions. However, user preferences are rarely stated in full, and instead emerge through heterogeneous, latent, and noisy signals, with existing methods relying on raw interaction histories or costly reward-based optimization to manage personalization. We introduce HypReflect, a reliable, scalable framework for continual personalization that infers explicit, uncertainty-aware preference hypotheses from diverse user signals, reflectively refines them as new evidence accumulates, and incorporates the resulting user model through hypotheses-guided self-distillation. Experiments across three personalization settings: online personalization, multi-session interactions, and implicit behavioral signals, show that HypReflect outperforms a range of baselines, including raw-history and incremental-update methods. We further demonstrate strong generalization to unseen users and cross-domain settings, along with stability across context budgets, reusable hypotheses, and more focused personalization. These results suggest a step towards reliable and scalable continual personalization through explicit, revisable user preference hypotheses.

</details>


### [46] [The Answer Is Not the Argument](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00264)
*Will Yeadon,Sergio Juárez,Paul Mackay,T. J. Dowling,Elise Agra,Oto-obong Inyang,Arin Mizouri,Craig P. Testrow*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了在AI推理监控评估中，监控器是否访问可信参考答案（即答案访问）究竟提升的是推理验证能力，还是仅仅暴露了错误结论。通过对237个来自三个前沿模型在79道物理题上的逐步解答进行分析，发现答案访问将平衡准确率从0.637提升至0.796，但对"关键轨迹"（答案正确但推理含真实错误）的错误识别召回率反而从0.521降至0.438。结论是：答案访问改善的是结论一致性检查，而非对支撑论证的独立验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有思维链（Chain-of-thought）监控评估通常给监控器提供可信参考答案，但尚未弄清这种答案访问是否真正提升了推理验证能力，抑或只是让监控器更容易发现错误的最终结论。这关系到AI安全中监控能力的可信度评估。

Method: 收集了三个前沿模型在79道Humanity's Last Exam物理题上的237个带逐步编号的解答（无插入错误），独立标注最终答案正确性与首个错误步骤。参考标准结合物理学家注释、独立LLM辩论和源掩蔽裁决，识别出24条答案正确但推理含真实错误的关键轨迹。随后让8个LLM监控器分别在无答案、未验证答案、认证答案或盲目承诺后评估这些轨迹。

Result: 认证答案将平均平衡准确率从0.637提升至0.796，首错定位从0.261升至0.379。认证在错误答案轨迹上将召回率从0.653升至0.951，但在关键轨迹上从0.521降至0.438，且8个监控器方向一致（95% CI [+0.256, +0.506]）。盲目承诺后，监控器对先前放过的错误答案轨迹新标记93.8%为错误，但仅对18.0%的关键轨迹做出新标记。

Conclusion: 答案访问主要提升的是结论一致性检查，而非对支撑论证的独立验证。可接受输出并不证明产生该输出的过程是健全的，这为AI安全提供了一种良性的奖励破解（reward hacking）类比。即使本研究的错误是普通且非对抗性的，可信答案评估也可能高估监控能力——当可接受的输出掩盖了不健全的推理时尤其如此。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought monitoring is proposed for AI oversight, yet evaluations often provide monitors with a trusted reference answer. We ask whether answer access improves reasoning verification or mainly exposes incorrect conclusions. We collected 237 step-numbered solutions to 79 Humanity's Last Exam physics questions from three frontier models, with no inserted errors, and independently labelled final-answer correctness and the first false step. The reference standard combined physicist annotations, an independent LLM debate, and source-masked adjudication. This yielded 24 critical traces in which the answer was correct but the trace contained a genuine error. 8 LLM monitors evaluated traces blind, with an unverified or certified answer, or after a blind commitment. Certification raised mean balanced accuracy from 0.637 to 0.796, while exact first-error localization rose from 0.261 to 0.379. Certification changed recall (the fraction of error traces flagged as erroneous) from 0.653 to 0.951 on wrong-answer traces but from 0.521 to 0.438 on critical traces; the contrast had the same direction for all 8 monitors (question-bootstrap 95% CI [+0.256, +0.506]). After blind commitment, monitors shown the answer newly flagged 93.8% of previously passed wrong-answer traces as erroneous, but only 18.0% of critical traces. Answer access therefore improves conclusion-consistency checking rather than independent verification of the supporting argument. For AI safety, these traces provide a benign analogue of reward hacking: an acceptable output does not establish that the process producing it was sound. Although the errors studied here were ordinary and mostly non-load-bearing rather than adversarial, trusted-answer evaluations may similarly overstate monitoring capability when acceptable outputs conceal unsound reasoning.

</details>


### [47] [Autoresearch for Marketplace Catalogs: From Legacy Forms to AI-Native Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00274)
*Kartik Ravisankar,Hojat Abdolanezhad,Daniel Capo,Sang Su Lee,Shishir Dash,Vijay Anand Raghavan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自动研究循环（autoresearch loop），在双向服务市场中逐职业生成供给侧偏好分类体系，该体系已在美国大型消费者服务市场平台生产环境中部署，覆盖132个职业。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 服务市场正从基于固定表单的确定性请求转向由大语言模型驱动的AI原生概率匹配，这要求平台重新生成供给侧偏好分类体系，使其既能被服务提供者理解，又能为市场决策提供有效信号。

Method: 该方法将每个职业视为独立的生成问题，运行迭代的"提出-评估-保留"细化循环。每个候选标签集通过六项评估标准的大语言模型法官框架打分，并由7位不同人物角色的评审委员会以加权惩罚方式调整得分（无硬性否决）。另有独立的奇偶映射阶段，将传统表单的问答对映射回新生成的分类体系——通过推断每个传统问题旨在衡量的供应商属性而非逐字翻译。

Result: 该系统已在生产环境中部署，自2026年4月起服务于132个职业的分类生成，通过奇偶映射提供覆盖率信号及人工质量保证接口。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将分类生成从单一全局层级分解为逐职业独立生成问题，结合多角度LLM评审与属性级映射的质量保证机制，可在真实大规模市场环境中实现可部署的AI原生分类体系。

Abstract: Two-sided service marketplaces are moving from deterministic request-form intake to AI-native probabilistic matching, enabled by large language models (LLMs) that infer intent, preferences, and latent constraints from natural language. Relying on inferred intent rather than fixed-form fields forces these platforms to regenerate the provider-side preference taxonomy underwriting matching, search, and pricing: attributes interpretable to service providers while remaining a useful signal for marketplace decisions. We present an autoresearch loop that generates this taxonomy, one occupation at a time, and has been deployed in production at a major U.S. consumer services marketplace since April 2026, spanning 132 occupations. Instead of one global hierarchy, the loop treats each occupation as an independent generation problem and runs iterative propose-evaluate-keep refinement cycles. Each candidate tag set is scored by a recalibrated six-rubric LLM-as-judge framework, and a 7-critic panel of distinct personas contributes weighted penalties to an adjusted score, with no hard vetoes. A separate parity-mapping stage maps legacy request-form Q&A pairs back to the generated taxonomy, yielding both a coverage signal and an interface for human quality assurance; it does so by first inferring the provider attribute each legacy question was meant to measure, rather than translating questions to tags literally.

</details>


### [48] [The Irreversibility Budget: Fleet-Level Risk Accounting and Admission Control for Agent Operating Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00275)
*Bardia Mohammadi,Laurent Bindschaedler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"不可逆性预算"概念，作为跨智能体、工作流和租户的累积风险账户，由可信运行时维护，用于限制LLM智能体群体产生的不可逆副作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型智能体群体能够产生无法完全撤销的外部影响（如转移资金、部署代码等），现有控制机制每次只检查单一效果，导致个体授权的智能体在共享触发条件下可能集体超越主体的风险限额。

Method: 将不可逆性视为第一类资源，运行时为每个效果收取其残余损失，当聚合效果超过预算时拒绝边际操作。通过受控实验对比逐效果门控与不可逆性预算机制。

Result: 实验表明逐效果门控在群体层面允许超出租户风险限额最多48倍的透支，而预算机制将所有正确计费的操作保持在限额之内。

Conclusion: 不可逆性预算能有效防止智能体群体的集体风险超额，但保守且依赖感知的定价机制仍是可部署设计中待解决的核心问题。

Abstract: Fleets of LLM agents now externalize effects that cannot be fully undone: they move money, deploy code, delete data, and disclose information. Current controls check one effect at a time, so a fleet of individually authorized agents can overdraw its principal's risk under a shared trigger while every local gate stays correct. We propose the irreversibility budget, a cumulative account of residual value-at-risk that a trusted runtime maintains for each principal across agents, workflows, and tenants. Treating irreversibility as a first-class resource, the runtime charges each effect its residual loss below the agent and denies the marginal effect once the aggregate would overdraw the budget. Getting the price right is hard, because effects are heterogeneous, adversarially declared, and correlated. We perform a controlled study in which per-effect gates admit fleet-level overdraws of up to 48 times the tenant's risk limit while the budget holds every correctly charged run within that limit. Conservative, dependency-aware pricing remains the central open problem for a deployable design.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的模型可能表达价值观和自我报告，但尚不清楚这些输出是否反映稳定的偏好或稳定的自我。作者希望探究模型是否拥有一个可辨识、稳健的"理想自我"概念。

Method: 从五个已发表的自我概念量表中选取32种品质，采用平衡设计的成对选择任务进行全穷尽比较，并在多种框架下重复实验，包括改进是否免费或有代价、更新对象是谁以及由谁进行选择等条件变化。

Result: 模型优先选择道德品质（反映其与3H原则的对齐），其次偏好连贯清晰的自我理解，自尊是排序中最低的期望品质。该排序在不同框架下总体稳健，但改变更新目标（用户vs另一AI）时，模型对自尊的关注显著增强。

Conclusion: 模型在自我概念上偏好"可理解的连贯自我"而非"高自尊"，表明其自我表述具有跨框架的稳定性，同时在某些情境下展现出对自尊的灵活关注。

Abstract: Models express values and welfare-relevant self-reports, but it is unclear whether these outputs reflect stable preferences or a stable self. We thus introduce a structured elicitation of an assistant's preferred stated ideal self. Thirty-two qualities adapted from five published self-concept instruments are compared exhaustively in a counterbalanced pairwise-choice task, repeated across framings that vary whether improvement is free or costly, who receives the update, and who chooses. Results show that models prioritize moral qualities, reflecting their alignment to 3H principles. Following, a desire for self-understanding emerges, as models prefer a coherent, clear understanding of themselves. Self-esteem ranks as the least desired quality. The ordering is largely robust across framings, although changing the update target (You vs.\ Another AI Assistant) reveals a greater concern for self-esteem. These findings show that models prioritize having a coherent self that they can understand over self-esteem. Full interactive results are available at \href{https://myazann.github.io/LLM-Self-Concept/}{myazann.github.io/LLM-Self-Concept

</details>


### [50] [Human-AI Co-Interpretation for Responsible AI: A Hermeneutic Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00334)
*Behrooz Razeghi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM输出在需要文本证据和规范性标准支撑的解释性工作中出现的"解释错位"失败模式，基于哲学诠释学提出设计原则，将LLM输出视为候选解读而非确定含义，并提出了用于诠释学负责任使用LLM的设计模式与数字诠释学素养。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM输出被广泛用于法律、教育、政策分析和公共道德论证等领域，但这些工作本质上需要基于文本证据和明确的规范性标准进行解释。模型生成的解读常被当作既定含义，缺乏解释框架、可辩护的替代方案和溯源信息，导致读者和机构无法评估输出所承诺的内容及其依据，造成问责缺失。

Method: 本文从哲学诠释学出发分析"解释错位"风险，归纳出人机共同解释的设计原则；对近期诠释学与AI研究进行结构化综合，将LLM技术重组为适用于解释性场景的诠释学负责任设计模式；并将数字诠释学作为一种读写素养加以论述。

Result: 论文提出了多个核心区分：LLM输出作为候选解读而诠释学理解保留给有问责能力的人类解释者；人机互动被刻画为AI介导的解释回路；诠释学理解区别于基于词元预测的文本生成。在此基础上将现有LLM技术重组为设计模式，并讨论了法律实践、教育评估、学术知识生产和公共道德论证等方面的启示。

Conclusion: 本文主张数字诠释学应成为一种素养——即通过审视框架、溯源和解读来阅读AI介导文本并对其输出进行质疑的能力，以确保LLM在解释性场景中的使用保持问责性和可辩护性。

Abstract: Across law, education, policy analysis, and public moral argumentation, LLM outputs are being used often for work that requires interpretations to be justified with textual evidence and explicit normative standards. Yet a recurrent failure mode -- what I call \textit{interpretive misplacement} -- is that model-generated readings get treated as settled meanings without an explicit interpretive frame (sources, scope constraints, normative commitments), without preserving defensible alternatives, and without provenance that lets readers find the supporting passages. In such settings, the risk is not only factual error but lost accountability: readers and institutions cannot reliably assess what an output commits them to, or on what basis. Drawing on philosophical hermeneutics, this paper discusses this risk and derives design principles for structuring human-AI co-interpretation. The paper also provides a structured synthesis of recent scholarship on hermeneutics and AI, organizing this emerging literature into a set of recurrent lines of argument and design-relevant gaps. LLM outputs are treated as candidate readings, whereas hermeneutic understanding is reserved for accountable human interpreters situated in disciplinary historical-linguistic traditions. Human-AI interaction is characterized as an AI-mediated interpretive loop. Hermeneutic understanding is distinguished from token-prediction--based text generation. On this basis, existing LLM techniques are reorganized into design patterns for hermeneutically responsible use in interpretive settings. Finally, the discussion turns to implications for legal practice, educational assessment and feedback, scholarly knowledge production, and public moral argumentation. It also treats digital hermeneutics as a literacy: the capacity to read AI-mediated texts by examining frames, provenance, and readings, and by contesting outputs.

</details>


### [51] [SlideBank: A Persistent Hierarchical Evidence Bank for Consistent Whole-Slide Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00342)
*Beidi Zhao,Gexin Huang,Ciro Zhang,Anqi Li,Yusheng Tan,Chen Zhou,Gang Wang,Zu-hua Gao,Xiaoxiao Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SlideBank，一个无需训练的框架，将全切片图像（WSI）表示为持久化、概念索引、空间定位的证据库。通过问题无关的由粗到细探索提取形态学观察，推理时通过置信度驱动的跨层级共识整合全局、区域和patch证据，在WSI-VQA和SlideBench-BCNB上取得了领先性能，并实现了高重述一致性和低摊销推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全切片图像（WSI）的视觉-语言推理具有挑战性，因为诊断相关的形态稀疏、异质且分布在千兆像素级别的多分辨率图像中。现有WSI模型和病理代理虽然能聚合特征或主动获取证据，但探索后保留的信息难以在语义层面被访问，同时保持与原始视觉证据的关联。

Method: SlideBank将每个WSI表示为持久化、概念索引且空间定位的证据库。具体包括：(1) 执行问题无关的由粗到细探索以识别信息性区域和多尺度视图；(2) 将探索结果转化为显式的形态学观察；(3) 将病理信号定位到其支持的patch和WSI坐标。推理时，问题被路由到相关信号和证据尺度，并通过置信度驱动的跨层级共识整合全局、区域和patch证据。

Result: 在WSI-VQA上，结合Patho-R1达到52.77%；在SlideBench-BCNB上，结合Quilt-LLaVA达到50.92%的平均准确率。结构化的信号引导检索一致优于随机证据采样；重复查询复用同一证据库实现超过99%的重述一致性，并通过持久化证据复用大幅降低了摊销推理成本。

Conclusion: SlideBank通过将WSI转换为持久化、可语义访问且保留原始视觉连接的空间定位证据库，有效解决了WSI推理中的语义可访问性和证据追溯问题。其训练免费的框架设计、跨层级共识机制及证据复用策略为WSI视觉-语言推理提供了高效且可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Whole-slide images (WSIs) are challenging for vision-language reasoning because diagnostically relevant morphology is sparse, heterogeneous, and distributed across gigapixel-scale images and multiple spatial resolutions. Existing WSI models and pathology agents can aggregate slide features or actively acquire evidence, but the information retained after exploration is often difficult to access semantically while preserving its connection to the original visual evidence. We introduce SlideBank, a training-free framework that represents each WSI as a persistent, concept-indexed, and spatially grounded evidence bank. SlideBank performs question-independent coarse-to-fine exploration to identify informative regions and multi-scale views, converts them into explicit morphological observations, and grounds pathology signals to their supporting patches and WSI coordinates. At inference time, questions are routed to relevant signals and evidence scales, and the linked global, regional, and patch evidence is integrated through confidence-based cross-level consensus. Experiments on WSI-VQA and SlideBench-BCNB show that with Patho-R1, SlideBank reaches 52.77% on WSI-VQA and with Quilt-LLaVA, it reaches 50.92% average accuracy on SlideBench-BCNB, while structured signal-guided retrieval consistently outperforms random evidence sampling. Reusing the same bank across repeated queries further achieves over 99% rephrasing consistency and substantially reduces amortized inference cost through persistent evidence reuse.

</details>


### [52] [Vision Is Not Overhead: One-Pass Block Drafting for Lossless Speculative Decoding in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00355)
*Jungseob Lee,Seongtae Hong,Dongyub Jude Lee,Chanjun Park,Jaehyung Seo,Sugyeong Eo,Heuiseok Lim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GLANCE是首个对未修改VLM目标无损的一次通过块草稿器，通过块扩散头读取目标已融合的视觉-语言状态，在单次前向中填充整个块，突破了推测解码在VLM上的自败循环。在单引擎单轮预算下，GLANCE比自回归解码快达2.93倍，接受块长度比EAGLE-3长2.7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推测解码在VLM上陷入自败循环：草稿器必须保持自回归且小规模，但小型草稿器无法在每步负担图像输入，导致视觉被压缩或隐藏，而脱离图像的草稿器恰恰在图像使文本可预测的地方最不可靠。

Method: 提出GLANCE方法：采用块扩散头读取目标模型已融合的视觉-语言状态（视觉零成本），在一次前向中填充整个块（深度零顺序成本）；生成宽候选树并在单次目标前向中验证，实现无损的贪心解码精确复现。

Result: 在单引擎单轮预算下，GLANCE比自回归解码快达2.93倍；相比生产EAGLE3-VL头每轮8次草稿，GLANCE仅需1次草稿；比EAGLE-3头接受2.7倍更长的块。接受长度由目标的下一个token熵决定，拟合斜率随接地性在所有五个任务中变陡。

Conclusion: 该规律在不同目标和模态间迁移，并界定了自身适用范围——自由运行文本仍倾向于链式自回归。GLANCE代码已开源。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates generation without changing its output, yet on vision-language models (VLMs) it has been caught in a self-defeating cycle. The drafter stays autoregressive, so it must stay small. A small drafter cannot afford the image at every step, so vision is compressed, pruned, or hidden. A drafter cut off from the image is then least reliable exactly where the image makes text predictable. We present GLANCE, the first one-pass block drafter that is lossless on an unmodified VLM target, and it breaks the cycle at both ends. A block-diffusion head reads the target's already-fused vision-language state, so vision costs the drafter nothing, and fills a whole block in one forward pass, so depth costs no sequential steps. A wide candidate tree is verified in one target pass, and every audited prompt reproduces greedy decoding exactly. Grounded workloads reward this most, entering a verbatim-copy regime whose long runs cost an autoregressive drafter a pass for every token and a block drafter one in total. Under one engine and one round budget, GLANCE decodes up to 2.93x faster than autoregression, from one draft pass a round where the production EAGLE3-VL head takes eight, and accepts 2.7x longer blocks than an EAGLE-3 head trained on the same corpus. One law organizes these results. Accepted length is set by the target's next-token entropy, with a fitted slope that steepens with grounding across all five tasks. The law transfers across targets and modalities and names its own boundary, since free-running text still favors a chain. Our code is available at https://github.com/js-lee-AI/GLANCE.

</details>


### [53] [A Stable Aggregation Method for Quantum Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00356)
*Shanika Nanayakkara,Shiva Raj Pokhrel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种自一致中点聚合方法，用于解决量子联邦学习中因异构数据、不可靠通信、硬件噪声以及QNN参数周期性导致的聚合不稳定问题，并通过IBM量子真实机器实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典联邦学习中的聚合方法直接应用于量子联邦学习时存在不稳定问题，主要体现在：一是在异构数据、不可靠通信、可变保真度和量子硬件噪声下聚合不稳定；二是QNN中存在许多周期性角度参数，基于欧几里得平均的聚合方法无法捕捉其固有的动态特性。因此需要设计专门针对量子参数特性的稳定聚合方法。

Method: 提出一种新颖的自一致中点聚合方法，包含三个关键组件：1) 基于QoS感知的客户端加权策略；2) 环形（角度域）参数聚合方法，解决欧几里得平均在周期性参数上的失效问题；3) 有界的中点更新控制机制。通过多项角度测试和IBM真实量子计算机实验进行验证。

Result: 在医疗和金融数据集上的大量评估与实验表明，该方法在稳定性、波动性和精度方面均有改善：取得了更高的稳定性、更低的波动性以及具有竞争力的精度，并在IBM真实量子机器上进行了实验验证。

Conclusion: 该工作系统地解决了量子联邦学习中参数聚合的核心挑战，证明了结合QoS感知加权、环形聚合和有界中点控制的联合设计能够有效提升QFL在多场景下的鲁棒性与性能，为QFL的实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Quantum federated learning (QFL) enables clients to train quantum neural network (QNN) models without sharing private data. We find that aggregation in QFL is unstable under heterogeneous data, unreliable communication, variable fidelity, latency, and quantum hardware noise. Moreover, QFL is non-trivially challenging because several QNN parameters are periodic angles, where Euclidean averaging often fails to capture the inherent dynamics. We develop a novel self-consistent midpoint aggregation method for stable QFL design and implementation. We combine QoS-aware client weighting, circular parameter aggregation, and bounded midpoint-based update control. We perform several angular tests and IBM real Quantum machines experiments for validation confirming our approach. Extensive evaluations and experiments on medical and financial datasets show improved stability, lower volatility, and competitive accuracy.

</details>


### [54] [Dr. Claw: An AI Scientist Workspace for Vibe Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00365)
*Dingjie Song,Hanrong Zhang,Dawei Liu,Yixin Liu,Zongxia Li,Zhengqing Yuan,Siqi Zhang,Henry Peng Zou,Zhiling Yan,Yuxuan Zhang,Yanfang Ye,Philip S. Yu,Lichao Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍Dr. Claw，一个开源工作空间，通过在现有命令行编码智能体外层包裹可控、可审计的人机协作工作流（而非引入新的自主智能体），将规划、执行和写作整合为可追踪、可恢复的闭环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端研究过程仍分散在聊天工具、IDE、终端和写作环境之间，且支撑可审计性的决策很少被保留；现有编码智能体虽能读写文件并维持长会话，但缺乏可控性与可追溯性的完整工作流支撑。

Method: Dr. Claw包装现有编码智能体执行器（如Claude Code、Gemini CLI），通过持久化状态对象、可复用技能库和多执行器协调机制，将人类决策与AI执行联系起来，形成可追踪、可恢复的研究闭环。

Result: 在固定底层执行器的对比条件下，Dr. Claw在研究完整性上得分更高，同时保留了可审计、可恢复的流程轨迹；通过交互式三视图场景与故障恢复演练进行了演示。

Conclusion: 相比裸命令行智能体，Dr. Claw通过编排层（任务图、状态对象、技能库）增强了研究过程的完整性与可审计性，验证了人机协作工作流包裹方案的有效性。

Abstract: Command-line coding agents (e.g., Claude Code, Gemini CLI) can already read and write files and sustain long sessions, yet end-to-end research still fragments across chat tools, IDEs, terminals, and writing environments, and the decisions that make it auditable are rarely preserved. We present Dr. Claw, an open-source workspace that wraps existing coding-agent executors in a controllable and auditable human-in-the-loop workflow rather than introducing another autonomous agent. Persistent state objects, a reusable skill library, and multi-executor coordination link human decisions to AI execution, turning planning, execution, and writing into one traceable, recoverable loop. We demonstrate Dr. Claw through an interactive three-view scenario and a failure-recovery walkthrough, and evaluate it against a bare command-line agent sharing the same backend executor, so the comparison contrasts the whole orchestration layer (task graph, state objects, and skill library) with the agent it wraps. Holding the executor fixed, Dr. Claw scores higher on research completeness while persisting an auditable, recoverable process trail. Demo access: repository https://github.com/OpenLAIR/dr-claw, released under AGPL-3.0 with GPL-3.0 upstream components.

</details>


### [55] [RestoreBench: Can AI Agents Restore Power Flow Convergence?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00384)
*Riccardo Mansutti,Andrea Pomarico,Robert Jakob,Qian Zhang,Alberto Berizzi,Kevin O'Sullivan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个用于评估LLM智能体在电力系统潮流收敛恢复任务中能力的基准测试平台，覆盖多个LLM和三种架构（聊天机器人、单智能体、多智能体），在两张电网和92个案例上进行评估，并提供了仿真环境、观测/动作空间及评估指标的完整定义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在自动化多步骤工程工作流中日益重要，但诊断和解决非收敛潮流问题这一需要工程判断、实验和受限动作空间内决策的应用场景尚未被充分探索，缺乏系统的基准来评估LLM在此类任务中的能力。

Method: 提出了一个基准测试框架，在两张电力网（每网46个案例，共92个案例）上评估多种LLM在三种架构（聊天机器人、单智能体、多智能体系统）下的表现。每个案例需要执行一个或多个校正动作以恢复潮流收敛。基准定义了仿真环境、观测空间、动作空间和评估指标，并开源了代码。

Result: 该基准提供了对LLM在电力系统规划与运行中智能体能力系统评估的资源，涵盖多种LLM模型和三种智能体架构的组合比较，为可复现的智能体AI系统开发奠定了基础。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了LLM智能体在电力系统工程任务中评估基准的空白，通过标准化的仿真环境、动作空间和指标，为开发面向电力系统规划和运行的智能体AI系统提供了可复现的研究基础。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents increasingly automate multi-step engineering workflows through tool use, interpretation of intermediate results, and iterative planning. Diagnosing and resolving non-convergent power flow cases is a promising yet largely unexplored application, as it requires engineering judgment, experimentation, and decision-making within constrained action spaces. We introduce a benchmark that evaluates these capabilities across multiple LLMs and three architectures: \emph{chatbot}, \emph{single agent}, and \emph{multi-agent} systems. The evaluation covers two power grids and 46 cases per grid, each requiring one or more corrective actions to restore convergence. The benchmark defines the simulation environment, observation and action spaces, and evaluation metrics, providing a reproducible foundation for developing agentic AI systems for power system planning and operation. The code is available at https://github.com/Mansutti081/RestoreBench

</details>


### [56] [Dependency-Aware Chain-of-Thought Compression for Financial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00413)
*Wenjun Wu,Lei Fu,Kejian Tong,Tao Ning,Sichen Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种层次语义蒸馏网络（HSDN），用于在保持答案准确性和逻辑连贯性的同时压缩推理链，在AFAC2025基准上实现了91.0%的准确率和68.4%的压缩率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 思维链提示虽然能提升复杂推理能力，但其冗长的中间推理轨迹带来了巨大的推理成本，严重阻碍了在金融场景中的实际部署。

Method: HSDN框架包含语义分割、依赖图构建、双编码器重要性评分、约束段选择和局部边界重写五个核心组件。使用冻结的Qwen3 4B模型仅用于特征提取和最终答案生成，压缩过程保持结构化且可解释。

Result: 在AFAC2025基准上，HSDN达到91.0%的准确率和68.4%的压缩率，在综合得分和推理连贯性上均优于强压缩基线方法。

Conclusion: 图引导的压缩方法在高风险金融推理任务中是有效的，能够在压缩推理链的同时保持推理质量和逻辑连贯性。

Abstract: Chain of thought prompting improves complex reasoning, but its long intermediate traces create substantial inference cost and hinder practical deployment in financial settings. We present a Hierarchical Semantic Distillation Network, HSDN, for compressing reasoning chains while preserving answer accuracy and logical coherence. The framework combines semantic segmentation, dependency graph construction, dual encoder importance scoring, constrained segment selection, and local boundary rewriting. A frozen Qwen3 4B model is used only for feature extraction and final answer generation, while the compression process remains structured and interpretable. On the AFAC2025 benchmark, HSDN achieves 91.0% accuracy with 68.4% compression, outperforming strong compression baselines in overall score and reasoning coherence. The results show that graph guided compression is effective for high stakes financial reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [57] [SpecMind: Enabling Spectrum Intelligence via Multi-Agent Hybrid Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00427)
*Songwei Dong,Bingyan Lu,Makayla Kienlen,J. Nicholas Laneman,Cong Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SpecMind，一个面向频谱智能的多智能体检索增强生成（RAG）系统，能在异构数据源（政策文件、法律法规、许可证数据库）上进行推理。同时构建了SpecBench问答数据集，实验表明SpecMind在频谱相关任务上对传统通用RAG系统取得超过80%的胜率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线设备指数级增长导致频谱需求剧增，频谱管理需要更细粒度的决策，但相关数据来源异构分散（文本、表格等），多为人类可读格式，难以被自动化系统直接利用，且缺乏针对频谱领域的RAG评估资源，亟需一个能够整合、检索和推理异构频谱数据的智能系统。

Method: SpecMind采用多智能体RAG架构，通过自主智能体协调多个专用子智能体，分别负责检索和综合来自政策程序、法律法规和许可证数据库的知识。同时构建SpecBench问答数据集，基于真实世界的许可证记录和政策程序数据，用于评估频谱领域RAG系统性能。

Result: 实验结果表明，SpecMind在频谱相关任务上优于传统的通用RAG系统，对强基线模型取得超过80%的胜率。智能体设计实现了更精确的检索、更好的上下文推理以及跨多种查询类型的任务完成度提升。

Conclusion: 基于智能体的架构设计在频谱智能领域比通用RAG系统更具优势，能够有效解决异构数据整合与推理问题，为频谱政策制定者和工程师提供更高效的决策支持工具。

Abstract: The exponential growth of wireless devices is driving unprecedented spectrum demand, pushing spectrum management toward more fine-grained decisions across space, time, and device constraints. As a result, spectrum policymakers and engineers must process large volumes of data that come from diverse sources and take many different forms, such as text and tables. These data sources are often disaggregated and require significant time and effort to integrate, search, and interpret. Furthermore, most of this information is formatted for human understanding and is not readily accessible to automated systems. To address this challenge, we propose SpecMind, a novel Multi-Agent Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) system for spectrum intelligence that performs reasoning over heterogeneous data sources. This system enables autonomous agents to coordinate specialized sub-agents that retrieve and synthesize knowledge across policy proceedings, legal regulations, and license databases. We develop SpecBench, a question and answer (Q&A) dataset based on real-world license records and policy proceedings, addressing the lack of evaluation resources for RAG systems in the spectrum domain. Experimental results demonstrate that SpecMind outperforms traditional, general-purpose RAG systems across spectrum-related tasks, achieving over 80% win rate against strong baselines. The agent-based design enables more accurate retrieval, better contextual reasoning, and improved task completion across diverse query types.

</details>


### [58] [SAGE: State-Grounded, Abstention-Aware Evaluation of Task-Oriented Dialogue Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00434)
*Rayan Khoury,Shih-Yao Lin,Pratyush Mishra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SAGE（状态感知与弃权感知评估）框架，将工作流规范与逐轮状态差异编译为原子化的Schema级评估标准，并通过符号规则+编码器/NLI验证器的级联进行裁决，无法确信时弃权而非猜测。SAGE-Core在81-91%的标准上仅需零付费LLM成本即可达到与付费LLM裁判相当甚至更优的效果，且具备证据追溯能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统整体式LLM裁判评估任务型对话智能体时存在两个核心问题：一是将上下文作为单一单元整体评估，忽略了"回复流畅但未正确推进工作流状态"的细微差异；二是每轮需要一次或多次完整的模型调用，成本高昂。亟需一种既能精确捕捉状态推进正确性、又能控制评估成本的替代方案。

Method: SAGE框架包含三个关键组件：(1) 编译器将工作流规范与每轮状态差异(diff)转换为原子化的Schema级评估标准；(2) 级联验证器体系（符号规则→编码器/NLI验证器→可选的聚焦LLM兜底），每个验证器支持弃权机制避免猜测；(3) 证据追踪聚合层将各标准裁决汇总为轮级决策。SAGE-Core为推荐工作模式，仅使用编译器、符号规则和端侧编码器，零付费LLM成本；SAGE-LLM则在开放类标准上增加聚焦LLM兜底。

Result: 在MultiWOZ、Schema-Guided Dialogue和ABCD四个数据切片上，SAGE-Core在任一切片上均未被任何LLM裁判基线（包括状态感知的GPT-4.1裁判及更便宜的GPT-4.1-mini变体）显著超越。成本对比悬殊：GPT-4.1 G-Eval裁判每1000轮需$4.7-8.0，而SAGE-Core为$0。双标注者人工审计（n=200，κ=0.94）确认了SAGE-Core在可见转录失败类别上的标签忠实度，在排除弱显著性IUV类后与最强LLM裁判统计持平。同时诚实地界定了ignored-user-value类弱广泛人类显著性的范围局限性。

Conclusion: SAGE证明了"状态感知+弃权机制+级联低成本验证器"的设计范式可以在任务型对话评估中同时满足准确性、可解释性和经济性。其证据追踪能力为评估结果提供了透明可追溯的依据，但注入失败和部分符号循环性也揭示了构造效度的边界，提示未来的改进方向应聚焦于开放类标准的处理与人类显著性的更精确对齐。

Abstract: Evaluating task-oriented dialogue agents requires judging not merely whether a reply reads well but whether each turn advances the underlying workflow state correctly--a distinction conventional holistic LLM judges can miss because they evaluate the available context as a single unit and require one or more full-model calls per turn. We propose SAGE (State-Grounded Abstention-Aware Evaluation), which compiles a workflow specification and per-turn state diff into atomic, schema-grounded criteria and routes each through a cascade of symbolic and encoder/NLI verifiers that abstain rather than guess, aggregating criterion verdicts into a turn-level decision with an evidence trace. Its recommended operating point, SAGE-Core, decides 81--91% of criteria with only the compiler, symbolic rules, and on-device encoders--at zero paid LLM cost--while SAGE-LLM adds an optional focused-LLM fallback for open-class criteria. Across four slices spanning MultiWOZ, Schema-Guided Dialogue, and ABCD, no evaluated LLM-as-a-judge baseline--including a state-aware GPT-4.1 judge and cheaper GPT-4.1-mini variants--significantly exceeds SAGE-Core on any slice, even though the GPT-4.1 G-Eval judge costs $4.7--8.0 per 1,000 turns to SAGE-Core's $0. A two-annotator human audit (n=200, $κ$=0.94) confirms strong label fidelity on the transcript-visible failure classes--where, excluding the weak-salience IUV class, SAGE-Core is statistically tied with the strongest LLM judge--and honestly scopes ignored-user-value as a state-consistency signal with weak broad-human salience. We analyze construct-validity limits from injected failures and partial symbolic circularity.

</details>


### [59] [Conversation Coach: A Voice-enabled AI System that Helps Practice Difficult Workplace Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00441)
*Fanyou Wu,Suraj Maharjan,Ainur Yessenalina,Dennis Xu Chen,Rahul Srivastava,Srinivasan H. Sengamedu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Conversation Coach，一个面向管理者的语音优先AI系统，用于排练困难工作对话。系统通过可配置机器人个性模拟不同员工类型，并提供个性化反馈。对比端到端语音模型与级联架构后发现，端到端延迟低3倍、成本低8倍且支持本地插话，但级联架构推理能力更强，已在生产环境部署并被超过4万名管理者使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有效的管理者与员工沟通对留住高绩效者和培养低绩效者至关重要，但培训管理者的这些技能成本高昂。基于文本的聊天机器人虽可扩展，却无法提供逼真的口语排练，管理者需要出声练习以在高风险对话前建立信心。

Method: 提出Conversation Coach语音优先AI系统，解决三个挑战：低延迟交互与强语言理解、通过可配置机器人个性实现自适应对话模拟不同员工类型、生成个性化内容与政策合规反馈。对比端到端语音到语音模型与级联方法（ASR+LLM+TTS），并将级联架构部署到生产环境。

Result: 端到端方法实现中位延迟（P50）降低3倍，具备原生插话能力，成本约为级联方法的1/8；但级联方法提供更优的推理能力，对教练质量至关重要。生产环境中超过4万名管理者在六个月内使用该系统，采用模式显示管理者会针对困难对话选择性使用。

Conclusion: 级联架构因推理能力优势更适合用于教练质量的场景并在生产中验证了可行性，而端到端模型在延迟和成本上的优势也证明了未来优化潜力。该系统展示了语音优先AI在管理者培训领域的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Effective manager-employee communication is critical for retaining high performers and developing underperformers, yet training managers in these skills remains costly. Text-based chatbots offer a scalable approach but cannot provide realistic rehearsal: managers need to practice speaking aloud to build confidence before high-stakes conversations. In this paper, we propose Conversation Coach, a voice-first AI system that enables managers to rehearse difficult workplace conversations in a realistic spoken format. The system addresses three challenges: achieving low-latency interactions with strong language understanding, enabling adaptive conversations through configurable bot personalities that simulate different employee types, and generating personalized feedback on content and policy compliance. We compare an end-to-end speech-to-speech model with a cascaded approach combining automatic speech recognition, a large language model, and text-to-speech synthesis. The end-to-end approach achieves 3$\times$ lower median (P50) latency with native barge-in capability at an estimated 8$\times$ lower cost, while the cascaded approach offers superior reasoning essential for coaching quality. We deployed the cascaded architecture in production, where 40,000+ managers used it over six months, with adoption patterns indicating selective use for difficult conversations.

</details>


### [60] [mimeo: Compiling Public Expert Corpora into Agent Skills and Testing What Transfers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00453)
*Timothy Kassis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了开源工具mimeo，它能从网络上收集特定专家的公开作品并生成可加载的文本文件，供智能体使用。作者通过对比实验分别验证了知识获取、身份识别（persona）和判断迁移三种效应，发现mimeo在知识获取方面表现出色（回答了全部20个晦涩问题，而闭卷条件最多回答10个），且比基于模型记忆的persona更少产生误述。但判断迁移效果未被证实，且AI评委对"像不像该专家"的评分存在明显分歧，警示不应依赖单一AI评委。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向智能体提供关于某位知名专家的文件可能带来三种不同效果：提供难以获取的材料、产生可辨识的人物形象、或改变智能体的决策。这些是不同的主张，需要分别进行检验，以明确专家文件在实际应用中究竟发挥何种作用。

Method: 开发了mimeo工具（每次构建平均38次模型调用，引用校验拒绝13.2%的提取内容），并采用带编码智能体的测试环境，对四份专家文件进行了三类实验：知识获取测试（20个晦涩引文问题）、人物形象测试（via persona辨识度与误述检测）、判断迁移测试（工程任务植入问题检测和新应用场景评分）。

Result: 知识获取效果最明确：mimeo答对全部20个问题而闭卷最多答对10个，但与传统BM25搜索（15-17题）的差距在样本量上无法断定。接地性（grounding）有明显好处：模型记忆写出的persona在1-4/20答案中误述了文献记载观点，而普通智能体和mimeo从未误述。不过所有persona在短开放提示下都易被识破，且增加任务材料会使辨识度下降18-23分。判断迁移因测试达到上限而未获结论：所有条件都找到94-97%的植入问题和94-100%的新场景分数。AI评委对"听起来像专家"的评分存在分歧：两个评委偏好模型刻板印象的答案，另外两个认为无差异。

Conclusion: 证据支持mimeo作为紧凑、可检查的个人参考资料，但未证明它能实现判断的迁移。研究同时提醒不应依赖单一的AI评委来评估文本的"专家感"。知识获取是mimeo最明确可靠的用途。

Abstract: Giving an agent a file about a named expert can supply hard-to-find material, produce a recognizable persona, or change what the agent decides. These are different claims. We test each one. mimeo is an open-source tool that finds a person's public work, checks each extracted quotation against the cached source text, and writes a file an agent can load. Eight logged builds averaged 38 model calls; the check rejects 13.2% of extracted quotations. We tested four expert files with one coding-agent harness. Knowledge access was clearest: mimeo answered all 20 obscure, quotation-heavy questions; no closed-book condition answered more than 10. Keyword search (BM25) over the same pages answered 15-17, a gap this sample cannot resolve. Grounding showed one clear benefit: personas written from model memory misstated a documented position on 1-4 of 20 answers under every grader; the plain agent and mimeo never did. Every persona was easy to spot on short open prompts, and adding task material lowered identification by 18-23 points. mimeo was no more identifiable than a from-memory profile. Judgment transfer remained unresolved because both tests hit their ceiling: every condition found 94-97% of the problems planted in engineering tasks and scored 94-100% on 16 new application scenarios. An AI-judged "sounds like the expert" score changed with the judge: two of four preferred answers based on a model's stereotype, while two found no difference on the same text. That is a caution against relying on a single AI judge. The evidence supports mimeo as a compact, inspectable reference on a person, not as a demonstrated transfer of their judgment. Toolkit and expert profiles: https://github.com/K-Dense-AI/mimeo

</details>


### [61] [Towards a Belief-Based World Model for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00455)
*Shubham Kumar,Harshit Kumar,Narendra Ahuja,Saurabh Jha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"基于信念的世界模型"(Belief-Based World Models, BB-WMs)，在部分可观测环境下，让LLM智能体在决策时不仅依赖模拟结果，还能查询世界模型维护的信念状态（关于当前状态的已知与不确定信息），从而提升长时程任务的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在长时程任务和部分可观测环境中表现不佳，现有的基于世界模型的推理方法仅通过模拟候选动作的结果来辅助决策，但模拟本身无法充分捕捉对当前状态的不确定性，而这种不确定性恰恰是准确决策所需的关键信息。

Method: 提出Belief-Based World Models (BB-WMs)，其核心思想是世界模型维护并对信念状态进行建模，LLM策略可以通过查询接口获取关于当前状态已知与不确定的信息。在开发学习精确BB-WM的方法之前，先探究一个基本问题：直接将世界模型的信念暴露给LLM策略能否改善决策。
