A federated mode shares GPU inference servers across concurrent evaluations, and a built-in verifier, small enough to run on CPU, keeps its score stable across engines and prompts where rule-based scoring fluctuates under configuration mismatch, matching cost-efficient commercial LLM judges without their recurring API cost. The system, demo video, and dashboard are publicly available. (https://github.com/naver-ai/omni-evaluator)

</details>


### [131] [Automated Event Log Generation from Unstructured Text Using Finetuned LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01320)
*Maximilian Seeth,Gabriel Marques Tavares,Daniel Schuster*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出利用大语言模型（LLMs）作为数据转换器，将非结构化文本资源自动转换为结构化事件日志，从而解决流程挖掘中对人工构造事件日志的依赖瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流程挖掘技术依赖于结构化事件日志，但事件日志通常需要领域专家和流程挖掘专家手工构建，成本高昂。这导致大量非结构化组织知识（如工单、手册、文本报告）未能得到充分利用。

Method: 提出一个可扩展框架，利用大语言模型作为数据转换器，在新建的文本转日志（text-to-log）数据集上对LLMs进行微调，使其能够从非结构化文本中提取高保真的事件日志。

Result: 微调后的模型在从非结构化资源中提取事件日志方面表现出色，且性能大幅优于少样本（few-shot）或零样本（zero-shot）提示方法。

Conclusion: 该方法为流程挖掘生态系统提供了将此前未使用数据转化为可用事件日志的可行管道，展示了微调是生成可靠事件数据的前提条件，有效扩展了流程挖掘在组织工作流研究中的应用可能性。

Abstract: Process mining (PM) provides a powerful framework for discovering and optimizing operational processes from event data. However, the efficacy of PM techniques is strictly predicated on the availability of structured event logs. Thus far, event logs have often been laboriously created by domain and process mining experts. This costly effort causes large portions of organizational knowledge, including incident tickets, manuals, and textual reports, to remain underutilized. We address this bottleneck by investigating the efficacy of Large Language Models (LLMs) as automated data translators. We propose a scalable framework that leverages LLMs as data translators to bridge the gap between unstructured textual resources and structured event data. We finetune LLMs on a newly created text-to-log dataset, demonstrating that the resulting models can extract high-fidelity event logs from unstructured resources. Our results show that this finetuning approach outperforms few-shot or zero-shot prompting by a large amount, highlighting finetuning as a necessary pre-condition for generating reliable event data. We conclude that our method provides a promising pipeline for making previously unused data available to process mining ecosystems, effectively expanding the possibilities of using PM to further investigate organizational workflows.

</details>


### [132] [LEAP: Likelihood Elicitation and Aggregation for LLM-based Probabilistic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01337)
*Yufei Chen,Yiran Zhao,Xiaogang Xu,Qipeng Xie,Jiafei Wu,Zhe Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LEAP方法，将LLM预测从"整体式预测"重构为"逐证据似然估计+确定性概率模型聚合"的两阶段流程，在多项预测与校准指标上优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM预测系统让模型一次性阅读所有证据后直接生成最终预测（称为"整体式预测"），这种方法会掩盖单个证据对结果的影响，并模糊竞争结果间的不确定性关系。

Method: LEAP将证据分别单独分析，为每条证据提取似然参数描述其目标含义；再通过显式先验和确定性概率模型将各似然组合为后验分布，支持连续、单选和多选预测且保持证据贡献可复现。

Result: 在自建基准（涵盖预测、信息搜寻和浏览任务）上，在自有agent循环及多个agent CLI框架中，在相同证据下LEAP在绝大多数预测与校准指标上优于基线模型，且在控制先验访问、推理预算和聚合方式的对比中依然更强。

Conclusion: LEAP通过将预测阶段从整体式阅读改为逐证据似然学习加概率模型聚合，提升了预测质量和校准性能，为LLM概率预测系统提供了更透明、更可靠的证据利用范式。

Abstract: LLM-based forecasting systems have improved on real-world tasks such as financial markets and sports outcomes, largely through stronger search and tool use. Many systems still ask an LLM to read all collected evidence together and produce the final forecast. We call this design Monolithic Prediction. It can obscure how individual evidence items affect the result and collapse uncertainty across competing outcomes. We propose LEAP (Likelihood Elicitation and Aggregation for Probabilistic forecasting), which reorganizes how collected evidence is used in the prediction stage. LEAP examines each evidence item separately and elicits likelihood parameters that describe its implications for the target. An explicit prior and a deterministic probabilistic model then combine these likelihoods into a posterior distribution. This procedure supports continuous, single-choice, and multi-choice forecasts while preserving reproducible evidence contributions. We build a benchmark covering forecasting, information-seeking, and browsing tasks, and evaluate LEAP on our own agent loop and several agent CLI frameworks. Given the same evidence, LEAP improves most prediction and calibration metrics across models and remains stronger under controlled comparisons of prior access, inference budget, and aggregation.

</details>


### [133] [Cheap Verifiers, Large Blind Spots: Measuring the Reliability Cost of Cost-Saving Cascades](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01345)
*Dushyant Rajput*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究"推理级联+自改进循环"（用便宜模型回答大多数问题，昂贵模型作为验证者处理困难尾部，并基于验证者的拒绝微调学生模型）在实际LLM上的表现，发现四个关键问题：验证者存在随学生能力增强而扩大的"盲区"，驯服盲区的代价极高，朴素纠正微调反而导致小模型崩溃，且级联自身的验证指标（显示3%错误）与实际错误（最高32%）严重脱节。作者提出解释该盲区的双总体守恒定律ε∞≲q0β0，结论是自改进级联的可靠性无法通过其自身验证者计算的任何指标来度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理级联通过廉价模型+高价验证者的方式降低成本，自然的扩展是"闭环"：用验证者的拒绝数据微调学生模型以降低升级率和成本。作者希望实证检验这种自改进循环是否真的能持续降低成本并提升质量。

Method: 在真实LLM上测量自改进级联循环，涉及不同规模的学生模型（0.5B至32B）和不同验证者，测量验证者的盲区（接受学生错误答案的比例）、升级率与真实错误率，并进行朴素纠正微调实验和合成研究；提出双总体守恒定律ε∞≲q0β0解释系统盲区。

Result: 四个发现：（1）验证者盲区β随学生能力增长（0.12→0.55）且随验证者能力缩小，在廉价学生+廉价验证者组合中最大；（2）前沿验证者虽将β降至0.05，但导致46%的查询被升级而真实错误率仅39%，成本极高；（3）朴素纠正微调不仅不改善学生反而使其退化甚至崩溃；（4）级联自身指标恒显示3%错误，而真实错误高达32%，系统对自身退化完全失明。

Conclusion: 自改进级联在实践上是自我挫败的，系统通过自身验证者计算的任何可靠性指标本质上不可信。验证者的"盲点"是系统性的且随学生变强而扩大，这一发现对依赖级联验证机制的AI系统设计具有重要的可靠性警示意义。

Abstract: Inference cascades cut cost by answering most queries with a cheap model and escalating a hard tail to a frontier model that acts as verifier. A natural extension closes the loop: fine-tune the cheap student on the verifier's rejections so the escalation rate, and cost, fall each round. We measure this loop on real LLMs and report four findings. First, the verifier's blind spot, the fraction of the student's wrong answers it accepts, is large and moves adversarially: it grows with student capability ($β$ from 0.12 to 0.55 as the student scales 0.5B to 32B) and shrinks with verifier capability, so it is worst in the cheap-student, cheap-verifier regime cascades exist to create. Second, buying it away returns the saving: a frontier verifier drives $β$ to about 0.05 but then escalates on 46% of hard-MATH queries against a 39% true error rate, paying the frontier price on nearly half of all traffic. Third, naive corrective fine-tuning on the verifier-rejected tail does not improve the small student but degrades and ultimately collapses it, across every teacher we tried (cross-family and same-family), so at this scale the self-improving loop is self-defeating. Fourth, through all of this the cascade's own dashboard, every metric computed through the verifier, reads a flat 3% error while true delivered error swings up to 32%: the system is blind to its own degradation by construction. We then give the theory that explains the blindness, a two-population conservation law, $ε_\infty \lesssim q_0 β_0$, under which every in-loop metric improves while true quality does not, and a synthetic study that validates the mechanism. The practical conclusion: the reliability of a self-improving cascade cannot be read from any metric computed through its own verifier.

</details>


### [134] [SymFold: Synergizing Evolutionary and Structural Priors for Accurate Protein Inverse Folding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01353)
*Handong Wang,Jiaxin Qi,Baisheng Lai,Jianqiang Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种对称双路径架构用于蛋白质逆折叠任务，同时利用蛋白质语言模型（PLMs）的序列进化知识和多模态蛋白质语言模型（MPLMs）的结构知识，迭代引导蛋白质序列生成，在标准基准上取得了最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蛋白质逆折叠方法多采用串行管线，由结构编码器预测粗略序列后由PLMs进行后期编辑，但PLMs的编辑能力受限于上游预测质量。虽然多模态蛋白质语言模型（MPLMs）可直接从结构生成序列，但实验发现其在逆折叠任务上效果不佳。因此需要一个能同时发挥PLMs和MPLMs各自优势的架构。

Method: 提出对称双路径架构，同时利用PLMs（预训练序列进化知识）和MPLMs（预训练结构知识），通过迭代方式相互指导蛋白质序列生成，两条路径对称设计，而非传统的串行管线。

Result: 在标准蛋白质逆折叠基准上取得最先进的性能，超越先前方法；消融实验验证了对称设计的合理性。

Conclusion: 对称双路径架构是一种有前景的蛋白质逆折叠方法，为社区揭示了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Protein inverse folding aims to recover amino acid sequences for a given 3D protein structure, underpinning broad applications such as enzyme engineering and drug discovery.Current methods often follow a serial pipeline, in which a structure encoder predicts a coarse sequence, which is then refined by protein language models (PLMs). However, because PLMs only perform post-hoc sequence edits, the refinement is bounded by the quality of upstream predictions.Thanks to recent multimodal protein language models (MPLMs), we could directly encode structure to generate sequences with pretrained structural knowledge, but we observe that they are not effective for inverse folding. Therefore, we introduce a symmetric dual-path architecture that both leverages PLMs for pretrained sequence evolution knowledge and MPLMs for pretrained structural knowledge to iteratively guide protein sequence generation.Through extensive experiments across standard protein inverse folding benchmarks, our method achieves state-of-the-art performance, surpassing prior approaches, and ablation studies validate the rationale of our symmetric design, revealing a promising direction for the community.

</details>


### [135] [EDGE: Error Dependency Graph-Guided Multi-Error Attribution in Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01360)
*Jun Hou,Priya Pitre,Yi Fang,Xuan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EDGE框架，通过构建错误依赖图并利用反事实回滚验证因果子集，实现大语言模型智能体故障的多错误归因，超越传统的单一根因分析方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型智能体的失败通常包含多个相互关联的错误，而非单一错误。现有归因方法只能识别单一责任主体、步骤或根因，无法显式建模错误之间的依赖关系，缺乏对多错误间关联结构的理解和利用。

Method: EDGE框架包含三个核心部分：首先从观测到的错误事件构建错误依赖图；然后通过反事实回滚（counterfactual rollout）验证其中可靠的因果子集；最后利用推理图指导两阶段的LLM-as-judge检测器进行错误归因，干预验证后的子图为解释和修复分析提供更可靠的依据。

Result: 在TRAIL和MAST数据集上的实验表明，EDGE在大多数评估模型和设置下提升了类别级多错误归因的性能。使用改编的Who&When风格提示的实验进一步表明，错误依赖图在不同提示策略下均能带来增益。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明依赖结构是智能体失败诊断的有效先验信息，超越了孤立的根因预测方法，为多错误归因提供了更全面的诊断视角。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agent failures often contain multiple related errors rather than a single mistake. Existing attribution methods usually identify a responsible agent, step, or root cause, but do not explicitly model dependency between errors. We introduce EDGE, an Error Dependency Graph-guided multi-Error attribution framework. EDGE constructs an error dependency graph from observed error events and validates a reliable causal subset through counterfactual rollout. The inference graph guides a two-stage LLM-as-judge detector for error attribution, and the intervention-validated subgraph provides a more reliable basis for explanation and repair analysis. Experiments on TRAIL and MAST show that EDGE improves category-level multi-error attribution across most evaluated models and settings. Experiments with adapted Who&When-style prompts show that the graph helps across prompting strategies. These results suggest that dependency structure is a useful diagnostic prior for agent failures beyond isolated root-cause prediction.

</details>


### [136] [Neuro-Symbolic Geometric Abstraction (NeuSOGA): From Observations to Symbolic Mathematical Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01408)
*Qingde Li,Qingqi Hong,Jie Tian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出NeuSOGA（神经符号几何抽象）框架，将几何观测逐步转化为符号数学表示，结合拓扑结构发现、基础模型感知、多尺度几何抽象和隐式面积样条符号合成，生成可解释、可编辑且支持任意阶平滑的解析隐式表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AI系统虽然在感知能力上表现卓越，但学到的知识编码在难以检查和操作的潜在参数中。受神经符号AI和人类抽象理论启发，本文旨在探索如何从几何观测中形成符号化数学表示，以解决深度学习模型缺乏可解释性和可操控性的根本挑战。

Method: NeuSOGA框架采用渐进式变换策略：首先利用欧氏距离变换进行拓扑引导的结构发现，然后使用Segment Anything基础模型进行感知，接着进行自适应多尺度几何抽象，最终通过隐式面积样条实现符号合成，生成支持任意阶平滑、加性组合和闭式计算的解析隐式模型。

Result: 在ModelNet40点云、任意视角投影和分割光学观测上的实验表明，NeuSOGA能够在不同感知模态和观察方向下，将多样化观测转化为紧凑的符号表示，同时保留关键几何与拓扑结构。

Conclusion: NeuSOGA提供了一条从观测到符号的可解释、可解释路径，建立了神经符号几何抽象的新范式。

Abstract: A fundamental challenge in artificial intelligence is the transformation of observations into explicit symbolic representations suitable for abstraction, interpretation, and reasoning. While modern AI systems achieve remarkable perceptual capabilities through large-scale statistical learning, the resulting knowledge is typically encoded within latent parameters that are difficult to inspect or manipulate analytically. Inspired by Neuro-Symbolic AI and theories of human abstraction, this paper investigates the formation of symbolic mathematical representations from geometric observations.
  We propose NeuSOGA (Neuro-Symbolic Geometric Abstraction), a framework that progressively transforms observations into topological abstractions, geometric abstractions, and ultimately symbolic mathematical representations. The architecture combines topology-guided structural discovery using Euclidean Distance Transforms, foundation-model perception using Segment Anything, adaptive multi-scale geometric abstraction, and symbolic synthesis through Implicit Area Splines.
  The resulting representation is an analytical implicit model supporting arbitrary-order smoothness, additive composition, and closed-form evaluation. Unlike neural latent encodings, the generated representation remains interpretable, editable, and mathematically explicit. Experiments on ModelNet40 point clouds, arbitrary-view projections, and segmented optical observations demonstrate that NeuSOGA transforms diverse observations into compact symbolic representations while preserving essential geometric and topological structure across sensing modalities and viewing directions.
  NeuSOGA provides an interpretable and explainable pathway from observation to symbol and establishes

</details>


### [137] [EdiTikZ: Scientific Figure Editing from Revision Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01409)
*Christian Greisinger,Zhixue Zhao,Steffen Eger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了DaEdiTikZ，首个大规模基于论文修订轨迹的科研图表编辑数据集（391K TikZ编辑对、781K指令），并训练了紧凑的EdiTikZ模型（4B/9B），通过联合学习重建与编辑及强化学习，在自动和人工评测中超越GPT-5.6-Sol并与Gemini-3.1-Pro持平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科研图表生成已取得进展，但生成出版级图表需要迭代编辑，而现有方法依赖昂贵的专有Agent系统、仅聚焦评测或依赖合成编辑数据进行监督。作者希望利用自然发生的科研修订轨迹作为可扩展的监督来源，解决图表编辑任务中监督数据稀缺且成本高的问题。

Method: 从arXiv、GitHub和TeX SE中挖掘391K个源自修订的TikZ编辑对，并利用VLM根据渲染图表和TikZ代码推断781K条有向编辑指令，构建DaEdiTikZ数据集；再构建人工精炼的DaEdiTikZ-Bench基准（790个实例）；训练两个基于Qwen3.5的紧凑EdiTikZ模型（4B和9B），联合学习重建与编辑任务，并采用强化学习（RL）结合渲染保真度和编辑应用两种互补奖励进行优化。

Result: 自动评测中9B模型超越所有测试基线；人工评测（9名标注者、4,320个评分）中超过GPT-5.6-Sol并与Gemini-3.1-Pro相当；在严重的分布外偏移下仍具有竞争力，接近其2K训练序列长度范围。

Conclusion: 通过利用自然科研修订轨迹作为监督信号，能够以紧凑模型规模达到甚至超越超大专有模型的图表编辑性能，验证了该数据构建范式的有效性，并将开源模型和数据集以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have shown strong performance in generating scientific figures from text or images. However, producing publication-ready figures requires iterative refinement, making scientific figure editing an important yet largely unexplored task. Existing approaches rely on costly proprietary agentic systems, focus primarily on evaluation, or construct training supervision from synthetically generated edits. Instead, we leverage naturally occurring scientific revision and development trajectories as a scalable source of supervision. To this end, we introduce DaEdiTikZ, the first large-scale dataset of revision-derived scientific figure edits, constructed by mining 391K plausible TikZ edit pairs from arXiv, GitHub, and TeX SE and inferring 781K directed edit instructions with a VLM conditioned on rendered figures and TikZ code. We further introduce DaEdiTikZ-Bench, a human-refined benchmark with 790 instances, and train two compact Qwen3.5-based EdiTikZ models (4B and 9B) by jointly learning reconstruction and editing, followed by reinforcement learning (RL) with complementary rewards for rendered fidelity and edit application. Automatic evaluation places our 9B model above all tested baselines, while human evaluation with 9 annotators and 4,320 ratings places it above GPT-5.6-Sol and on par with Gemini-3.1-Pro. Under severe out-of-distribution shifts, it remains competitive with GPT-5.6-Sol near its 2K training sequence-length regime. Models and datasets will be released.

</details>


### [138] [Parsing the Stream: A Live Trace Model for Long-Horizon Agents and Their Observers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01466)
*Egor Pakhomov,Erik Nijkamp*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种"实时轨迹折叠"（live trace model）机制，将长时程智能体的追加式事件日志增量折叠为类型化运行状态，并为人类观察者和智能体自身两大消费者编译差异化视图。实验表明，对观察者（以LLM为代理），折叠视图相比直接读取原始轨迹在监控问题上减少约14-15倍输入token、成本降低5-7倍且准确率更高（0.85-0.87 vs 0.48）；对智能体，在多步顺序依赖任务上（30/30 vs 8/30成功率），维护运行统计量的折叠机制优于全上下文提示。作者还从失败中提炼出11条候选需求，并用一个对顺序敏感的家族任务界定其适用范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程智能体的执行轨迹（trace）规模会迅速超出两类消费者的处理能力：一是监控运行的人类观察者，二是需要将轨迹折回自身有界上下文中的智能体本身。现有方案要么直接读取原始轨迹导致token和成本爆炸，要么缺乏结构化的增量折叠方法，亟需一种可审计、紧凑且面向双消费者的轨迹管理范式。

Method: 提出"实时轨迹模型"：采用追加式事件账本，将事件增量折叠为类型化运行状态（fold），并分别为观察者和智能体编译定制视图。观察者侧用LLM作为读者代理评估折叠视图的问答效率；智能体侧在120链路顺序依赖任务上对比维持运行统计量的折叠机制、全上下文提示和提示级scratchpad，并采用双臂分解归因折叠的准确率与成本优势来源。

Result: 观察者侧：折叠视图比单次预算封顶的原始轨迹读取少约14x/15x输入token，成本低5-7倍，且准确率显著更高（0.85-0.87 vs 0.48）。智能体侧：30/30任务成功 vs 全上下文的8/30；scratchpad在更低成本下匹配折叠准确率。双臂分解表明准确率来自确定性聚合，成本优势来自紧凑性。该方法在顺序敏感任务族上失效。发布了代码、基准、合成语料与工作台轨迹。

Conclusion: 轨迹折叠在双消费者场景下均可显著提升效率与准确率，其剩余价值在于确定性可审计性和以同一状态服务观察者；但其有效性受任务的结构性质（如顺序敏感性）制约，具有清晰的使用边界，论文提炼的11条候选需求为后续泛化提供了方向。

Abstract: A long-horizon agent's trace outgrows both of its consumers: the human observer monitoring the run, and the agent itself, whose bounded context the trace must be folded back into. We present a live trace model, an append-only event ledger folded incrementally into typed run state and compiled into per-consumer views, and evaluate it for both consumers against deterministic ground truth. For the observer side, evaluated with an LLM reader as proxy, the compiled view answers monitoring questions using approximately 14x and 15x fewer input tokens (by reader) and at 5-7x lower cost than a budget-capped single-call reading of the raw trace, with higher accuracy (0.85-0.87 versus 0.48). Because the questions were co-designed with the view schema, we treat the token and cost reduction, conditional on schema coverage, as the transferable result. For the agent, on 120-link sequential-dependency tasks, mechanisms that maintain the task's running statistic in per-step state succeed where full-context prompting fails (30/30 versus 8/30 under a clean protocol, n=30, labeled descriptive owing to benchmark-system co-development); a prompt-level scratchpad matches the fold's accuracy at lower cost, and a two-arm decomposition attributes the fold's accuracy to its deterministic aggregate and its cost advantage to its compactness. The fold's remaining value over cheaper alternatives is deterministic auditability and serving the observer from the same state. We derive eleven candidate requirements for trace folding from observed failures and delimit them with an order-sensitive task family on which the fold ceases to help. Code, benchmarks, a regenerable synthetic corpus, and all workbench traces are released.

</details>


### [139] [Harness-of-Harness: Multi-Day Autonomous Software Development with Continual Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01481)
*Haoyang Yan,Min-le Su,Hangfan Zhang,Zhanhao Li,Chen Zhang,Shao Zhang,Yang Chen,Lei Bai,Shuyue Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Harness-of-Harness (HoH)框架，使基于LLM的编码智能体能够在自主软件开发过程中持续改进软件。HoH通过将现有编码智能体的执行组织为迭代的规划-编码-测试循环，在GameCraft-Bench、FrontierSWE和ProgramBench三个基准上平均相对提升52.25%，最大提升82.86%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主软件开发中，LLM编码智能体需将高层需求转化为完整、功能可用且无需人工干预的软件系统，但现有智能体在一次执行中难以持续优化软件质量，缺乏有效的迭代改进机制。

Method: HoH operates on existing coding-agent harnesses，组织其执行为迭代式规划-编码-测试循环。该框架通过以下机制实现持续改进：平衡修复与能力增长、将软件开发拆分为小而可验证的增量、将实现期测试与独立评估分离、约束可验证输出而非规定智能体工作流、渐进式暴露交付物/角色特定工具/技能、鼓励复用而非全新创建、维护版本化项目历史。

Result: 在GameCraft-Bench、FrontierSWE和ProgramBench三个基准上，三对harness-model组合（Codex+GPT-5.5、OpenCode+DeepSeek-V4-Pro、Pi+MiniMax-M3）均优于独立harness，平均相对提升52.25%，三次迭代后最大提升82.86%。在超过70次迭代的多日部署中，HoH自主开发出一款第一人称射击游戏，具备连贯故事情节、完整核心机制、可玩体验、精美视觉和集成音频。

Conclusion: HoH通过通用的harness-of-harness架构有效增强现有编码智能体框架的自主软件开发能力，在多个基准和实际部署中验证了其持续改进效果与泛化性，为自主软件开发提供了新的范式。

Abstract: This paper studies autonomous software development, in which LLM-based coding agents transform high-level requirements into complete, functional, and usable software systems without human intervention. We introduce Harness-of-Harness (HoH), a framework that enables coding agents to continually improve software during autonomous development. HoH operates on existing coding-agent harnesses, and organizes their executions into iterative planning-coding-testing loops. To sustain improvement across loops, HoH balances repair with capability growth, scopes development into small and verifiable increments, separates implementation-time testing from independent evaluation, and constrains verifiable outputs rather than prescribing agent workflows. It progressively exposes deliverables, role-specific tools, and skills, encourages reuse rather than recreation, and maintains versioned project histories. On GameCraft-Bench, FrontierSWE, and ProgramBench, three harness-model pairs (Codex with GPT-5.5, OpenCode with DeepSeek-V4-Pro, and Pi with MiniMax-M3), HoH consistently outperforms the corresponding standalone harnesses, achieving an average relative gain of 52.25 percent and a maximum gain of 82.86 percent after three iterations. In a multi-day deployment with more than 70 iterations, HoH autonomously develops a first-person-shooter game, featuring a coherent storyline, fully implemented core mechanics, human-playable experience, polished visuals and integrated audio. Github: https://github.com/Flesymeb/HarnessOfHarness Project Page: https://flesymeb.github.io/HarnessOfHarness/

</details>


### [140] [When Guardrails Look Effective: Construct Validity Failures in LLM Agent Commerce Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01519)
*Peiying Zhu,Sidi Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文审计了由语言模型代理驱动的交互式市场模拟中的构造有效性（construct validity）风险。作者在多轮买卖双方酒店交易测试平台上发现，最初报告的护栏（guardrails）福利增益（+87.4、+35.0、+28.8）在控制方案与选择程序后大幅缩水（+7.2、-13.9、+23.8），且多代生成结果不具统计显著性。研究提出一套"构造有效性契约"，区分激励有效性、协议隔离、随机稳定性与福利核算，最终判定原估计在协议隔离下INVALID，受控研究在激励有效性与随机稳定性下仍INCONCLUSIVE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型代理的市场模拟所输出的价格、利润和福利看似经济合理，但可能并未真正实例化其所声称的行为。这种"表面经济"会误导政策评估，因此需要审计和验证模拟结果的构造有效性。

Method: 在可配置的酒店交易多轮买卖双方测试平台中，通过系统性控制（固定offer schema和buyer chooser）、多次生成（3 generations per profile-condition）、脚本化正向对照（scripted positive controls）以及四重检验（激励有效性、协议隔离、随机稳定性、福利核算）来审计原始福利估计的有效性。

Result: 原报告福利增益+87.4、+35.0、+28.8在固定协议后变为+7.2、-13.9、+23.8；单次生成的14B效应平均+229，三次生成后降至+37.6（95%置信区间[-34.2,109.3]），生成残差占变异的49.9%；卖家激励检查呈非单调性，利润最大化卖家已可达最优福利，护栏主要产生再分配与福利降低效应，仅在卖家被明确编程强制低效时才能创造福利。

Conclusion: 该案例表明，语言模型代理模拟中的护栏效果并非无效，而是其表观价值在代理与协议通过构造有效性检查前根本无法识别。原始的估计在协议隔离下无效，受控研究在激励有效性与随机稳定性下仍不确定。论文呼吁政策声明前必须完成制度化的验证流程。

Abstract: Interactive simulations increasingly evaluate policies in markets populated by language-model agents. Their outputs can look economic---prices, profits, consumer surplus, and welfare---without instantiating the behavior named in the claim. We audit this risk in a multi-turn buyer--seller testbed for configurable hotel transactions. An initial implementation reported welfare gains from two marketplace guardrails of +87.4, +35.0, and +28.8 across a Qwen2.5 1.5B--14B ladder. It also gave guarded and unguarded agents different offer schemas and choice procedures. Holding the schema and buyer chooser fixed changes the paired contrasts to +7.2, -13.9, and +23.8. The four largest 14B single-generation effects averaged +229; after three generations per profile-condition, they averaged +37.6 (95% bootstrap interval [-34.2, 109.3]), while generation residuals account for 49.9% of variation in this post-hoc probe. A seller-incentive check is non-monotone: increasing profit pressure produces less profit than the default seller prompt. Scripted positive controls show why this matters. A profit-maximizing seller already attains first-best welfare, so guardrails mostly redistribute and reduce welfare; they create welfare only when the seller is explicitly programmed to force inefficient bundles. We contribute a construct-validity contract separating incentive validity, protocol isolation, stochastic stability, and welfare accounting, and returning INVALID or INCONCLUSIVE before substantive policy claims. In our case, the original estimate is INVALID under protocol isolation, while the controlled study remains INCONCLUSIVE under incentive validity and stochastic stability. The case does not show that guardrails are ineffective; it shows their apparent value is unidentified until the simulated agents and protocol pass these checks.

</details>


### [141] [EvoSCM: Scientific Belief Revision Through Causal Model Evolution and Experimentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01526)
*Qing Zhao,Haowei Li,Weijian Deng,Pengxu Wei,Liang Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EvoSCM，为科学智能体配备可演化的显式结构因果模型（SCM），通过闭环发现循环（溯因、干预、预测、演绎验证）使智能体在实验过程中不断修正其因果假设，并在DiscoverPhysics基准上验证了其优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM科学智能体通常以自由文本形式表达科学假设，导致其信念隐含且难以测试或修正。科学智能体不仅要学会推理，还要学会在证据积累中形成可检验、可修正的明确信念。

Method: EvoSCM维护一个竞争性的SCM假设种群，每个假设编码环境的一种候选因果解释。每轮循环中：1）从累积证据中溯因隐式机制；2）设计判别性干预实验；3）做出可证伪预测并测试；4）将预测与观测的差异归纳提炼为修正规则，修订因果结构和机制；5）演绎性地验证修订后的种群，指导下一轮发现。

Result: 在DiscoverPhysics基准（要求智能体通过实验发现非规范物理世界的隐藏动态）上，EvoSCM持续优于基线方法，产生更准确的解释和预测，同时更有效地利用实验交互。

Conclusion: 将科学假设表示为显式且可演化的结构因果模型，结合归纳修正与演绎验证的闭环机制，能显著提升科学智能体的发现能力和实验效率，为可检验、可修正的科学AI打开新方向。

Abstract: Scientific agents must learn not only how to reason, but also what to believe. However, existing LLM agents typically express scientific hypotheses in free-form text, leaving their beliefs implicit and difficult to test or revise. We introduce EvoSCM, which equips scientific agents with explicit structural causal models that evolve as new experimental evidence is collected. EvoSCM maintains a population of competing SCM hypotheses, each encoding a candidate causal explanation of the environment, and evolves them through a closed discovery loop. In each round, the agent abduces latent mechanisms from accumulated evidence, designs discriminative interventions, and commits to falsifiable predictions that it tests through experimentation. Discrepancies between prediction and observation are inductively distilled into correction rules that revise the causal structures and mechanisms of each hypothesis, and the agent then deductively validates the revised population against accumulated evidence and structural consistency to guide the next round. We evaluate EvoSCM on DiscoverPhysics, a benchmark requiring agents to uncover the hidden dynamics of noncanonical physical worlds through experimentation. EvoSCM consistently improves scientific discovery over baselines, yielding more accurate explanations and predictions while making more effective use of experimental interactions.

</details>


### [142] [Can LLMs Discover Scientific Laws in Real and Parallel Worlds?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01552)
*Yiming Huang,Ziche Liu,Zhuohang Wu,Yiqian Wang,Junxia Cui,Xinkai Zou,Linjun Mao,Nan Huang,Naicheng Yu,Kaijie Zhu,Yue Ma,Kun Zhou,Letian Peng,Jingbo Shang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SCILAWS-BENCH基准，用于评测大语言模型在真实科学数据中发现科学定律的能力，涵盖来自381篇论文的118个问题，包含固定记录发现（REAL）和主动查询恢复（PARALLEL）两个互补设置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学定律发现的评测要么使用合成数据简化问题，要么复用LLM可能已经见过的公开目标，难以真实评估LLM能否像科学家一样发现新的科学定律。

Method: 构建基于已发表研究和真实科学数据的基准，包含118个问题、291个候选定律和约800万真实数据点，覆盖六个学科；每个问题在SCILAWS-REAL（从固定真实观测中提出定律，评估留出预测拟合和文献来源的科学有效性）和SCILAWS-PARALLEL（主动查询残差校准世界并恢复合成的隐藏定律）两个设置下实例化。

Result: 发现预测拟合与科学有效性可能不一致；记忆效应影响模型是复现还是超越已发表公式；best-of-N研究揭示了选择瓶颈现象。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个基于论文的基准和新经验视角，用于评估AI在科学发现中的应用，揭示了当前LLM在该任务上的关键局限。

Abstract: Scientific equation discovery has long been central to scientific progress, proceeding through iterative cycles of hypothesis generation, observational testing, and refinement under scientific constraints. As LLM capabilities advance and their role in AI for Science expands, it remains an open problem whether they can genuinely discover scientific laws and how this ability should be evaluated. Existing evaluations, however, often either simplify discovery through synthetic settings or reuse published targets that may already be familiar to LLMs. We therefore introduce SCILAWS-BENCH, a benchmark for scientific law discovery built from published research and real scientific data. It comprises 118 problems drawn from 381 scientific papers, covering 291 candidate laws and roughly 8M real data points across six scientific disciplines. Each problem is instantiated in two complementary settings: (1) SCILAWS-REAL asks models to propose laws from fixed real observations and evaluates held-out predictive fit and scientific validity derived from the source literature, and (2) SCILAWS-PARALLEL asks models to actively query residual-calibrated worlds and recover synthesized hidden laws derived from published forms. This two-setting task design preserves each problem's scientific context while separately evaluating fixed-record law discovery and active recovery of a newly synthesized hidden law. We find that predictive fit can diverge from scientific validity, memorization shapes whether models reproduce or move beyond published formulas, and our best-of-N study reveals a selection bottleneck. Our work provides a paper-grounded benchmark and new empirical perspectives for evaluating AI for scientific discovery. Project page: https://yiyihum.github.io/SciLaws-Bench

</details>


### [143] [Selective Agent Guidance via Entropy: Learning Autonomous Policies from Imperfect VLM Teachers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01567)
*Matteo Merler,Giovanni Bonetta,Davide Zago,Rossella Cancelliere,Bernardo Magnini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SAGE框架，通过熵不确定性机制在训练中选择性查询VLM教师模型，并将其指导蒸馏到轻量级RL策略中，减少VLM推理成本的同时提升自主策略的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM作为直接策略使用成本高且脆弱：每步都需查询、无法从环境交互中改进、且会重复系统性错误。因此需要一种方法，既能利用VLM提供的有用先验，又能学习出廉价的自主策略。

Method: SAGE框架的核心包括：(1) 只在学习者不确定时（基于熵）查询VLM教师；(2) 训练中执行教师建议的动作；(3) 将教师的指导蒸馏到轻量级RL策略中；(4) 使用环境回报的优势加权而非平等对待所有教师建议，以应对VLM建议不完全可靠的问题。

Result: 在稀疏奖励的视觉推理和导航任务中，SAGE学习的策略在评测时无需VLM指导即可行动，并在多个环境中优于无指导的RL，甚至在某些场景下超过了VLM教师本身。选择性指导在VLM能帮助智能体发现高回报轨迹时最有效，在无指导探索已成功或教师动作不带来有效经验时收益有限。SAGE还大幅减少了VLM的使用频率。

Conclusion: VLM不需要作为固定策略使用才有价值，它们可以充当临时的、不完美的指导来源，其价值通过环境交互被检验和内化。这为VLM与RL结合提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) provide useful priors for interactive decision-making, but using them directly as policies is expensive and brittle: they must be queried at every step, do not improve from environment interaction, and can repeat systematic errors. We study how to learn a cheap autonomous policy from an online, expensive, and imperfect but informative VLM teacher. We propose SAGE (Selective Agent Guidance via Entropy), a framework that queries a VLM only when the learner is uncertain, executes the suggested action during training, and distills guidance into a lightweight Reinforcement Learning (RL) policy. Because VLM advice is not always reliable, SAGE can weight teacher-action distillation using environment-derived advantages rather than treating all suggestions as equally useful. Across sparse-reward visual reasoning and navigation tasks, SAGE learns policies that act without VLM guidance at evaluation time and improves over unguided RL in several environments, including settings where the learned policy exceeds its VLM teacher. The results show that selective guidance is most beneficial when the VLM can help the agent discover high-reward trajectories, and less useful when unguided exploration already succeeds or teacher actions do not lead to informative experience. SAGE also reduces VLM usage by prompting the teacher only on a fraction of training steps and requiring no VLM calls at deployment. Overall, our results suggest that VLMs don't need to be used as fixed policies to be useful; they can instead act as temporary, imperfect sources of guidance whose value is tested and internalized through interaction.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [144] [Task-Specific Prompt with Global Context for Multi-Task Graph Pre-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00047)
*Zhiyang Qiu,Yangtao Wang,Xiaocui Li,Yanzhao Xie,Siyuan Chen,Wensheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TPGC提出双先验提示初始化方法，通过任务先验注入模块和结构先验注入模块显式建模先验知识，解决多任务图预训练中随机初始化提示导致的提示空间与预训练目标及图结构对齐不佳问题，在6个基准的少样本场景下取得更优性能且参数和耗时更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于多任务图预训练框架的图提示学习方法通常使用随机初始化的提示，导致提示空间与预训练任务目标及图结构特征之间缺乏良好对齐，严重削弱了提示表示的任务相关性、结构感知能力和可迁移性。

Method: 提出TPGC框架，包含两个模块：任务先验注入模块在辅助图上进行短期的同源多任务预训练，使提示初始化继承多个预训练任务的优化偏好；结构先验注入模块基于任务感知表示进一步提取辅助图中可迁移的全局结构上下文，通过聚合结构信息丰富的节点嵌入生成逐层提示向量。

Result: 在涵盖节点和图分类任务的6个主流基准上的实验表明，TPGC在少样本设置下持续优于最先进基线，同时下游可调参数更少、运行时间更低。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模任务先验和结构先验的协同作用，双先验提示初始化有效提升了提示空间与预训练目标和图结构之间的对齐度，增强了提示表示的任务相关性和结构感知能力，显著提升了图提示学习在下游少样本任务上的表现。

Abstract: Graph prompt learning is an effective paradigm to adapt pre-trained graph models to downstream tasks in low-resource scenarios. However, existing multi-task graph pre-training frameworks generally use randomly initialized prompts, leading to poor alignment between the prompt space, pretext objectives and graph structural characteristics. This greatly weakens the task relevance, structural awareness and transferability of prompt representations. To address this challenge, we propose TPGC, a dual-prior prompt initialization solution that explicitly models the synergy between task prior and structural prior. Specifically, the Task-Prior Injection Module first conducts a short homologous multi-task pre-training on an auxiliary graph, enabling prompt initialization to inherit optimization preferences associated with multiple pretext tasks. Built on the task-aware representations, the Structure-Prior Injection Module further extracts transferable global structural context from the auxiliary graph, converting it into layer-wise prompt vectors by aggregating structurally informative node embeddings. Extensive experiments on 6 mainstream benchmarks covering node and graph classification show that TPGC achieves consistently better performance under few-shot settings than state-of-the-art baselines, with fewer downstream tunable parameters and lower runtime. The code is available at https://github.com/Virgilqiu/TPGC

</details>


### [145] [REAL-Q: E2E LLM Quantization via Dynamic Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00049)
*Qian Zhang,Yaoming Li,Zhewen Tan,Yanshu Wang,Heng Lu,Kun Su,Zongwei Lv,Wenhan Yu,Yongge Ma,Yinjun Han,Ruikuang Liu,Tong Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: REAL-Q是一种新的后训练量化范式，通过端到端对齐的损失代理函数和细粒度动态块级梯度下降，替代传统的单一闭式二阶求解器方法，有效解决了信息错位问题，在LLaMA-3.1和Qwen3系列模型上显著降低了端到端KL散度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进的PTQ方法为了保持解析可处理性，对全局损失做了大量近似（丢弃跨通道耦合、池化输出行），且冻结Hessian无法随损失景观逐列变化而更新，导致信息错位问题，影响了量化精度。

Method: REAL-Q提出端到端对齐的全局损失代理函数，并在每个列块（128列）之后应用细粒度的动态块级梯度下降进行精炼；同时引入滑动窗口机制实现平滑的跨层过渡，以缓解误差在网络中的传播。

Result: 在LLaMA-3.1（8B和70B）及Qwen3（0.6B-32B）的W4A16配置下，REAL-Q相比最先进的全局引导方法，端到端KL散度最多降低约49%。

Conclusion: 通过打破"为解析可解性而稀释目标"的传统框架，REAL-Q验证了动态细粒度更新策略在LLM量化中的有效性，为解决PTQ中的信息错位问题提供了一条新的技术路线。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) is essential for deploying large language models (LLMs) under strict resource constraints. State-of-the-art PTQ methods quantize each layer with a single closed-form second-order solver: to remain analytically tractable, they heavily approximate the global loss (dropping cross-channel coupling, pooling output rows into groups), and they then freeze the resulting Hessian across the entire layer, with no way to refresh it as the loss landscape shifts column by column--a phenomenon we call information misalignment. We propose REAL-Q (Real-time E2E-loss Aligned LLM Quantization), a novel PTQ paradigm that breaks this compromise: instead of diluting the objective for the sake of analytic tractability, REAL-Q targets an end-to-end-aligned surrogate of the global loss and refines it via fine-grained, dynamic Block-wise Gradient Descent applied after every column block (128 columns). By coupling this fine-grained correction with a sliding window mechanism for smooth cross-layer transitions, REAL-Q effectively mitigates error propagation across the network. On LLaMA-3.1 (8B and 70B) and Qwen3 (0.6B-32B) at W4A16, REAL-Q reduces end-to-end KL divergence by up to ~49% relative to state-of-the-art globally-guided methods.

</details>


### [146] [Convergence issues in Relational Concept Analysis based on AOC-posets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00054)
*Xavier Dolques,Agnès Braud,Alain Gutierrez,Marianne Huchard,Florence Le Ber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于AOC-poset的Relational Concept Analysis (RCA)在迭代过程中收敛性丢失的问题，分析了收敛性无法保证的原因，提出了确保收敛的条件、数据集变换方法以及一种保留AOC-poset结构的收敛变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RCA传统上在概念格上定义并保证迭代收敛，但当应用于更小、更易计算的AOC-poset结构时，这种收敛性保证会丢失。理解这一收敛性丢失的机制并提出解决方案，对在实际应用中使用RCA于AOC-poset具有重要意义。

Method: 作者详细分析了AOC-poset设置下RCA迭代收敛性丢失的原因，识别了可以保证收敛的特定条件，探讨了通过数据集变换恢复收敛的方法，并提出一种不收关系属性（一旦创建永不删除）的收敛变体方案。

Result: 研究揭示了AOC-poset下RCA收敛性不保证的一般性原因，找到了可确保收敛的条件，提出了通过变换数据集恢复收敛的策略，并设计了一种保证收敛的变体——通过保留已创建的关系属性来确保收敛，代价是可能产生指向最终结构中不存在概念的属性。

Conclusion: 虽然RCA在AOC-poset上会丢失收敛性保证，但通过在特定条件下操作或采用不删除关系属性的收敛变体策略，可以在保持AOC-poset结构优势的同时确保迭代过程的收敛性。

Abstract: Formal Concept Analysis (FCA) is an approach for conceptual classification building and rule discovery from a binary table describing a set of objects by a set of attributes. Extensions have been proposed to deal with non-binary and more complex data, such as Relational Concept Analysis (RCA) for multi-relational data. RCA aims to highlight groups of objects characterized by their relationships with other groups of objects. The richer and more complex nature of the underlying data allows RCA to produce richer results than FCA, at the expense of higher computational and interpretive complexity. The most commonly used conceptual classification structure in FCA is the concept lattice. However, in many applications, concept lattice substructures, such as AOC-posets, are preferred over the full lattice, either to mitigate combinatorial blow-up or to focus on the most informative parts of the structure. Indeed, in an AOC-poset, only concepts introducing an object or an attribute are represented, which makes AOC-posets smaller and easier to compute and use than concept lattices. Although RCA was originally defined on concept lattices, it can also be instantiated on AOC-posets. RCA is iterative and its convergence is guaranteed in the lattice-based setting, but this guarantee is lost when using AOC-posets. In this paper, we investigate this loss of convergence in detail. We show why convergence is no longer guaranteed in the general case, identify conditions under which it can still be ensured, and discuss how a dataset can be transformed to recover convergence. We also propose a convergent variant of the process, which preserves the AOC-poset structure: relational attributes, once created, are never removed, which guarantees convergence at the price of attributes that may refer to concepts absent from the final structures.

</details>


### [147] [DISTAL: Distillation and Self-Supervised Pretraining for Structure-Agnostic Materials Property Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00059)
*Weiran Wang,Xintong Huo,Yueying Wang,Yusi Fan,Wenyan Wang,Xin Feng,Ruihao Xin,Lan Huang,Kewei Li,Fengfeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了DISTAL框架，一种用于材料性能预测的双先验框架，结合自监督组分预训练与结构感知知识蒸馏，在推理时无需晶体结构输入即可实现高精度预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料性能预测在低数据场景下仍具挑战性，且现有高精度模型依赖晶体结构信息，限制了其在早期筛选阶段的应用（此时结构信息有限或不可用）。

Method: DISTAL包含两部分：1）利用145种组分衍生描述符在大规模虚拟组分空间上进行自监督组分预训练；2）将预训练的ALIGNN教师模型的结构知识蒸馏到组分条件学生模型中。最终将显式组分描述符、预训练潜在特征和蒸馏结构特征统一整合到预测流程中。

Result: 在39个基准任务中，结合三种信号的最佳多模态配置在37个任务上优于参考基准，整体表现优于所有其他特征组合。

Conclusion: 组分预训练与结构蒸馏提供了互补的先验知识，为小数据材料信息学中稳健的纯组分预测提供了一条实用路径。

Abstract: Materials property prediction remains difficult in low-data settings, where many target properties are supported by only a limited number of labeled samples. Models with the strongest predictive accuracy often depend on crystal structures, which restricts their use in early-stage screening when structural information is limited or unavailable. To address this challenge, we propose DISTAL, a dual-prior framework for structure-agnostic materials property prediction that combines self-supervised compositional pretraining with structure-aware knowledge distillation. DISTAL first learns transferable compositional representations from a large virtual composition space using 145 composition-derived descriptors. It then distills structural knowledge from a pretrained ALIGNN teacher into a composition-conditioned student. This setting allows structural priors to be used during training without requiring structural inputs at inference. By integrating explicit compositional descriptors, pretrained latent features, and distilled structural features within a unified prediction pipeline, DISTAL captures complementary signals that are difficult to recover from any single representation alone. Across 39 benchmark tasks, the best-performing multimodal configuration combines all three signals, and improves over the reference benchmark on 37 tasks. DISTAL achieves the strongest overall performance among all evaluated feature combinations. These results indicate that compositional pretraining and structural distillation provide complementary priors and offer a practical route to robust composition-only prediction in small-data materials informatics. The source code and the pre-trained models are anonymously available at: https://osf.io/eq96d/overview?view_only=451617f42f7849e08750bd1852b48980 and will be released at the official link after acceptance.

</details>


### [148] [ReNFT: Repairing Mode Collapse in Reward Post-Training via Internal Probability-Mass Recalibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00061)
*Yuchen Bao,Chao Wen,Haowei Wang,Ruoxin Chen,Donghao Luo,Jiahui Zhan,Wenjian Huang,Shen Chen,Yiting Wang,Taiping Yao,Chengjie Wang,Shouhong Ding,Jianguo Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ReNFT，一种通过内部概率质量重新校准来修复已发生模式坍缩的扩散生成器适配器的方法，在不依赖外部信号的情况下，在保留奖励的同时恢复提示内多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奖励后训练会导致扩散生成器将概率质量集中到少数奖励偏好的模式上，产生模式坍缩，破坏提示内多样性。现有缓解方法依赖外部信号或接口，且无法修复已经坍缩的适配器，同时保留已获得的奖励。

Method: ReNFT首先使用无条件探针优先选择"反枢纽"提示以暴露提示无关偏差；然后通过两条策略主导的混合路径从相同提示和初始噪声生成匹配的反事实候选——一条探测冻结的基座方向以获取被抑制的替代内容，另一条暴露后训练的无条件倾向；最后使用带自适应翻转守卫的奖励排序分配拉/推角色，并通过联合配对NFT更新实现修复。

Result: 在PickScore和GenEval基准上，ReNFT保留了NFT奖励的98.9%和99.0%，同时将DreamSim-Div分别提高了58.8%和55.0%。

Conclusion: 坍缩是抑制而非删除，可以从生成器内部逆转。ReNFT提供了一种与外部干预互补的替代方案，在保持高奖励的同时有效恢复多样性。

Abstract: Reward post-training of diffusion generators inevitably concentrates probability mass on a few reward-favored modes, a mode collapse that erases within-prompt diversity. Existing methods for mitigating collapse rely on external signals or interfaces, augmenting the reward with perceptual objectives, adjusting reference regularization, or modifying the text encoder, but none repairs an adapter that has already collapsed while preserving the acquired reward. We observe that online post-training primarily reallocates probability mass over capabilities inherited from pretraining rather than learning new visual content. Collapse is therefore suppression, not deletion, and can be reversed from within the generator. We propose ReNFT, which repairs a high-reward, low-diversity adapter through internal probability-mass recalibration. Unconditional probes first prioritize "anti-hub" prompts where the prompt-independent bias is easiest to expose. Two policy-dominated mixed routes then generate matched counterfactual proposals from the same prompt and initial noise, one probing the frozen base direction for suppressed alternatives and the other exposing the post-trained unconditional tendency. Reward ranking with an adaptive flipping guard assigns pull and push roles, and a joint-and-paired NFT update realizes the repair. On PickScore and GenEval, ReNFT retains 98.9% and 99.0% of NFT's reward while improving DreamSim-Div by 58.8% and 55.0%, respectively, offering a complementary alternative to external interventions.

</details>


### [149] [Attention Sensitivity Is Not Enough: Dissociating Attention-Level and Behavioural In-Context Learning under Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00064)
*Jinyuan Zhang,Peng He,He Hu,Yin Yuan,ShengShuo Jiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了以注意力变化作为上下文学习（ICL）保留程度的代理指标在优化后是否仍然可信。作者形式化了In-Context Sensitivity (ICS)和ICL-GAP两个度量，在Llama-2-7B上进行受控消融实验，发现最大化ICS的正则化器可使ICS接近其几何上限（1.413），但行为层面的ICL-GAP几乎为零且MMLU准确率大幅下降，呈现典型的Goodhart解离现象，即注意力层面的代理指标被"钻空子"而牺牲了真实的ICL能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调会侵蚀大语言模型的上下文学习能力，现有的保留诊断常通过检查注意力是否随演示变化而改变来判定模型是否上下文敏感。然而，这些注意力层面的代理指标一旦被作为优化目标，可能不再反映真实的行为能力，其可信度值得质疑。

Method: 形式化定义了ICS（最后token注意力在匹配与不匹配演示前缀间的平均行距离）和ICL-GAP（相同前缀间的行为精度差距）两个指标。在Llama-2-7B上进行四臂消融实验，包括ICS最大化正则化器（armKL）、行为门控以及锚定预训练计算的训练目标，并配合端点评测、随机标签协议和构建性扫描进行机制分析与验证。

Result: ICS最大化正则化器将ICS推至1.413（几何上限的0.5%以内），但ICL-GAP保持接近零，MMLU准确率从0.371降至0.279。端点评测发现注意力变得尖锐且跨前缀近乎不相交，但路由到格式化和演示正文token而非标签token。随机标签协议确认行为探针家族在同一检查点仍保有动态范围。行为门控可部分缓解效应，锚定预训练计算的目标则能保持在高质量区域。

Conclusion: 注意力层面的ICL代理指标在被用作训练目标之前，必须经过行为差距的验证。单纯优化注意力层面的代理会产生Goodhart式解离，导致代理指标虚高而真实上下文学习能力受损，因此诊断框架应结合行为层面的验证。

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) lets large language models adapt to new tasks from demonstrations, and fine-tuning can erode this behaviour. Many preservation diagnostics inspect attention: if attention changes when demonstrations change, the model is treated as context-sensitive. This paper asks how far that proxy can be trusted once it is optimised. We formalise \emph{In-Context Sensitivity} (ICS), the average row distance between last-token attention on matched and mismatched demonstration prefixes, and pair it with \emph{ICL-GAP}, the behavioural accuracy gap between the same prefixes. In a controlled four-arm ablation on Llama-2-7B, an ICS-maximising regulariser ($\armKL$) drives ICS to $1.413$, within $0.5\%$ of its geometric ceiling. The behavioural readout tells a different story: ICL-GAP stays near zero and MMLU accuracy moves from $0.371$ to $0.279$, a Goodhart dissociation of the bounded attention proxy. Endpoint statistics locate the mechanism: attention grows sharp and near-disjoint across prefixes yet routes to formatting and demonstration-body tokens rather than labels. A random-label protocol confirms that the behavioural probe family retains dynamic range at the same checkpoints. In a constructive sweep, behaviour gating partially mitigates the effect, while objectives anchored to pretrained computation hold the high-MMLU, moderate-ICS region that divergence maximisers leave. The main lesson is diagnostic: attention-level ICL proxies earn their place as training targets only after validation against behavioural gaps.

</details>


### [150] [RW-LoRA: Communication-Efficient Decentralized LoRA Fine-Tuning via Random Walks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00078)
*Xingran Chen,Rohit Bhagat,Ghadir Ayache,Rawad Bitar,Yanmin Gong,Salim El Rouayheb*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于随机游走的LoRA微调方案，不同于传统的集中式聚合或基于gossip的分散式LoRA方法，该方法让单一模型令牌在网络中游走并利用本地微调目标顺序更新模型，从而消除了全局同步的需求，大幅降低通信与计算开销，并避免了聚合误差。作者在非凸目标下提供了严格的收敛性保证，并在多个NLP任务和图拓扑上证明了该方法在通信和计算效率上显著优于gossip-based LoRA，同时保持竞争力的任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分布式LoRA方法主要分为两类：集中式方法依赖中心化聚合，gossip-based分散式方法则需要多个模型副本之间的反复同步。这两种方法都存在显著的通信开销，并且由于同时聚合多个模型更新而引入误差。作者希望通过创新的视角解决这些分布式微调中的固有挑战。

Method: 提出一种基于随机游走的LoRA微调方案：不在网络中维护多个模型副本，而是让单个模型令牌在网络节点间游走，在每个节点上利用本地微调目标顺序更新模型。这种方法完全不需要全局同步机制，避免了多个更新的同时聚合，从而显著降低了通信和计算成本。

Result: 在标准假设下为非凸目标提供了严格的收敛性保证；在多个NLP任务和图拓扑上的实证结果表明，所提出方法在任务性能上与gossip-based LoRA相当，但通信和计算开销显著更少。

Conclusion: 随机游走式LoRA微调为分布式环境下的参数高效微调提供了一种新的、高效的范式，在保持竞争力的同时大幅减少了资源开销，为大规模基础模型的分散式适配提供了可行的替代方案。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning methods such as LoRA have become a standard approach for adapting large foundation models. Adopting fine-tuning to distributed settings faces several challenges. Most existing distributed LoRA methods rely on centralized aggregation, and gossip-based decentralized LoRA requires repeated synchronization among multiple model copies. Both methods incur significant communication overhead and introduce errors due to simultaneous aggregation of multiple model updates. In this paper, we take a different perspective and propose a random-walk-based LoRA fine-tuning scheme. Instead of maintaining multiple model replicas, a single model token traverses the network and is updated sequentially using local fine-tuning objectives. This design eliminates the need for global synchronization, substantially reduces communication and computation costs, and avoids aggregation errors. We provide rigorous convergence guarantees for non-convex objectives under standard assumptions. Through empirical results on multiple NLP tasks and graph topologies, we show that the proposed method achieves competitive task performance with substantially less communication and computation than gossip-based LoRA.

</details>


### [151] [Stochastic complexity of vectors containing cluster structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00084)
*Daniel Nicorici,Olli Yli-Harja,Jaakko Astola*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究基于归一化最大似然（NML）模型计算含聚类结构编码向量的随机概率（最短编码长度）问题，提出一种递归公式实现归一化常数的有效计算，将时间复杂度从多项式降至线性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于最小描述长度（MDL）原理的数据聚类中，估计最佳聚类数和聚类结构需要计算编码向量的最短编码长度，而基于NML模型的直接计算需要多项式时间，效率低下。

Method: 作者证明了该问题具有可解性（tractable），通过引入一种递归公式来计算NML模型中的归一化常数，从而实现对最短编码长度的有效计算。

Result: 新公式的时间复杂度为线性，相对于此前与向量大小和聚类数相关的多项式时间复杂度有了显著提升。

Conclusion: 该递归公式使基于MDL原则的聚类分析中关键计算从多项式时间降为线性时间，具有重要的理论与实践价值。

Abstract: This paper studies the problem of computing the stochastic probability (shortest code length) of the encoded vectors containing cluster structure using Normalized Maximum Likelihood (NML) model. This is of great theoretical and practical importance in data clustering based on Minimum Description Length (MDL) principle, such as for estimating the best number of clusters and best cluster structure for the data. Straightforward computation of the shortest code length of the vector containing cluster structure based on the NML model requires polynomial time with respect to the size of the vector and number of clusters. We show that this is a tractable problem by introducing a recursion formula for the efficient computation of normalizing constant from the NML model. The time complexity of the new formula is linear opposed to previous polynomial time with respect to the size of the vector and number of clusters.

</details>


### [152] [Foundation models for electricity price forecasting and battery arbitrage: Can they replace market-specific forecasting models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00089)
*Arkadiusz Lipiecki,Rafał Weron*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对比了五个基础模型家族的九种变体（零样本模式）与两种最先进的电价预测基准模型，在德国、波兰和西班牙市场2021-2025年间的表现。结果显示仅TabPFN模型在所有市场和统计指标上持续显著优于基准，但这种统计优势并未直接转化为经济优势——在高风险策略下TabPFN表现最佳，而在低风险容忍度下基准模型更有利可图，表明基础模型无法普遍替代市场专用模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型声称能在很少或无需任务特定训练的情况下提供准确预测，但它们在电价预测领域能否替代专门设计的模型仍不明确。作者旨在通过系统的跨国比较来回答这一关键问题。

Method: 选取五个基础模型家族的九种变体在零样本模式下进行评估，与两种最先进的电价预测基准模型对比。研究覆盖德国、波兰和西班牙三个市场2021-2025年数据，从点预测精度、概率预测精度以及电池储能套利的经济价值三个维度进行综合评价。

Result: 仅在基础模型家族中，TabPFN模型在所有三个市场和所有统计指标上持续且显著优于基准模型。但在经济评估中，TabPFN仅在无限竞价和风险较高的分位数策略下表现最佳，而Distributional Deep Neural Network基准模型在风险容忍度较低时更为有利可图。

Conclusion: 基础模型不能普遍替代市场专用模型，其价值既取决于模型架构也取决于具体的决策问题。统计预测优势并不必然转化为经济决策优势。

Abstract: Foundation models promise accurate forecasts with little or no task-specific training, but whether they can replace models designed specifically for electricity price forecasting remains unclear. We compare nine variants from five foundation model families, evaluated in zero-shot mode, with two state-of-the-art electricity price forecasting benchmarks in Germany, Poland, and Spain over 2021-2025. Their performance is assessed in terms of point and probabilistic forecasting accuracy, as well as economic value in battery energy storage arbitrage. Only the TabPFN models consistently and significantly outperform the benchmarks across all three markets and all statistical measures. However, this statistical dominance does not translate directly into economic dominance: TabPFN performs best under unlimited bids and riskier quantile-based strategies, whereas the Distributional Deep Neural Network benchmark is more profitable when risk tolerance is lower. Thus, foundation models cannot universally replace market-specific models, and their value depends on both model architecture and the decision problem.

</details>


### [153] [Assessing Alignment and Stability of Feature Importance Explanations via Weight of Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00090)
*Eddie Conti,Claudio Daka,Álvaro Parafita,Antonio L. Alfeo,Axel Brando,Mario G. C. A. Cimino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于证据权重（WoE）的假设检验框架来评估特征重要性方法（FIMs），通过量化观测证据对特征重要性假设的支持程度，实现对FIMs的对比性和基于证据的评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有特征重要性方法（FIMs）广泛用于可解释AI，但归因分数本身对底层推理过程提供的洞察有限，缺乏系统评估FIMs与先验知识对齐程度及其变异性的原则性方法。

Method: 将FIMs嵌入基于证据权重（WoE）的假设检验框架中，量化观测证据对任意特征重要性假设的支持强度；参考假设可来自领域知识、真实标签或FIM自身，并建立WoE与归因方差之间的理论联系。

Result: 在LIME和SHAP解释上，针对不同参考假设的场景进行了实证验证，展示了框架的适用性和灵活性；同时提供了WoE与归因方差相关联的理论结果。

Conclusion: 该框架为通过对比性、基于证据的视角评估FIMs提供了一种补充性工具，能同时捕捉FIMs与先验知识的一致性及其变异性。

Abstract: Feature importance Methods (FIMs) are widely used in Explainable AI to interpret model predictions, yet attribution scores alone often provide limited insight into the underlying reasoning process. In this work, we introduce a novel perspective by embedding FIMs within a hypothesis-testing framework based on Weight of Evidence (WoE). We quantify how strongly the observed evidence supports any given hypothesis on feature importance. The reference hypothesis can stem from domain knowledge, ground truth, or be derived from the FIM itself. This formulation enables a principled evaluation of FIMs, capturing both their alignment with prior knowledge and their variability. We further provide theoretical results linking WoE to attribution variance. Empirical results shows the applicability and flexibility of our strategy analyzing LIME and SHAP explanations in settings with different reference hypotheses. Overall, our framework offers a complementary tool for assessing FIMs through a contrastive, evidence-based lens.

</details>


### [154] [Safin-1: Safety from Within through Memory-Native State Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00092)
*Ming Zhang,Kaisen Yang,Shu Yu,Ermo Hua,Zhekai Chen,Cheng Jin,Jingnan Zheng,Yi Zhang,Zhongtian Ma,Jiawei Zhou,Sirui Chen,Qiaosheng Zhang,Xiang Wang,Ning Ding,Xia Hu,Bowen Zhou,Youbang Sun,Chaochao Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Safin-1系列基础模型，通过基于MARCH架构的记忆锚定路由与状态演化实现"由内而外的安全"（Safety from Within），使安全能力成为模型自身原生计算的一部分，而非依赖外部护栏或事后对齐。通过Safety State实现测试时状态适应，在提升下游安全任务表现的同时保持通用能力的稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基础模型的安全保障主要依赖外部防护或事后监督微调等对齐手段，这种做法将安全视为一种外部强加的行为约束而非模型自身的固有属性。作者认为安全应当内嵌于模型本身的表示与计算之中，以支持长时间复杂交互场景下信息的持续累积与状态维护。

Method: 方法核心包括：(1) MARCH（跨上下文历史的记忆锚定路由）架构，维护结构化记忆状态并通过内容条件化路由选择性检索相关历史信息；(2) 持久能力状态的测试时适应机制，在不反复修改骨干网络的前提下实现受控特化；(3) 以Safety State作为该接口在下游安全任务上的具体实现，将上下文记忆与持久能力适应统一于模型原生计算中。

Result: 在通用能力、长上下文理解、检索与效率等多个维度的评估验证了Safin-1的有效性；基于Safety State的状态适应在下游安全任务上带来了显著的安全性能提升。

Conclusion: 该工作将记忆从被动记录先前上下文的角色重构为主动维持和演化模型行为的底层载体，为"安全作为状态原生且可自适应维护的能力"这一愿景提供了初步架构探索路径，同时也承认该方向仍需大量后续工作才能实现更宏大的目标。

Abstract: Long-horizon complex tasks require foundation models to accumulate information, maintain internal states, and adapt over extended interactions. Safety should be an intrinsic property of the model itself, rather than a behavioral constraint relying solely on external safeguards or post-hoc alignment such as supervised fine-tuning. This motivates Safety from Within, where safety-relevant capabilities are represented and invoked through the model's native computation. We present Safin-1, a family of foundation models realizing this principle through memory routing and state evolution. Safin-1 is built on Memory-Anchor Routing across Context History (MARCH), a network architecture that maintains structured memory states and selectively retrieves relevant historical information through content-conditioned routing. It supports test-time adaptation of persistent capability states without repeatedly modifying the backbone, enabling controlled specialization over a shared foundation. We investigate this interface on downstream safety tasks through a Safety State, demonstrating effective state-based adaptation with substantial safety improvements. More broadly, the routed-state interface unifies contextual memory and persistent capability adaptation within the model's native computation, reframing memory from a passive record of prior context into an active substrate for maintaining and evolving model behavior. Evaluations across general capabilities, long-context understanding, retrieval, and efficiency further validate Safin-1. These findings provide a path toward safety as a state-native and adaptively maintainable capability. This work is only an initial architectural exploration of Safety from Within, and substantial further work is needed to realize this broader vision.

</details>


### [155] [Local Reference Geometry Residual Augmentation for Imbalanced Time Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00093)
*Chuanhang Qiu,Yanran Xu,Yue Wang,Anthony Bagnall*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Local Reference Geometry (LRG)，一种轻量级的post-hoc特征增强模块，用于修复不平衡时间序列分类中少數類样本在特征空间中局部几何不可靠的问题。LRG在固定特征提取器与分类头之间，利用训练特征计算局部暴露度与类别混合风险，并附加标准化符号位移与LDA投影残差摘要来增强特征。实验表明LRG在多种表示（学习/预训练/固定）上均能提升性能，且增益集中在高风险区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不平衡时间序列分类方法通常针对训练分布、目标函数、logits或最终阈值进行干预，但未回答一个表征层面的关键问题：当少数类支持被减少后，学到的特征空间在少数类区域附近是否仍然局部可靠？作者识别出一种"训练局部几何失败"：在不平衡条件下，即使表征保留了有用的全局类别结构，少数类样本可能处于稀疏、被多数类主导或混合的特征空间邻域中。

Method: 提出Local Reference Geometry (LRG)模块，作为固定特征提取器与分类头之间的轻量级post-hoc特征增强。LRG仅使用训练特征：(1) 度量局部暴露度和类别混合风险；(2) 将每个固定特征附加一个标准化的、来自附近训练几何的带符号位移；(3) 附加一个LDA投影的残差摘要。该方法可与训练级干预和后编码器分类器修正组合使用。

Result: 在受控的UCR/Bake Off Redux不平衡基准上，配对raw-versus-LRG比较表明：对于学习表示、预训练表示和固定表示，LRG均带来增益；与训练级干预及后编码器分类器修正组合时同样有效。消融实验显示增益来自附加到原始特征上的带符号局部残差，而非通用原型距离、亲和特征、标量统计或VLAD风格编码。

Conclusion: 分析结果支持所提出的"局部几何失败"假设：不平衡条件下少数类邻域越来越多地被多数类暴露，训练局部风险能识别易错区域，且LRG的增益集中在这些高风险区域。这表明仅靠全局类结构保留不足以保障少数类的可靠分类，需要针对局部特征几何进行显式诊断与修复。

Abstract: Imbalanced time series classification is often addressed by changing the training distribution, objective, logits, or final threshold. These interventions address important biases, yet leave a representation-level question unmeasured: after minority support is reduced, does a learned feature space remain locally reliable around minority regions? We identify a training-local geometry failure: under imbalance, minority cases can lie in sparse, rest-dominated, or mixed feature-space neighborhoods, even when the representation retains useful global class structure. To diagnose and repair this failure, we propose Local Reference Geometry (LRG), a lightweight post-hoc feature augmentation module applied between a fixed feature extractor and the classifier head. Using training features only, LRG measures local exposure and class-mixture risk, then augments each fixed feature with a standardized signed displacement from nearby training geometry and an LDA-projected residual summary. On controlled UCR/Bake Off Redux imbalance benchmarks, paired raw-versus-LRG comparisons show gains for learned, pretrained, and fixed representations, including when LRG is combined with training-level interventions and post-encoder classifier corrections. Ablations show that the gain comes from the signed local residual appended to the original feature, rather than from generic prototype distances, affinity features, scalar statistics, or VLAD-style codes. Further analyses support the proposed local-geometry failure hypothesis: minority neighborhoods become increasingly rest-exposed under imbalance, training-local risk identifies error-prone regions, and LRG gains concentrate in those high-risk regions.

</details>


### [156] [Faster Than Flash: Exploiting Attention Sparsity for Efficient Long-Context Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00097)
*Zhigeng Liu,Zhiyuan Ning,Ruixiao Li,Xiaoran Liu,Yuerong Song,Min Zhang,Ziwei He,Xipeng Qiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Faster Flash Decoding (FFD)，一种硬件-算法协同设计的框架，通过全融合内核和top-delta稀疏策略，突破长上下文解码的内存墙瓶颈，实现最高11.6倍内核加速和2.37倍端到端吞吐提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文大语言模型在解码过程中面临内存带宽瓶颈和注意力机制二次复杂度的问题。现有的基于元数据度量的方法存在内存开销问题，而自适应选择策略存在计算效率低下的问题，二者之间存在难以调和的权衡。

Method: FFD将选择器和计算器整合为全融合内核，通过低比特量化实现内容感知扫描以替代外部元数据索引；引入top-delta策略动态过滤块，实现分布自适应稀疏性而无需全局同步；支持免训练即插即用，并实现扫描结果复用。

Result: FFD实现了最高11.6倍内核级加速，可扩展到256K上下文长度，端到端吞吐提升2.37倍。在RULER和LongBench基准上的实证验证表明，FFD在保持模型精度的同时实现了高比例稀疏性。

Conclusion: FFD通过硬件-算法协同设计有效解决了长上下文解码中的内存墙问题，是一种高效且保持精度的免训练解决方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: The development of long-context Large Language Models (LLMs) is constrained by the memory bandwidth bottleneck and quadratic complexity of the attention mechanism during decoding. To overcome the inherent trade-offs between the memory overhead of metadata-based metrics and the computational inefficiency of adaptive selection strategies, we present Faster Flash Decoding (FFD), a novel hardware-algorithm co-design framework designed to break the memory wall in long-context decoding. FFD integrates the selector and computer into a fully fused kernel, replacing external metadata indices with content-aware scanning via low-bit quantization. Furthermore, we introduce the top-delta strategy, which dynamically filters blocks to achieve distribution-adaptive sparsity without global synchronization. Offering a training-free and plug-and-play solution, FFD also enables the reuse of scanning results for computation, achieving up to 11.6x kernel-level speedup and scaling to 256K context length, with 2.37x end-to-end throughput improvement. Empirical validation on RULER and LongBench confirms that FFD maintains model accuracy while delivering high-ratio sparsity, with code available at https://github.com/qluoluo/faster-flash-decoding

</details>


### [157] [Generative artificial intelligence for reliable mechanistic reasoning for corrosion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00099)
*Bharath M N,R K Singh Raman,Alankar Alankar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向腐蚀知识合成的领域自适应检索增强生成（RAG）框架，在镁合金腐蚀数据集上对三种开源语言模型进行微调，并结合混合稠密-词法检索管线。通过Reason Map命题图框架，系统能够检测因果方向反转和推断跳跃等平面事实性指标无法暴露的问题。实验表明检索增强使Token F1提升143-194%，盲外部验证确认了趋势级泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腐蚀占全球GDP约4%，准确预测对及时缓解至关重要。机器学习虽能预测腐蚀速率，但无法解释潜在机制。在安全关键的材料工程中，不仅需要准确检索，还需要机制上可辩护的推理，而现有事实性指标无法评估这种能力。

Method: 构建领域自适应的检索增强生成（RAG）框架：1）在840篇同行评审论文生成的3,309个专家验证问答对上微调三种开源模型（Llama-3.1-8B、Qwen-2.5-7B、Mistral-7B）；2）集成混合稠密-词法检索管线；3）引入Reason Map命题图框架，从生成答案和检索文献中独立构建有向证据图，以检测因果方向反转和推断跳跃。

Result: 检索增强使Token F1获得143-194%的提升，系统忠实度为0.964，上下文召回率为0.988。在新发表文献和内部电化学数据上的盲外部验证确认了趋势级泛化能力。Reason Map能够系统性地检测平面事实性指标无法暴露的因果方向反转和未支持的推断跳跃。

Conclusion: 该模块化架构可跨领域应用，为可信的AI辅助知识合成提供了通用蓝图，不仅适用于腐蚀领域，也可推广至其他工程领域。

Abstract: Corrosion accounts for approximately 4% of global GDP, and reliable prediction is essential for timely mitigation. Machine learning effectively predicts corrosion rates from composition, microstructure, and environmental variables, but cannot explain the underlying mechanisms. A reliable approach in safety-critical materials engineering requires not only accurate retrieval but also mechanistically defensible reasoning, a capability that existing factuality metrics cannot assess. This work presents a domain-adapted retrieval-augmented generation framework for corrosion knowledge synthesis, demonstrated on magnesium alloy corrosion. Three open-weight language models (Llama-3.1-8B, Qwen-2.5-7B, Mistral-7B) are fine-tuned on 3,309 expert-verified question-answer pairs from 840 peer-reviewed papers and integrated with a hybrid dense-lexical retrieval pipeline. Retrieval augmentation produces Token F1 gains of 143-194%, with system faithfulness of 0.964 and context recall of 0.988. Blind external validation on newly published literature and in-house electrochemical data confirms trend-level generalisation. Reason Map, a proposition-graph framework, is further introduced; it independently constructs directed evidence graphs from generated answers and retrieved literature, enabling systematic detection of causal direction inversions and unsupported inferential leaps that flat factuality metrics cannot expose. The modular architecture can be applied across domains, offering a generalizable blueprint for trustworthy AI-assisted knowledge synthesis to circumvent corrosion, which can also be applied to other engineering domains.

</details>


### [158] [Good Memory Has ECC: Evaluating the Memory of Vision-Language Models Beyond Accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00103)
*Shmuel Berman,Jia Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ECCBench基准与评估协议，从效率(Efficiency)、压缩(Compression)、校准(Calibration)三个维度衡量LLM/VLM的记忆能力，超越了传统仅关注准确率的评估方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆基准仅通过长文本或视频上的准确率来评估模型记忆能力，但准确率无法反映真实长时程任务中记忆的效率、压缩性和不确定度校准等关键属性，这些属性对实际部署至关重要。

Method: 提出ECCBench基准，通过三个轴进行评估：(1)效率—以FLOPs衡量从记忆中回答问题所需的计算量；(2)压缩—验证可压缩输入是否被更准确或更高效地记忆；(3)校准—考察系统是否根据自身不确定性和错误代价进行弃权。对预训练VLM以及更广泛的记忆骨干架构（包括非Transformer架构）进行评估对比。

Result: 发现预训练VLM能够对文本进行记忆压缩但无法对视频压缩，且两者校准性均较差。在更广泛的记忆骨干中，多种非Transformer架构在压缩-校准权衡上优于RoPE Transformer，可能有潜力用于长时程智能体。

Conclusion: 记忆评估需要超越单纯准确率，ECCBench提供了一个涵盖效率、压缩和校准的多维评估框架，且非Transformer架构在记忆压缩-校准权衡上的优势值得进一步探索。

Abstract: Memory is widely viewed as an important unsolved problem for LLMs and VLMs, and current benchmarks typically evaluate it by testing accuracy over long text or video. However, accuracy alone misses properties that matter for real long-horizon tasks. We introduce ECCBench, a benchmark and evaluation protocol that measures memory beyond a system's capacity--its raw accuracy at a specific budget--via three axes we call ECC: efficiency--the computation, in FLOPs, needed to answer from memory; compression--whether compressible inputs are remembered more accurately or efficiently; and calibration--whether the system abstains in response to its own uncertainty and the cost of an error. We find that pretrained VLMs compress their memory over text but not video and are poorly calibrated on both. Among a broader set of memory backbones, several non-Transformer architectures achieve better compression-calibration tradeoffs than RoPE Transformers, suggesting they may be useful components for agents operating over long horizons.

</details>


### [159] [Flawed in Nature, Perfect through Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00129)
*J. M. Diederik Kruijssen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"天生缺陷，演化完美"（Flawed in Nature, Perfect through Evolution）的机制，通过对AI/ML模型群施加故意偏离最优的突变，使其在环境变化（非平稳）条件下可靠且可持续地提升性能，作为对抗非平稳性的统计对冲。通过四个定理证明了遗憾减少的保证，并在合成线性回归任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI/ML模型在训练分布发生漂移时性能会下降，这是现实世界问题的普遍特征。虽然生物进化通过自然选择解决了这一难题，但AI/ML中维持模型多样性与优化收敛之间存在根本性矛盾，亟需一种能在变化环境中持续保持模型性能的机制。

Method: 提出"Flawed-in-Nature"机制：对一组AI/ML模型施加远离最优化的故意系数突变，使模型群作为统计对冲来应对环境非平稳性。通过四个定理从理论上证明遗憾减少的保证，并在合成线性回归任务上验证，同时设计了一个自适应控制器来调节突变漂移率以匹配环境的未知漂移率。

Result: 在合成线性回归任务中，突变模型群在约80%的环境变化中能提供最佳模型，且推理综合能将个体优势转化为群体优势。机制在突变漂移率与环境漂移率匹配时最为有效。

Conclusion: 该机制可作为一种通用化的AI/ML系统设计原则，与生物进化的相似性暗示它可能是实现更接近生物智能的AI形式的关键缺失要素。

Abstract: The performance of artificial intelligence (AI) and machine learning (ML) models degrades when the problem they were trained on drifts. This is a near-universal feature of real-world problems, which often change unpredictably. Biological evolution has achieved intelligence by overcoming this obstacle through natural selection acting on heritable variation. AI/ML techniques have long incorporated forms of natural selection, but it has been challenging to maintain model diversity as optimization naturally drives convergence. Here we show that a swarm of AI/ML models subjected to deliberate mutations of their model coefficients away from optimality can reliably and sustainably improve performance in changing environments by acting as a statistical hedge against non-stationarity. We call this mechanism 'Flawed in Nature, Perfect through Evolution', reflecting that the collective performance gain goes at the expense of individual performance. We prove via four theorems that the resulting regret reduction is guaranteed under general conditions, establishing the Flawed-in-Nature mechanism as a generalizable design principle for AI/ML systems. We validate these results on synthetic linear regression tasks, demonstrating that the mutated swarm delivers the best model in $\sim80\%$ of environment changes and that inference synthesis successfully translates this individual advantage into a collective one. The mechanism proves to be most effective when the mutation drift rate matches the drift rate of the environment. We outline a simple, adaptive controller that enables practical applications by tuning the mutation drift rate to match the unknown drift rate of the environment. The close analogy of the Flawed-in-Nature mechanism to biological evolution suggests it may have been a critical missing ingredient for the organic discovery of AI forms that more closely mimic biological intelligence.

</details>


### [160] [Elite-Weighted Supervised Fine-tuning for Goal-Directed Molecular Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00189)
*Shiyun Wa,Yifei Wang,Anna G. Green,Simone Sciabola,Ye Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Elite-Weighted Supervised Fine-tuning (EW-SFT)的分子生成优化方法，通过奖励引导的精英选择和模型原生预训练损失更新，无需轨迹级强化学习即可跨不同生成架构和设计任务进行统一优化，且在多个基准上优于原生优化器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分子生成目标导向优化通常依赖策略梯度强化学习，这需要与模型架构和生成过程强耦合的轨迹对数概率，导致优化器难以在不同架构和条件生成设计间复用。监督微调虽然简单，但其更新由固定数据集驱动，奖励无法进入更新过程，缺乏奖励引导能力。

Method: EW-SFT利用奖励引导高分子的精英选择，仅对选中的精英分子子集使用模型自身的预训练损失进行更新。消融实验表明奖励信息主要通过精英选择而非所选集合内的连续加权传递。该方法仅需已评分的分子和模型原生损失，因此可统一应用于自回归、掩码扩散和离散流生成器，以及从头设计、基序扩展和连接子设计任务。

Result: 在固定预算的3D形状对齐oracle调用下（针对两个激酶参考化合物），EW-SFT一致优于对应的原生优化器；在2D相似性oracle下（四个保留参考化合物）进一步改善目标导向优化；在样本效率基准上无需轨迹级RL公式即可达到可比性能。

Conclusion: EW-SFT是一种统一且有效的分子生成优化器，能够跨分子生成器、设计约束、参考化合物和oracle类型推广，为奖励引导的分子生成优化提供了一种架构无关的简洁方案。

Abstract: Goal-directed optimization is essential for steering molecular generators to propose candidates with desired properties. However, it is often implemented with policy-gradient reinforcement learning, which requires a generation-trajectory log-probability whose form depends on the model architecture and generation procedure. This makes an optimizer difficult to reuse across architectures and conditional generative designs. Supervised fine-tuning needs none of that machinery, but its update is driven by a fixed dataset, so the reward never enters the update. We introduce Elite-Weighted Supervised Fine-tuning (EW-SFT), which uses reward to guide elite selection of high-scoring molecules, and updates the model by its own pretraining loss on that set. Ablations show that reward information is passed primarily through elite selection, rather than through continuous weighting within the selected set. Because the update consumes only scored molecules and the model's native loss, the same rule applies across autoregressive, masked-diffusion, and discrete-flow generators, and across de novo, motif-extension, and linker-design tasks. Under a fixed budget of 3D shape alignment oracle calls on two kinase reference compounds, EW-SFT consistently outperforms the corresponding native optimizers. It further improves goal-directed optimization under a 2D similarity oracle on four held-out references and achieves comparable performance on a sample-efficiency benchmark without a trajectory-level RL formulation. These results demonstrate that EW-SFT is a unified and effective optimizer across molecular generators, design constraints, references, and oracles.

</details>


### [161] [WHALE: A Simple Recipe for Joint Harness-Weight Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00196)
*Haechan Kim,Yoonho Lee,Gisang Lee,Chelsea Finn,Kangwook Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Weight-Harness Alternating Learning (WHALE)，一种交替优化模型权重与执行代码（harness）的联合自适应方法。在三个领域的智能体任务上，WHALE较仅权重优化、仅harness优化及Fast-Slow Training方法在最佳mean@8准确率上提升4.15-24.38个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体性能同时依赖于模型参数和可执行harness代码（管理上下文与控制流），但现有方法要么仅优化权重与文本提示词（保持harness固定），要么单独优化其中一个组件，导致系统受限于另一个冻结的组件。权重更新可能改变有效的harness，harness更新也可能改变被暴露的模型能力，因此需要联合自适应方法。

Method: WHALE交替执行两个阶段：一是在当前harness下通过在线拒绝采样微调更新模型；二是使用Meta-Harness在更新后的模型下搜索更好的harness。切换时机的设计使用固定阶段时长或基于训练信号的适应性忍耐规则，以区分真实改进与噪声，同时避免对不断变化的对手过度优化。

Result: 基于Qwen3.5-2B/4B智能体，在搜索问答、数学推理和象棋谜题三个领域中进行验证。WHALE在最佳mean@8准确率上超过仅权重、仅harness和Fast-Slow Training方法4.15-24.38个百分点。实验还发现任一组件都可能成为瓶颈：harness搜索在SearchQA中用更少rollout即达到峰值仅权重准确率，但在数学任务中需要权重更新后才提升；交错的小更新在准确率和rollout成本上均优于stagewise的权重后harness优化。

Conclusion: 模型的权重参数与harness执行代码需要联合交替优化而非固定其一。WHALE通过交替的模型微调与harness搜索，能自适应打破任一组件成为瓶颈的局面，且交替策略优于顺序式分阶段优化。

Abstract: Agent performance depends jointly on the model parameters and the executable harness code that manages context and control flow. Optimizing either component in isolation can leave the system bottlenecked by its frozen counterpart: weight updates can change which harness is effective, while harness updates can change which model capabilities are exposed. Existing joint-adaptation methods optimize weights and textual prompts but leave the broader harness fixed. We propose Weight-Harness Alternating LEarning (WHALE), a simple recipe that alternates two phases: updating the model under the current harness, then searching for a better harness under the updated model. We instantiate these two phases with online rejection-sampling fine-tuning and Meta-Harness, respectively. When to switch is a key design choice: to separate real improvements from noise without over-optimizing against a changing counterpart, WHALE uses either fixed phase durations or an adaptive patience rule over training signals. Using Qwen3.5-2B/4B agents across three domains (search question answering, mathematical reasoning, and chess puzzles), WHALE outperforms weight-only, harness-only, and Fast-Slow Training by 4.15-24.38 percentage points in best mean@8 accuracy. Either component can be the bottleneck: harness search matches peak weight-only accuracy with far fewer rollouts in SearchQA, but improves math accuracy only after a weight update. Small interleaved updates also outperform stagewise weight-then-harness optimization in accuracy and rollout cost. The code is available at https://github.com/krafton-ai/WHALE.

</details>


### [162] [QTEA: Ternary LLMs with Sparse Residual Salient Weight and By-Column Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00224)
*Yipin Guo,Arun M George,Jie Fu,Tareq Mahmoud,Sixue Xing,Siddharth Joshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QTEA是一个面向大语言模型的sub-2-bit权重量后量化框架，将权重量化为三值并用显著权重作为残差补偿，通过列级缩放精炼和误差衰减技术提升精度，在Qwen3-14B上实现1.7比特有效压缩并较最强三值PTQ基线提升16.7%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有权重量后量化方法在低于2比特时容易遭受严重精度损失且难以跨模型泛化，而采用非结构化稀疏缓解精度损失又会牺牲规则性和GPU执行效率。

Method: 将权重量化为三值，使用显著性权重作为残差误差补偿器，残差以1:4半结构化稀疏方式分配到选中列；在GPTQ风格的逐列量化中加入列级缩放精炼，交替更新每列缩放与三值分配；引入误差衰减以抑制GPTQ中顺序相关的误差累积。同时开发基于查找表的推理内核。

Result: 在Qwen3-14B上实现1.7比特有效压缩，平均准确率较最强三值PTQ基线提升16.7%；WikiText和C4困惑度分别降低1.40倍和2.61倍。在Llama3-8B上获得6.6%准确率提升及1.34倍/1.95倍困惑度降低。生成速度较FP16基线提升7.2倍。

Conclusion: QTEA通过三值量化结合半结构化稀疏与多种精炼策略，在sub-2-bit低比特下同时保持了精度和硬件执行效率，为大规模LLM低比特推理部署提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Weight-only post-training quantization (PTQ) can alleviate the computational burden of serving large language models (LLMs) at scale. However, existing PTQ methods often fail to generalize across models and suffer severe accuracy loss below 2 bits. Many leverage unstructured sparsity to mitigate this loss, but at the cost of regularity and GPU-friendly execution. We present QTEA, a sub-2-bit PTQ framework that quantizes weights into ternary values and uses salient weights as residual error compensators. To maintain hardware efficiency, residuals are assigned to selected columns with semi-structured \(1{:}4\) sparsity within the salient columns. We further add column-wise rescale refinement to GPTQ-style column-by-column quantization, alternately updating per-column scales and ternary assignments to reduce reconstruction error. We also identify order-dependent error propagation in GPTQ and introduce error decay to attenuate late-stage error accumulation. On Qwen3-14B, QTEA compresses all weights to an effective 1.7 bits per weight while improving average accuracy over the strongest ternary PTQ baseline by 16.7\%. It also achieves 1.40\(\times\) and 2.61\(\times\) lower perplexity on WikiText and C4 respectively. This trend holds on Llama3-8B, where QTEA obtains a 6.6\% accuracy gain and 1.34\(\times\)/1.95\(\times\) lower perplexity on the same datasets. Finally, we develop a lookup-table based kernel that achieves 7.2\(\times\) faster per-token generation over an FP16 baseline. Code is available at https://github.com/Intelligent-Microsystems-Lab/QTEA.

</details>


### [163] [Geometry-aware Latent Autoregressive Generative Model for PDEs in Complex Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00297)
*Zi Wang,Minghui Xu,Tapan Mukerji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GeoLAMP，一种几何感知的潜在自回归生成模型，用于在高度不规则和曲折的微米级复杂几何结构中求解多物理场偏微分方程（PDE）。该模型采用双编码器架构捕捉全局拓扑和细尺度几何特征，在潜空间中通过因果自注意力Transformer和流匹配实现稳定可扩展的分块自回归时间预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 求解多物理场PDE是科学计算中的重大挑战，尤其是在能量和化学工程中复杂的微米级曲折几何结构中，传统数值方法难以高效处理。

Method: GeoLAMP包含三个关键组件：(1) 双编码器架构基于图表示，联合捕捉全局拓扑与细尺度几何特征；(2) 潜空间中采用带流匹配的因果自注意力Transformer，实现稳定可扩展的分块自回归时间预测；(3) 灵活的解码器可在任意点上重建高分辨率物理场。

Result: 作者建立了覆盖反应流、热对流和弹性力学的三个多物理场基准数据集。GeoLAMP在所有数据集上均实现最稳定的自回归性能，在整个展开（rollout）过程中保持低误差。

Conclusion: 该工作为微米级复杂几何结构中的PDE几何感知学习提供了系统性研究，并为基于流匹配框架的潜在自回归PDE建模的分块时间推进提供了新见解。

Abstract: Solving multiphysics partial differential equations (PDEs) remains a major challenge in scientific computing, especially for highly complex $μ$m-scale tortuous geometries critical to energy and chemical engineering. We address this challenge by proposing a Geometry-aware Latent Autoregressive generative Model for PDEs (GeoLAMP) for solving physics within highly irregular and tortuous structures. GeoLAMP introduces a dual-encoder architecture on graph representations to jointly capture global topology and fine-scale geometric features, enabling an effective transition from real-space fields to compact latent representations. In the latent space, we propose a causal self-attention transformer with flow matching to model temporal dynamics, allowing stable and scalable block-wise autoregressive prediction. A flexible decoder reconstructs high-resolution physical fields on arbitrary points. We establish three multiphysics benchmark datasets in complex geometries, covering reactive flow, heat convection, and elasticity. GeoLAMP consistently achieves the most stable autoregression performance on these datasets, maintaining low errors throughout the entire rollout horizon. Our results provide a systematic study of geometry-aware learning for PDEs in $μ$m-scale complex geometries and offer new insights into block-wise time marching of latent autoregressive PDE modeling via a flow matching framework.

</details>


### [164] [Do LLMs Know Your Neighborhood? Auditing LLM Priors for Neighborhood-Level Mobility Prediction and Structural Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00345)
*Saad Mohammad Abrar,Eesha Kurella,Arnav Dadarya,Naman Awasthi,Kazi Tasnim Zinat,Vanessa Frias-Martinez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究评估了零样本大语言模型（LLMs）在邻里级（人口普查区组）人类流动性预测中的表现，使用四个美国都市区的匿名Cuebiq数据构建多层面流动性结果，并与监督学习基线对比。研究发现监督模型平均准确率显著优于最佳LLM（0.580 vs 0.435），LLM过多依赖粗粒度且稳定的预测因子先验知识，且在敏感群体变量上存在不对称处理，表明LLM预测需经验校准而非直接视为结构性可靠的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
